	espBoundingBox.Name = "ESPBoundingBox"
	espBoundingBox.Parent = canvas
	espBoundingBox.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	espBoundingBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	espBoundingBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)

	self.bbstroke = self.gio(espBoundingBox, Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 0)
	self.wbstroke = self.gio(espBoundingBox, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 1)

	self.billboard = self.maid:mark(billboardGui)
	self.canvas = self.maid:mark(canvas)
	self.bbox = self.maid:mark(espBoundingBox)

	self.hbar = self:add("HealthBar", "left", 6, function(container)
		self:cgb(container, true, true, Color3.new(1.0, 1.0, 1.0))
	end)

	self:extra()

	self.dcontainer = self:add("Distance", "bottom", 16, function(container)
		local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		textLabel.Parent = container
		textLabel.Text = "0m"
		textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 400, 1, 0)
		textLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
		textLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0)
		textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1.0
		textLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
		textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.0
		textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1.0, 1.0, 1.0)
		textLabel.TextSize = 13
		textLabel.TextWrapped = false
		textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Code
	end)

	self.ncontainer = self:add("Name", "top", 16, function(container)
		local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		textLabel.Parent = container
		textLabel.Text = "N/A"
		textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 400, 1, 0)
		textLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
		textLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0)
		textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1.0
		textLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
		textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.0
		textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(0.0117647, 1, 1)
		textLabel.TextSize = 13
		textLabel.TextWrapped = false
		textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Code
	end)
end)

---@param entity Model
---@param identifier string
---@param label string
function EntityESP.new(entity, identifier, label)
	local self = setmetatable({}, EntityESP)
	self.label = label
	self.entity = entity
	self.identifier = identifier
	self.maid = Maid.new()
	self.elements = {
		top = {},
		bottom = {},
		left = {},
		right = {},
	}

	return self
end

return EntityESP
end)
__bundle_register("Features/Visuals/Objects/MobESP", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
---@module Features.Visuals.Objects.EntityESP
local EntityESP = require("Features/Visuals/Objects/EntityESP")

---@class MobESP: EntityESP
local MobESP = setmetatable({}, { __index = EntityESP })
MobESP.__index = MobESP
MobESP.__type = "MobESP"

local ESP_HEALTH = "[%i/%i]"

---@param self MobESP
MobESP.update = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(self)
	local humanoid = self._cachedHumanoid
	if not humanoid or not humanoid.Parent then
		humanoid = self.entity:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		self._cachedHumanoid = humanoid
	end
	if not humanoid then
		return self:visible(false)
	end

	EntityESP.update(self, { ESP_HEALTH:format(humanoid.Health, humanoid.MaxHealth) })
end)

---@param identifier string
---@param model Model
---@param label string
function MobESP.new(identifier, model, label)
	local self = setmetatable(EntityESP.new(model, identifier, label), MobESP)
	self:setup()
	self:build()
	self:update()
	return self
end

return MobESP
end)
__bundle_register("Features/Exploits/Exploits", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
---@module Utility.Maid
local Maid = require("Utility/Maid")

---@module Utility.InstanceWrapper
local InstanceWrapper = require("Utility/InstanceWrapper")

local voidMaid = Maid.new()

local clientPart = Instance.new('Part')
local peer = gethiddenproperty(clientPart, "NetworkOwnerV3")
clientPart:Destroy()

local originalFPDH = workspace:GetAttribute("OriginalFPDH") or workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = 0/0
workspace:SetAttribute("OriginalFPDH", originalFPDH)

---@param model Model
---@param part Part
local function voidPart(model, part)
    local targetY = (originalFPDH or -500) - 50

    if not part:FindFirstChild("BodyVelocity") then
        local partConstantVelocity = InstanceWrapper.create(voidMaid, part, "BodyVelocity", part)
        partConstantVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1/0, 1/0, 1/0)
        partConstantVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, -12500, 0)
        partConstantVelocity.P = 1/0
    end

    local velocityController = part:FindFirstChild("ControlVel")
        or part:FindFirstChild("SafetyBV")
        or part:FindFirstChild("SwimBV")
        or part:FindFirstChild("Holder")

    if velocityController and velocityController:IsA("BodyMover") then
        velocityController:Destroy()
    end

    part.CanCollide = false
    part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -12500, 0)

    local root = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local posX = root and root.Position.X or part.Position.X
    local posZ = root and root.Position.Z or part.Position.Z
    
    part.CFrame = CFrame.new(posX, targetY, posZ)

    sethiddenproperty(part, "NetworkIsSleeping", false)
end

return LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
	local Exploits = { forceVoid = false, ownershipTimestamp = 0, ownershipItem = nil, currentRootPart = nil }

	---@module Utility.Signal
	local Signal = require("Utility/Signal")

	---@module Utility.Configuration
	local Configuration = require("Utility/Configuration")

	---@module Utility.OriginalStore
	local OriginalStore = require("Utility/OriginalStore")

	---@module Features.Game.OwnershipWatcher
	local OwnershipWatcher = require("Features/Game/OwnershipWatcher")

	---@module Utility.Logger
	local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

	---@module Features.Automation.EchoFarm
	local EchoFarm = require("Features/Automation/EchoFarm")

	local runService = game:GetService("RunService")
	local players = game:GetService("Players")
	local physicsService = game:GetService("PhysicsService")
	local replicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

	local collisionGroupName = tostring(Exploits)

	local exploitsMaid = Maid.new()
	local pathfindBreakerMaid = Maid.new()

	local allowSleep = voidMaid:mark(OriginalStore.new())

	local heartbeat = Signal.new(runService.Heartbeat)
	local preRender = Signal.new(runService.PreRender)

	local lastVelocity = nil

	local lastVector = 1

	local function cleanVoidMobs()
		voidMaid:clean()
		allowSleep:restore()
	end

local function updateVoidMobs()
    local localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer
    sethiddenproperty(localPlayer, "MaxSimulationRadius", 3000)
    sethiddenproperty(localPlayer, "SimulationRadius", 3000)

    allowSleep:set(settings().Physics, "AllowSleep", false)

    for part, data in next, OwnershipWatcher.parts do
        local model = data.model
        if not model or not data.owned then continue end

        local success, ownerID = pcall(function()
            return gethiddenproperty(part, "NetworkOwnerV3")
        end)
        
        local isActuallyOwned = (success and ownerID == peer) or (part.ReceiveAge == 0)

        if isActuallyOwned then
            if model.Parent == workspace.Live and model.Name:sub(1,1) == "." then
                if model.Name:lower():find("chaser") and EchoFarm.voiding then continue end

                for _, instance in pairs(model:GetChildren()) do
                    if instance:IsA("BasePart") then
                        instance.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
                
                voidPart(model, part)
            end
        end
    end
end

	local function getKnockedOwnershipItem()
		local localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer
		if not localPlayer then
			return
		end

		local backpack = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
		if not backpack then
			return
		end

		local selected = nil

		for _, item in pairs(backpack:GetChildren()) do
			if item.Name:match("Talent") then
				continue
			end

			if not item:IsA("Tool") then
				continue
			end

			if not item:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart") then
				continue
			end

			selected = item
		end

		if not selected then
			selected = localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Weapon")
			selected = selected or backpack:FindFirstChild("Weapon")
		end

		return selected
	end

	local function updateMoveWhileKnocked()
		local effectReplicator = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("EffectReplicator")
		local effectReplicatorModule = require(effectReplicator)

		if not effectReplicatorModule:FindEffect("Knocked") then
			return
		end

		local localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer
		if not localPlayer then
			return
		end

		local character = localPlayer.Character
		if not character then
			return
		end

		local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not rootPart then
			return
		end

		local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
		if not humanoid then
			return
		end

		local backpack = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
		if not backpack then
			return
		end

		local ownershipItem = Exploits.ownershipItem or getKnockedOwnershipItem()
		if not ownershipItem then
			return
		end

		if not Exploits.ownershipItem or not Exploits.ownershipItem:IsDescendantOf(game) then
			Exploits.ownershipItem = ownershipItem
		end

		if tick() - Exploits.ownershipTimestamp <= 0.15 then
			return
		end

		local leftHand = character:FindFirstChild("LeftHand")
		local rightHand = character:FindFirstChild("RightHand")
		local hasHandWeapon = (leftHand and leftHand:FindFirstChild("HandWeapon"))
			or (rightHand and rightHand:FindFirstChild("HandWeapon"))

		if not hasHandWeapon then
			local thrown = workspace:FindFirstChild("Thrown")
			local attach = thrown and thrown:FindFirstChild("Attach_" .. character.Name)
			hasHandWeapon = attach and attach:FindFirstChild("HandWeapon")
		end

		local characterHandler = character:FindFirstChild("CharacterHandler")
		local requests = characterHandler and characterHandler:FindFirstChild("Requests")
		local equipWeapon = requests and requests:FindFirstChild("EquipWeapon")

		if ownershipItem.Name == "Weapon" and hasHandWeapon then
			return equipWeapon and equipWeapon:FireServer(false)
		end

		if ownershipItem.Name == "Weapon" and not hasHandWeapon then
			return equipWeapon and equipWeapon:FireServer(true)
		end

		if ownershipItem.Parent == character then
			return humanoid:UnequipTools()
		end

		if ownershipItem.Parent == backpack then
			humanoid:EquipTool(ownershipItem)
		end

		Exploits.ownershipTimestamp = tick()
	end

	local function updateFlingHeartbeat()
		local character = players.LocalPlayer.Character
		if not character then
			return
		end

		local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not humanoidRootPart then
			return
		end

		-- Reference fling: dump a constant huge downward velocity onto the root every frame. Collision pins
		-- us in place while that replicated velocity launches anyone who touches us. (FallenPartsDestroyHeight
		-- is already forced to NaN globally at the top of this module, so flung players don't void-destroy.)
		-- A farm can crank the fling for one fight (the Titus farms do, on the boss) by setting
		-- shared.Luma.aggressiveFling; otherwise it uses the user's FlingPower slider.
		local power = (shared.Luma and shared.Luma.aggressiveFling)
			or Configuration.expectOptionValue("FlingPower")
			or 100000
		humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(16, -power, -16)
	end

	local function updateExploits()
		local localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer
		if not localPlayer then
			return
		end

		if Configuration.expectToggleValue("VoidMobs") then
			updateVoidMobs()
		else
			cleanVoidMobs()
		end

		if Configuration.expectToggleValue("MoveWhileKnocked") then
			updateMoveWhileKnocked()
		end

		if EchoFarm.voiding or Configuration.expectToggleValue("Fling") then
			updateFlingHeartbeat()
		end
	end

	-- (The old PreRender velocity-restore is gone: Fling keeps a constant velocity each frame instead of
	-- snapshotting + restoring it, which is what makes it actually fling rather than just break pathfinding.)

	function Exploits.init()
		exploitsMaid:add(heartbeat:connect("Exploits_Heartbeat", updateExploits))

		---@note: Wrapped in a PCall to prevent errors.
		pcall(function()
			physicsService:RegisterCollisionGroup(collisionGroupName)
			physicsService:CollisionGroupSetCollidable(collisionGroupName, collisionGroupName, false)
			physicsService:CollisionGroupSetCollidable(collisionGroupName, "Default", false)
			physicsService:CollisionGroupSetCollidable(collisionGroupName, "Player", false)
			physicsService:CollisionGroupSetCollidable(collisionGroupName, "WalkThrough", false)
		end)

		if not LPH_OBFUSCATED or shared.Luma.verbose then
			Logger.warn("Exploits initialized.")
		end
	end

	function Exploits.detach()
		exploitsMaid:clean()
		voidMaid:clean()
		pathfindBreakerMaid:clean()

		Logger.warn("Exploits detached.")
	end

	return Exploits
end)()
end)
__bundle_register("Features/Game/Movement", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
---@module Utility.Configuration
local Configuration = require("Utility/Configuration")

---@module Utility.OriginalStoreManager
local OriginalStoreManager = require("Utility/OriginalStoreManager")

---@module Utility.Maid
local Maid = require("Utility/Maid")

---@module Utility.Freecam
local Freecam = require("Utility/Freecam")

local movementMaid = Maid.new()

local players = game:GetService("Players")
local replicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local animationInstance = Instance.new("Animation")
animationInstance.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://12106091136"

return LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
	local Movement = {}

	---@module Utility.Signal
	local Signal = require("Utility/Signal")

	---@module Utility.InstanceWrapper
	local InstanceWrapper = require("Utility/InstanceWrapper")

	---@module Utility.OriginalStore
	local OriginalStore = require("Utility/OriginalStore")

	---@module Utility.ControlModule
	local ControlModule = require("Utility/ControlModule")

	---@module Utility.Finder
	local Finder = require("Utility/Finder")

	---@module Features.Game.Tweening
	local Tweening = require("Features/Game/Tweening")

	---@module Utility.Logger
	local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

	---@module Game.Timings.SaveManager
	local SaveManager = require("Game/Timings/SaveManager")

	local runService = game:GetService("RunService")
	local userInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

	local agilitySpoofer = movementMaid:mark(OriginalStore.new())

	local noClipMap = movementMaid:mark(OriginalStoreManager.new())

	local preSimulation = Signal.new(runService.PreSimulation)
	local heartbeat = Signal.new(runService.Heartbeat)

	local cachedTarget = nil

	-- Infinite zoom out: remember the original max zoom so we can restore it when the toggle is off.
	local originalMaxZoom = nil

	local noClipSpoofedClasses = { PrepareTP = true, TPSafe = true }
	local oldEffectReplicatorHasAny = nil
	local noClipHasAnyHooked = false

	local function onNoClipHasAny(effectReplicatorSelf, ...)
		if Configuration.expectToggleValue("NoClip") then
			for _, class in next, { ... } do
				if noClipSpoofedClasses[class] then
					return true
				end
			end
		end

		return oldEffectReplicatorHasAny(effectReplicatorSelf, ...)
	end

	local function updateNoClipHasAnyHook(enabled)
		local effectReplicator = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("EffectReplicator")
		local effectReplicatorModule = effectReplicator and require(effectReplicator)

		if not effectReplicatorModule or typeof(effectReplicatorModule.HasAny) ~= "function" then
			return
		end

		if enabled and not noClipHasAnyHooked then
			oldEffectReplicatorHasAny = effectReplicatorModule.HasAny
			effectReplicatorModule.HasAny = onNoClipHasAny
			noClipHasAnyHooked = true
		elseif not enabled and noClipHasAnyHooked then
			effectReplicatorModule.HasAny = oldEffectReplicatorHasAny
			oldEffectReplicatorHasAny = nil
			noClipHasAnyHooked = false
		end
	end

	---@param character Model
	---@param rootPart BasePart
	local function updateNoClip(character, rootPart)
		local effectReplicator = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("EffectReplicator")
		if not effectReplicator then
			return
		end

		local controllerManager = character:FindFirstChild("ControllerManager")
		if not controllerManager then
			return
		end

		local airController = controllerManager:FindFirstChild("AirController")
		if not airController then
			return
		end

		local groundController = controllerManager:FindFirstChild("GroundController")
		if not groundController then
			return
		end

		if Configuration.expectToggleValue("Fly") then
			controllerManager.ActiveController = airController
		else
			controllerManager.ActiveController = groundController
		end

		local effectReplicatorModule = require(effectReplicator)
		local knockedRestore = effectReplicatorModule:FindEffect("Knocked")
			and Configuration.expectToggleValue("NoClipCollisionsKnocked")

		if Tweening.active then
			knockedRestore = false
		end

		for _, instance in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
			if not instance:IsA("BasePart") then
				continue
			end

			noClipMap:add(instance, "CanCollide", knockedRestore and noClipMap:get(instance):get() or false)

			local bone = instance:FindFirstChild("Bone")
			if not bone then
				continue
			end

			local success, result = pcall(function()
				return bone.CanCollide ~= nil
			end)

			if not success or not result then
				continue
			end

			noClipMap:add(bone, "CanCollide", knockedRestore and noClipMap:get(bone):get() or false)
		end
	end


---@param rootPart BasePart
---@param humanoid Humanoid
---@param dt number
local function updateBoatfly(rootPart, humanoid, dt)
    local players = game:GetService("Players")
    local replicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    
    local effectReplicator = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("EffectReplicator")
    local effectReplicatorModule = effectReplicator and require(effectReplicator)
    
    if not effectReplicatorModule then
        return
    end

    local localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer
    local speed = 1000

    local steeringEffect = effectReplicatorModule:FindEffect("Steering")
    if not steeringEffect or not steeringEffect.Value then return end
    
    local steeringValue = steeringEffect.Value
    local ship = steeringValue:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Model")
    if not ship then return end

    local character = localPlayer.Character
    local characterHandler = character and character:FindFirstChild("CharacterHandler")
    
    if characterHandler then
        local requests = characterHandler:FindFirstChild("Requests")
        local equipWeapon = requests and requests:FindFirstChild("DrawWeapon")
        if equipWeapon then
            equipWeapon:FireServer(true)
            equipWeapon:FireServer(false)
        end
    end

    local moveDirection = humanoid.MoveDirection

    if moveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
        local velocityVector = moveDirection.Unit * speed
        local bv = ship:FindFirstChild("BodyVelocity", true)

        if bv then
            bv.Velocity = velocityVector
            bv.P = 99999
        else
            ship:TranslateBy(moveDirection.Unit * dt * speed)
        end
    else
        local bv = ship:FindFirstChild("BodyVelocity", true)
        if bv then
            bv.Velocity = Vector3.zero
            bv.P = 99999
        end
    end

    local shipControls = steeringValue:FindFirstChild("ShipControls")
    if shipControls then
        local args = {
            "s",
            true,
            rootPart.CFrame
        }
        shipControls:FireServer(unpack(args))
    end
end

	---@param rootPart BasePart
	---@param humanoid Humanoid
	local function updateSpeedHack(rootPart, humanoid)
		if Configuration.expectToggleValue("Fly") then
			return
		end

		rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity * Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)

		local moveDirection = humanoid.MoveDirection
		if moveDirection.Magnitude <= 0.001 then
			return
		end

		rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity
			+ moveDirection.Unit * Configuration.expectOptionValue("SpeedhackSpeed")
	end

	-- CFrame Speed (ported from mira): CFrame-based movement in the humanoid's move direction. Smoother
	-- than velocity speedhack; standalone from Speedhack.
	---@param rootPart BasePart
	---@param humanoid Humanoid
	---@param deltaTime number
	local function updateCFrameSpeed(rootPart, humanoid, deltaTime)
		if Configuration.expectToggleValue("Fly") then
			return
		end

		local moveDirection = humanoid.MoveDirection
		if not moveDirection or moveDirection.Magnitude <= 0.001 then
			return
		end

		local speed = Configuration.expectOptionValue("CFrameSpeedMultiplier") or 1
		rootPart.CFrame = rootPart.CFrame + moveDirection * (speed * deltaTime)
	end

	---@param rootPart BasePart
	local function updateInfiniteJump(rootPart)
		if Configuration.expectToggleValue("Fly") then
			return
		end

		if not userInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
			return
		end

		rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)
		rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity
			+ Vector3.new(0, Configuration.expectOptionValue("InfiniteJumpBoost"), 0)
	end

	---@param rootPart BasePart
	---@param humanoid Humanoid
	local flyActive = false -- tracks fly on->off transitions so we restore the controller/sensor/speed-cap once

	-- Project Rain's fly: a tagged BodyVelocity driven by camera-relative WASD + Space/LeftControl, with any
	-- stray body-movers cleared each frame. The "AllowedBM" CollectionService tag is Deepwoken's body-mover
	-- allowlist, so the anticheat doesn't flag/strip it; OverrideSpeedCap uncaps speed while flying; the
	-- AirController + GroundSensor tweaks stop the ground controller from fighting the movement.
	local function updateFlyHack(character, rootPart, humanoid)
		local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not camera then
			return
		end

		if rootPart:FindFirstChild("HelioFlight") then
			rootPart.HelioFlight:Destroy()
		end

		-- Direction straight from the camera + held keys (PR).
		local look = camera.CFrame.LookVector
		local direction = Vector3.zero
		local keymap = {
			[Enum.KeyCode.W] = look,
			[Enum.KeyCode.S] = -look,
			[Enum.KeyCode.A] = Vector3.new(look.Z, 0, -look.X),
			[Enum.KeyCode.D] = Vector3.new(-look.Z, 0, look.X),
			[Enum.KeyCode.Space] = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0),
			[Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl] = Vector3.new(0, -1, 0),
		}
		for key, vec in pairs(keymap) do
			if userInputService:IsKeyDown(key) then
				direction = direction + vec
			end
		end
		if direction.Magnitude > 0 then
			direction = direction.Unit
		else
			direction = Vector3.zero
		end

		-- Clear stray body-movers, then (re)use our tagged one.
		local collectionService = game:GetService("CollectionService")
		for _, descendant in pairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("BodyVelocity") and descendant.Name ~= "LumaFly" then
				descendant:Destroy()
			end
		end

		local flyBodyVelocity = InstanceWrapper.create(movementMaid, "flyBodyVelocity", "BodyVelocity", rootPart)
		flyBodyVelocity.Name = "LumaFly"
		flyBodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1000000, 1000000, 1000000)
		pcall(function()
			collectionService:AddTag(flyBodyVelocity, "AllowedBM")
		end)

		-- Speed: user's FlySpeed, clamped higher while knocked (PR caps 250 / 350 knocked).
		local effectReplicator = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("EffectReplicator")
		local effectReplicatorModule = effectReplicator and require(effectReplicator)
		local knocked = effectReplicatorModule and effectReplicatorModule:FindEffect("Knocked")
		local speed = math.min(knocked and 350 or 250, Configuration.expectOptionValue("FlySpeed") or 50)

		flyBodyVelocity.Velocity = direction * speed

		-- Keep the air controller active + the ground sensor from grabbing us.
		local controllerManager = character:FindFirstChild("ControllerManager")
		if controllerManager then
			local airController = controllerManager:FindFirstChild("AirController")
			if airController then
				controllerManager.ActiveController = airController
			end
		end

		local groundSensor = rootPart:FindFirstChild("GroundSensor")
		if groundSensor then
			if Configuration.expectToggleValue("NoClip") then
				groundSensor.SensorMode = Enum.SensorMode.ClassicLadder
				groundSensor.UpdateType = Enum.SensorUpdateType.OnRead
			else
				groundSensor.SensorMode = Enum.SensorMode.Floor
				groundSensor.UpdateType = Enum.SensorUpdateType.Manual
			end
		end

		-- Uncap speed while flying.
		if effectReplicatorModule and not effectReplicatorModule:FindEffect("OverrideSpeedCap") then
			pcall(function()
				effectReplicatorModule:CreateEffect("OverrideSpeedCap")
			end)
		end
	end

	local lastMomentumUpdate = 0
	local function updateMaxMomentumSpoofer()
		if tick() - lastMomentumUpdate < 0.1 then
			return
		end
		lastMomentumUpdate = tick()

		local effectReplicator = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("EffectReplicator")
		local effectReplicatorModule = effectReplicator and require(effectReplicator)
		if not effectReplicatorModule then
			return
		end

		local forcedMomentumEffect = effectReplicatorModule:FindEffect("ForceMomentum")

		if not Configuration.expectToggleValue("MaxMomentumSpoof") then
			if forcedMomentumEffect then
				forcedMomentumEffect.Disabled = true
				forcedMomentumEffect.Value = 0
			end
			return
		end

		if not forcedMomentumEffect then
			return effectReplicatorModule:CreateEffect("ForceMomentum", { Value = 10 })
		end

		if forcedMomentumEffect.Value ~= 10 then
			forcedMomentumEffect.Value = 10
		end

		if forcedMomentumEffect.Disabled ~= false then
			forcedMomentumEffect.Disabled = false
		end
	end

	---@note: The client recomputes WalkSpeed from effects every frame, so a
	local function updateForceSpeedBuff()
		local effectReplicator = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("EffectReplicator")
		local effectReplicatorModule = effectReplicator and require(effectReplicator)
		if not effectReplicatorModule then
			return
		end

		local forceSpeedEffect = effectReplicatorModule:FindEffect("ForceSpeedBuff")

		if not Configuration.expectToggleValue("ForceSpeedBuff") then
			if forceSpeedEffect then
				forceSpeedEffect.Value = 0
				forceSpeedEffect.Disabled = true
			end
			return
		end

		if not forceSpeedEffect then
			return effectReplicatorModule:CreateEffect("ForceSpeedBuff")
		end

		local amount = Configuration.expectOptionValue("ForceSpeedBuffAmount") or 0.0
		if forceSpeedEffect.Value ~= amount then
			forceSpeedEffect.Value = amount
		end

		forceSpeedEffect.Disabled = false
	end

	---@param character Model
	local function updateAgilitySpoofer(character)
		local agility = character:FindFirstChild("PassiveAgility")
		if not agility then
			return
		end

		---@note: For every 10 investment points, there are two real agility points.
		local agilitySpoofValue = 8 + (Options.AgilitySpoof.Value / 10) * 2

		if Toggles.BoostAgilityDirectly.Value then
			agilitySpoofValue = Options.AgilitySpoof.Value
		end

		agilitySpoofer:set(agility, "Value", agilitySpoofValue)
	end

	local function updateTweenToBack()
		local validTargets = Finder.geir(300, Configuration.expectToggleValue("AttachIgnorePlayers"))
		if not validTargets then
			return true
		end

		local attachTarget = Configuration.expectToggleValue("StickyAttach") and cachedTarget or validTargets[1]
		if not attachTarget then
			return true
		end

		if not attachTarget.Parent then
			return false
		end

		local attachTargetHrp = attachTarget:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not attachTargetHrp then
			return false
		end

		local attachTargetHumanoid = attachTarget:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
		if not attachTargetHumanoid then
			return false
		end

		if attachTargetHumanoid.Health <= 0 then
			return false
		end

		cachedTarget = cachedTarget or attachTarget

		local offsetCFrame = CFrame.new(
			0.0,
			Configuration.expectOptionValue("HeightOffset"),
			Configuration.expectOptionValue("BackOffset")
		)

		local targetPosition = (attachTargetHrp.CFrame * offsetCFrame).Position
		local goalCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(targetPosition, attachTargetHrp.Position)

		Tweening.goal("TweenToBack", goalCFrame, false)

		return true
	end

	-- Infinite zoom out: crank the local player's max camera zoom while enabled, restore it when off.
	local function updateInfiniteZoom()
		local localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer
		if not localPlayer then
			return
		end

		if Configuration.expectToggleValue("InfiniteZoom") then
			if originalMaxZoom == nil then
				originalMaxZoom = localPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance
			end
			-- Re-assert every frame; the game resets this on some state changes.
			if localPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance ~= 1000000 then
				localPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 1000000
			end
		elseif originalMaxZoom ~= nil then
			localPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = originalMaxZoom
			originalMaxZoom = nil
		end
	end

	-- Freecam: Project Rain's freecam module. Detaches the camera (WASD move, Q/E down/up, mouse
	-- look, Up/Down adjust speed, LeftShift slow). The character stays put. The module runs its own
	-- BindToRenderStep, so this just enters/exits on the toggle and feeds the speed slider in.
	local function updateFreecam()
		if Configuration.expectToggleValue("Freecam") then
			if not Freecam.running() then
				Freecam.enter()
			end

			Freecam.set_speed((Configuration.expectOptionValue("FreecamSpeed") or 60) / 60)
		elseif Freecam.running() then
			Freecam.exit()
		end
	end

	---@param dt number
	local function updateMovement(dt)
		updateInfiniteZoom()
		updateFreecam(dt)

		local localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer
		local character = localPlayer.Character
		if not character then
			return
		end

		local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not rootPart then
			return
		end

		local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
		if not humanoid then
			return
		end

		if not Configuration.expectToggleValue("TweenToBack") or not updateTweenToBack() then
			cachedTarget = nil
		end

		if Configuration.expectToggleValue("AgilitySpoof") then
			updateAgilitySpoofer(character)
		else
			agilitySpoofer:restore()
		end

		updateMaxMomentumSpoofer()

		updateForceSpeedBuff()

		if Tweening.active or Configuration.expectToggleValue("NoClip") then
			updateNoClip(character, rootPart)
			updateNoClipHasAnyHook(true)
		else
			noClipMap:restore()
			updateNoClipHasAnyHook(false)
		end

		if Tweening.active then
			return
		end

		if Configuration.expectToggleValue("Fly") then
			flyActive = true
			updateFlyHack(character, rootPart, humanoid)
		else
			movementMaid["flyBodyVelocity"] = nil
			if flyActive then
				flyActive = false
				-- Restore normal movement: hand control back to the ground controller, reset the ground
				-- sensor, and drop the speed-cap override the fly loop created.
				local controllerManager = character and character:FindFirstChild("ControllerManager")
				if controllerManager then
					local groundController = controllerManager:FindFirstChild("GroundController")
					if groundController then
						controllerManager.ActiveController = groundController
					end
				end
				local groundSensor = rootPart and rootPart:FindFirstChild("GroundSensor")
				if groundSensor then
					groundSensor.SensorMode = Enum.SensorMode.Floor
					groundSensor.UpdateType = Enum.SensorUpdateType.Manual
				end
				local effectReplicator = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("EffectReplicator")
				local effectReplicatorModule = effectReplicator and require(effectReplicator)
				local capEffect = effectReplicatorModule and effectReplicatorModule:FindEffect("OverrideSpeedCap")
				if capEffect then
					pcall(function()
						capEffect:Debris(1)
					end)
				end
			end
		end
		
		if Configuration.expectToggleValue("BoatSpeed") then
			updateBoatfly(rootPart, humanoid)
		end

		if Configuration.expectToggleValue("Speedhack") then
			updateSpeedHack(rootPart, humanoid)
		end

		-- CFrame Speed is standalone and can be used independently of Speedhack.
		if Configuration.expectToggleValue("CFrameSpeed") then
			updateCFrameSpeed(rootPart, humanoid, dt)
		end

		if Configuration.expectToggleValue("InfiniteJump") then
			updateInfiniteJump(rootPart)
		end
	end

	function Movement.init()
		movementMaid:add(preSimulation:connect("Movement_PreSimulation", updateMovement))
		movementMaid:add(heartbeat:connect("Movement_Heartbeat", Tweening.update))

		if not LPH_OBFUSCATED or shared.Luma.verbose then
			Logger.warn("Movement initialized.")
		end
	end

	function Movement.detach()
		movementMaid:clean()

		if Freecam.running() then
			Freecam.exit()
		end

		Logger.warn("Movement detached.")
	end

	return Movement
end)()
end)
__bundle_register("Utility/Freecam", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
---@class Freecam
--- Project Rain's freecam, adapted for luma. Detaches the camera from the character:
---   WASD  - move (relative to look)      Q / E        - down / up
---   mouse - look                         Up / Down    - adjust speed live
---   LeftShift - move slowly
--- The character stays put. Speed is scaled by the FreecamSpeed slider via set_speed().
local Freecam = {}

local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local ContextActionService = game:GetService("ContextActionService")

local Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
	local newCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if newCamera then
		Camera = newCamera
	end
end)

local INPUT_PRIORITY = Enum.ContextActionPriority.High.Value

local running = false
local cameraPos = Vector3.new()
local cameraRot = Vector2.new()

local NAV_KEYBOARD_SPEED = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
local PAN_MOUSE_SPEED = Vector2.new(1, 1) * (math.pi / 64)
local NAV_ADJ_SPEED = 0.75
local NAV_SHIFT_MUL = 0.25

local navSpeed = 2

local keyboard = { W = 0, A = 0, S = 0, D = 0, E = 0, Q = 0, Up = 0, Down = 0 }
local mouse = { Delta = Vector2.new() }

local function inputVel(dt)
	navSpeed = math.clamp(navSpeed + dt * (keyboard.Up - keyboard.Down) * NAV_ADJ_SPEED, 0.01, 4)

	local kKeyboard = Vector3.new(
		keyboard.D - keyboard.A,
		keyboard.E - keyboard.Q,
		keyboard.S - keyboard.W
	) * NAV_KEYBOARD_SPEED

	local shift = UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift)

	return kKeyboard * (navSpeed * (shift and NAV_SHIFT_MUL or 1))
end

local function inputPan()
	local kMouse = mouse.Delta * PAN_MOUSE_SPEED
	mouse.Delta = Vector2.new()
	return kMouse
end

local function keypress(_, state, input)
	keyboard[input.KeyCode.Name] = state == Enum.UserInputState.Begin and 1 or 0
	return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
end

local function mousePan(_, _, input)
	local delta = input.Delta
	mouse.Delta = Vector2.new(-delta.Y, -delta.X)
	return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
end

local function zero(t)
	for k in next, t do
		t[k] = 0
	end
end

local function startCapture()
	ContextActionService:BindActionAtPriority(
		"FreecamKeyboard",
		keypress,
		false,
		INPUT_PRIORITY,
		Enum.KeyCode.W,
		Enum.KeyCode.A,
		Enum.KeyCode.S,
		Enum.KeyCode.D,
		Enum.KeyCode.E,
		Enum.KeyCode.Q,
		Enum.KeyCode.Up,
		Enum.KeyCode.Down
	)
	ContextActionService:BindActionAtPriority(
		"FreecamMousePan",
		mousePan,
		false,
		INPUT_PRIORITY,
		Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
	)
end

local function stopCapture()
	navSpeed = 1
	zero(keyboard)
	mouse.Delta = Vector2.new()
	ContextActionService:UnbindAction("FreecamKeyboard")
	ContextActionService:UnbindAction("FreecamMousePan")
end

local function getFocusDistance(cameraFrame)
	local znear = 0.1
	local viewport = Camera.ViewportSize
	local projy = 2 * math.tan(70 / 2)
	local projx = viewport.X / viewport.Y * projy
	local fx = cameraFrame.RightVector
	local fy = cameraFrame.UpVector
	local fz = cameraFrame.LookVector

	local minVect = Vector3.new()
	local minDist = 512

	for x = 0, 1, 0.5 do
		for y = 0, 1, 0.5 do
			local cx = (x - 0.5) * projx
			local cy = (y - 0.5) * projy
			local offset = fx * cx - fy * cy + fz
			local origin = cameraFrame.Position + offset * znear

			local result = workspace:Raycast(origin, offset.Unit * minDist)
			local hitPos = result and result.Position or (origin + offset.Unit * minDist)
			local dist = (hitPos - origin).Magnitude
			if minDist > dist then
				minDist = dist
				minVect = offset.Unit
			end
		end
	end

	return fz:Dot(minVect) * minDist
end

local function stepFreecam(dt)
	local vel = inputVel(dt)
	local pan = inputPan()

	cameraRot = cameraRot + pan * Vector2.new(0.75, 1) * 8 * dt
	cameraRot = Vector2.new(math.clamp(cameraRot.X, -math.rad(90), math.rad(90)), cameraRot.Y % (2 * math.pi))

	local cameraCFrame = CFrame.new(cameraPos)
		* CFrame.fromOrientation(cameraRot.X, cameraRot.Y, 0)
		* CFrame.new(vel * 64 * dt)
	cameraPos = cameraCFrame.Position

	Camera.CFrame = cameraCFrame
	Camera.Focus = cameraCFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -getFocusDistance(cameraCFrame))
end

-- Saved player camera + mouse state (restored on exit).
local savedCameraType, savedCFrame, savedFocus, savedMouseIcon, savedMouseBehavior

local function push()
	savedCameraType = Camera.CameraType
	Camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom

	savedCFrame = Camera.CFrame
	savedFocus = Camera.Focus

	savedMouseIcon = UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled
	UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = true

	savedMouseBehavior = UserInputService.MouseBehavior
	UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
end

local function pop()
	Camera.CameraType = savedCameraType or Enum.CameraType.Custom
	Camera.CFrame = savedCFrame or Camera.CFrame
	Camera.Focus = savedFocus or Camera.Focus
	UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = savedMouseIcon
	if savedMouseBehavior then
		UserInputService.MouseBehavior = savedMouseBehavior
	end

	savedCameraType, savedCFrame, savedFocus, savedMouseIcon, savedMouseBehavior = nil, nil, nil, nil, nil
end

---@param pos CFrame? Optional starting camera CFrame.
function Freecam.enter(pos)
	if running then
		Freecam.exit()
	end

	local cameraCFrame = pos or Camera.CFrame
	cameraRot = Vector2.new()
	cameraPos = cameraCFrame.Position

	push()
	RunService:BindToRenderStep("Freecam", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value, stepFreecam)
	startCapture()
	running = true
end

function Freecam.exit()
	if not running then
		return
	end

	stopCapture()
	RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("Freecam")
	pop()
	running = false
end

---@param speed number Multiplier on the base nav speed (1 = default feel).
function Freecam.set_speed(speed)
	NAV_KEYBOARD_SPEED = Vector3.new(speed, speed, speed)
end

---@return boolean
function Freecam.running()
	return running
end

return Freecam
end)
__bundle_register("Features/Automation/InteligenceFarm", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
local InteligenceFarm = {}

---@module Utility.Maid
local Maid = require("Utility/Maid")

---@module Utility.Signal
local Signal = require("Utility/Signal")

---@module Utility.Attributes
local Attributes = require("Utility/Attributes")

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Throttle
local throttle = require("Utility/Throttle")

local players = game:GetService("Players")
local runService = game:GetService("RunService")

local renderStepped = Signal.new(runService.RenderStepped)

local autoIntelligenceMaid = Maid.new()

local updateInteligence = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
	local inteligenceFarm = Toggles["AutoIntelligence"]
	if not inteligenceFarm or not inteligenceFarm.Value then
		return
	end

	local inteligenceFarmCap = Options["IntelligenceCap"]
	if not inteligenceFarmCap then
		return
	end

	local localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer
	local localPlayerCharacter = localPlayer.Character
	if not localPlayerCharacter then
		return
	end

	if not Attributes.isNotAtCap(localPlayerCharacter, "Stat_Intelligence", inteligenceFarmCap.Value - 1) then
		Logger.longNotify("Intelligence AutoFarm is automatically stopping.")
		return inteligenceFarm:SetValue(false)
	end

	local humanoid = localPlayerCharacter:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
	if not humanoid then
		return
	end

	local backpack = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
	if not backpack then
		return
	end

	local characterBook = localPlayerCharacter:FindFirstChild("Math Textbook")
	local backpackBook = backpack:FindFirstChild("Math Textbook")

	if not characterBook and backpackBook then
		return humanoid:EquipTool(backpackBook)
	end

	local choicePrompt = localPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ChoicePrompt")
	if not choicePrompt then
		return characterBook and characterBook:Activate()
	end

	local choiceRemote = choicePrompt:FindFirstChild("Choice")
	if not choiceRemote then
		return
	end

	local choiceFrame = choicePrompt:FindFirstChild("ChoiceFrame")
	if not choiceFrame then
		return
	end

	local descSheet = choiceFrame:FindFirstChild("DescSheet")
	local description = descSheet and descSheet:FindFirstChild("Desc")
	if not description then
		return
	end

	local options = choiceFrame:FindFirstChild("Options")
	if not options then
		return
	end

	local answers = {}

	for _, instance in next, options:GetChildren() do
		if not instance:IsA("TextButton") then
			continue
		end

		local number = tonumber(instance.Name)
		if not number then
			continue
		end

		table.insert(answers, number)
	end

	local descriptionText = tostring(description.Text)
	local parsedDescription = descriptionText:gsub("What is", ""):gsub(" ", ""):gsub("?", ""):gsub("by", "")

	local operation = nil

	if parsedDescription:match("divided") then
		operation = "divided"
	end

	if parsedDescription:match("times") then
		operation = "times"
	end

	if parsedDescription:match("plus") then
		operation = "plus"
	end

	if parsedDescription:match("minus") then
		operation = "minus"
	end

	if not operation then
		return
	end

	local parsedNumbers = parsedDescription:split(operation)
	local firstNumber = tonumber(parsedNumbers[1] or "")
	local secondNumber = tonumber(parsedNumbers[2] or "")
	if not firstNumber or not secondNumber then
		return
	end

	local answer = nil

	if operation == "divided" then
		answer = firstNumber / secondNumber
	end

	if operation == "times" then
		answer = firstNumber * secondNumber
	end

	if operation == "plus" then
		answer = firstNumber + secondNumber
	end

	if operation == "minus" then
		answer = firstNumber - secondNumber
	end

	local closestAnswer = nil
	local closestDifference = nil

	for _, possible in next, answers do
		local delta = math.abs(answer - possible)

		if closestDifference and delta > closestDifference then
			continue
		end

		closestAnswer = possible
		closestDifference = delta
	end

	if not closestAnswer then
		return
	end

	choiceRemote:FireServer(tostring(closestAnswer))
end)

function InteligenceFarm.init()
	autoIntelligenceMaid:add(renderStepped:connect("AttributeFarmIntelligence_OnPreRender", throttle(1 / 10, updateInteligence)))

	if not LPH_OBFUSCATED or shared.Luma.verbose then
		Logger.warn("Inteligence Farm initialized.")
	end
end

function InteligenceFarm.detach()
	autoIntelligenceMaid:clean()

	Logger.warn("Inteligence Farm detached.")
end

return InteligenceFarm
end)
__bundle_register("Utility/Attributes", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
return LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
	local Attributes = {}

	---@param playerCharacter Model
	---@param attribute string
	---@param cap string
	---@return boolean
	function Attributes.isNotAtCap(playerCharacter, attribute, cap)
		local useDefault = cap == ""
		local maxStat = useDefault and 100 or tonumber(cap)
		local statCap = maxStat == 0 and 100 or maxStat

		local currentStat = tonumber(playerCharacter:GetAttribute(attribute) or 0)
		if not currentStat or currentStat >= statCap then
			return false
		end

		return true
	end

	return Attributes
end)()
end)
__bundle_register("Features/Automation/CharismaFarm", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
local CharismaFarm = {}

---@module Utility.Maid
local Maid = require("Utility/Maid")

---@module Utility.Signal
local Signal = require("Utility/Signal")

---@module Utility.Attributes
local Attributes = require("Utility/Attributes")

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Throttle
local throttle = require("Utility/Throttle")

local players = game:GetService("Players")
local runService = game:GetService("RunService")

local renderStepped = Signal.new(runService.RenderStepped)

local autoCharismaMaid = Maid.new()

local updateCharisma = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
	local charismaFarm = Toggles["AutoCharisma"]
	if not charismaFarm or not charismaFarm.Value then
		return
	end

	local charismaFarmCap = Options["CharismaCap"]
	if not charismaFarmCap then
		return
	end

	local localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer
	local localPlayerCharacter = localPlayer.Character
	if not localPlayerCharacter then
		return
	end

	if not Attributes.isNotAtCap(localPlayerCharacter, "Stat_Charisma", charismaFarmCap.Value - 1) then
		Logger.longNotify("Charisma AutoFarm is automatically stopping.")
		return charismaFarm:SetValue(false)
	end

	local humanoid = localPlayerCharacter:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
	if not humanoid then
		return
	end

	local backpack = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
	if not backpack then
		return
	end

	local characterBook = localPlayerCharacter:FindFirstChild("How to Make Friends")
	local backpackBook = backpack:FindFirstChild("How to Make Friends")

	if not characterBook and backpackBook then
		return humanoid:EquipTool(backpackBook)
	end

	local choicePrompt = localPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ChoicePrompt")
	if not choicePrompt then
		return characterBook and characterBook:Activate()
	end

	local choiceFrame = choicePrompt:FindFirstChild("ChoiceFrame")
	if not choiceFrame then
		return
	end

	local chatChoice = choicePrompt:FindFirstChild("ChatChoice")
	if not chatChoice then
		return
	end

	local description = choiceFrame:FindFirstChild("Desc")
	if not description then
		return
	end

	local text = description.Text:split("\n")
	local charismaLine = text[2]:sub(2, -2)

	chatChoice:InvokeServer(charismaLine)
end)

function CharismaFarm.init()
	autoCharismaMaid:add(renderStepped:connect("AttributeFarmCharisma_OnPreRender", throttle(1 / 10, updateCharisma)))

	if not LPH_OBFUSCATED or shared.Luma.verbose then
		Logger.warn("Charisma Farm initialized.")
	end
end

function CharismaFarm.detach()
	autoCharismaMaid:clean()

	Logger.warn("Charisma Farm detached.")
end

return CharismaFarm
end)
__bundle_register("Menu", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
local Menu = {}

---@module GUI.ThemeManager
local ThemeManager = require("GUI/ThemeManager")

---@module GUI.SaveManager
local SaveManager = require("GUI/SaveManager")

---@module GUI.Library
local Library = require("GUI/Linoria")

---@module Menu.CombatTab
local HomeTab = require("Menu/HomeTab")

local CombatTab = require("Menu/CombatTab")

---@module Menu.GameTab
local GameTab = require("Menu/GameTab")

---@module Menu.AutomationTab
local AutomationTab = require("Menu/AutomationTab")

---@module Menu.TimingBuilderTab
local TimingBuilderTab = require("Menu/TimingBuilderTab")

---@module Menu.VisualsTab
local VisualsTab = require("Menu/VisualsTab")

---@module Menu.ExploitTab
local ExploitTab = require("Menu/ExploitTab")

---@module Menu.LumaTab
local LumaTab = require("Menu/LumaTab")

---@module Menu.UtilityTab
local UtilityTab = require("Menu/UtilityTab")

---@module Menu.ConfigTab
local ConfigTab = require("Menu/ConfigTab")

---@module Menu.AdminTab

---@module Menu.CustomTab
local CustomTab = require("Menu/CustomTab")

---@module Features.Custom.Custom
local Custom = require("Features/Custom/Custom")

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Maid
local Maid = require("Utility/Maid")

---@module Utility.Signal
local Signal = require("Utility/Signal")

---@module Utility.Configuration
local Configuration = require("Utility/Configuration")

local runService = game:GetService("RunService")
local stats = game:GetService("Stats")
local players = game:GetService("Players")
local userInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

local menuMaid = Maid.new()
local menuAlive = false
local uiIssueKeys = {}

local function reportUiIssue(key, message)
    if uiIssueKeys[key] then return end
    uiIssueKeys[key] = true
    pcall(HomeTab.recordIssue, tostring(message))
end
local ignoreSavedControls = {
    "Fly", "NoClip", "Speedhack", "InfiniteJump", "TweenToObjective", "TweenToBack",
}

local MENU_TITLE = "CM1  |  DEEPWOKEN"

if LRM_UserNote then
	MENU_TITLE = string.format(
		"CM1  |  DEEPWOKEN",
		string.sub("6c10af8d79e3dc253ba6db7f343613361c37e378", 1, 6)
	)
end

function Menu.init()
    if menuAlive then return end
    table.clear(uiIssueKeys)
    menuAlive = true
	local window = Library:CreateWindow({
		Title = MENU_TITLE,
		Center = true,
		AutoShow = not shared.Luma.silent,
		TabPadding = 8,
		MenuFadeTime = 0.0,
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(625, 650),
	})

	ThemeManager:SetLibrary(Library)
	ThemeManager:SetFolder("CM1-Deepwoken-Themes")

	SaveManager:SetLibrary(Library)
	SaveManager:IgnoreThemeSettings()
	SaveManager:SetFolder("CM1-Deepwoken-Configs")
	SaveManager:SetIgnoreIndexes(ignoreSavedControls)

	-- Eager: only the first tab, so the menu has content the instant it auto-shows. The remaining
	-- tabs are built on background frames (see the deferred builder below) so the menu appears
	-- immediately instead of blocking ~1s on control creation.
	HomeTab.init(window)

    menuMaid:mark(task.spawn(function()
        while menuAlive do
            task.wait(0.5)
            if not menuAlive then break end
            local ok, err = pcall(function()


			local networkStats = stats:FindFirstChild("Network")
			local workspaceStats = stats:FindFirstChild("Workspace")
			local performanceStats = stats:FindFirstChild("PerformanceStats")
			local serverStats = networkStats and networkStats:FindFirstChild("ServerStatsItem") or nil

			local pingData = serverStats and serverStats:FindFirstChild("Data Ping") or nil
			local heartbeatData = workspaceStats and workspaceStats:FindFirstChild("Heartbeat") or nil
			local cpuData = performanceStats and performanceStats:FindFirstChild("CPU") or nil
			local gpuData = performanceStats and performanceStats:FindFirstChild("GPU") or nil

			local ping = pingData and pingData:GetValue() or 0.0
			local fps = heartbeatData and heartbeatData:GetValue() or 0.0
			local cpu = cpuData and cpuData:GetValue() or 0.0
			local gpu = gpuData and gpuData:GetValue() or 0.0

			local mouse = players.LocalPlayer and players.LocalPlayer:GetMouse()
			local character = players.LocalPlayer and players.LocalPlayer.Character
			local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local position = humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart.Position
			local positionFormat = position and string.format("(%.2f, %.2f, %.2f)", position.X, position.Y, position.Z)
				or "N/A"

			local str = string.format("%s | %.2fms | %.1f/s | %.1fms | %.1fms", MENU_TITLE, ping, fps, cpu, gpu)

			if Configuration.expectToggleValue("ShowDebugInformation") then
				str = str .. string.format(" | %s", positionFormat)
				str = str .. string.format(" | %s", mouse and mouse.Target and mouse.Target:GetFullName() or "N/A")
			end

			-- YSL's SetWatermark(Text, RText, Sections) measures the frame width from RText, not Text, so
			-- it must be passed too (Obsidian sized off the single arg). Without it the frame collapses and
			-- the watermark renders blank.
            if menuAlive then Library:SetWatermark(str) end
            end)
            if not ok then
                reportUiIssue("watermark", "Watermark: " .. tostring(err))
            end
        end
    end))

    local menuUIHidden = false
    menuMaid:mark(task.spawn(function()
        while menuAlive do
            task.wait(0.25)
            if not menuAlive then break end
            local ok, err = pcall(function()

			local playerGui = players.LocalPlayer and players.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
			local backpackGui = playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild("BackpackGui")
			local leftFrame = backpackGui and backpackGui:FindFirstChild("LeftFrame")
			local inMenu = leftFrame and leftFrame:IsA("GuiObject") and leftFrame.Visible

			if Configuration.expectToggleValue("HideUIInMenus") and inMenu then
				Library.KeybindFrame.Visible = false
				Library:SetWatermarkVisibility(false)
				menuUIHidden = true
				return
			end

			if not menuUIHidden then
				return
			end

			menuUIHidden = false
			Library.KeybindFrame.Visible = Options.KeybindList and Options.KeybindList:GetState() or false
			Library:SetWatermarkVisibility(Options.Watermark and Options.Watermark:GetState() or false)
            end)
            if not ok then
                reportUiIssue("visibility", "Visibility: " .. tostring(err))
            end
        end
    end))

	local inputBegan = Signal.new(userInputService.InputBegan)

	menuMaid:mark(inputBegan:connect("Menu_InputBegan", function(input, gameProcessed)
		if gameProcessed then
			return
		end

		if not Configuration.expectToggleValue("ShowDebugInformation") then
			return
		end

		if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.F8 then
			local mouse = players.LocalPlayer and players.LocalPlayer:GetMouse()
			local target = mouse and mouse.Target
			if not target then
				return
			end

			target.Name = tostring(math.random(100000, 1000000))

			return Logger.mnnotify("The target has been renamed to '%s' as a marker.", target.Name)
		end

		if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Equals and Library.Recording then
			local Character = players.LocalPlayer and players.LocalPlayer.Character
			local HumanoidRootPart = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local Position = HumanoidRootPart and HumanoidRootPart.Position
			local PositionFormat = Position
					and string.format("CFrame.new(%.2f, %.2f, %.2f)", Position.X, Position.Y, Position.Z)
				or "N/A"

			Library.PositionList[#Library.PositionList + 1] = PositionFormat
		end
	end))

	-- Deferred tab build: every tab except the first is created here, one per frame, so the menu is
	-- visible and interactive immediately and the rest pop into the sidebar as they finish. The menu
	-- keybind binding and autoload run only AFTER every tab exists -- their controls (Toggles/Options/
	-- KeyPickers, incl. Options.MenuKeybind) must be built or saved configs, keybinds and default-on
	-- features would read nil and silently not apply.
	menuMaid:mark(task.spawn(function()
        local errors = 0
		local function diagTab(n, f)
            if not menuAlive then return end
			local ok, e = pcall(f, window)
			if not ok then
                errors += 1
                reportUiIssue("tab:" .. n, n .. " init: " .. tostring(e))
            end
			task.wait()
		end
        diagTab("CombatTab", CombatTab.init)
        diagTab("CombatAntiExploit", CombatTab.initAntiExploit)
		diagTab("TimingBuilderTab", TimingBuilderTab.init)
		diagTab("VisualsTab", VisualsTab.init)
		diagTab("UtilityTab", UtilityTab.init)
		diagTab("AutomationTab", AutomationTab.init)
		diagTab("LumaTab", LumaTab.init)
		-- Custom tab is unfinished -- not registered yet (CustomTab.init is intentionally not called).
		-- diagTab("CustomTab", CustomTab.init)
		diagTab("ConfigTab", ConfigTab.init)

        if not menuAlive then return end
        Library:IgnoreTemporaryControls(SaveManager, ignoreSavedControls)
        Library.ToggleKeybind = Options.MenuKeybind
        if errors == 0 then
            local ok, err = pcall(SaveManager.LoadAutoloadConfig, SaveManager)
            if not ok then reportUiIssue("autoload", "Config autoload: " .. tostring(err)) end
        else
            reportUiIssue("tabs", tostring(errors) .. " tab(s) failed; autoload skipped")
        end

		if not LPH_OBFUSCATED or shared.Luma.verbose then
			Logger.warn("Menu initialized.")
		end
	end))
end

function Menu.detach()
    menuAlive = false
	menuMaid:clean()
    pcall(HomeTab.detach)

	-- Tear down any running custom features (their render/step/input connections outlive the ScreenGui).
	pcall(Custom.stopAll)

    pcall(TimingBuilderTab.detach)

    pcall(Library.Unload, Library)

	Logger.warn("Menu detached.")
end

return Menu
end)
__bundle_register("Features/Custom/Custom", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
-- Manager for user-built custom features: storage (workspace files), sandboxed execution via CustomAPI,
-- and optional AI code generation (OpenAI / Anthropic / Google) against a described Luma feature API.

---@module Utility.Filesystem
local Filesystem = require("Utility/Filesystem")

---@module Utility.JSON
local JSON = require("Utility/JSON")

---@module Features.Custom.CustomAPI
local CustomAPI = require("Features/Custom/CustomAPI")

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

local Custom = {}

local storage = Filesystem.new("Luma/Custom")

local host = nil -- the panel widgets render into (set by CustomTab)
local running = {} -- name -> CustomAPI instance
local settingsCache = nil

local DEFAULT_SETTINGS = {
	provider = "OpenAI",
	model = "gpt-4o-mini",
	apiKey = "",
	autorun = {},
}

Custom.PROVIDERS = { "OpenAI", "Anthropic", "Google" }
Custom.DEFAULT_MODELS = {
	OpenAI = "gpt-4o-mini",
	Anthropic = "claude-3-5-sonnet-latest",
	Google = "gemini-1.5-flash",
}

Custom.TEMPLATE = table.concat({
	"-- Custom feature. The Luma API is available as globals (and under `Luma`).",
	"-- Widgets appear in a section on the right; everything is cleaned up on reload.",
	"",
	'local enabled = Toggle("Enabled", function(on)',
	'    Notify(on and "Enabled" or "Disabled")',
	"end)",
	"",
	'Slider("Speed", { min = 16, max = 200, default = 16, callback = function(v)',
	"    local hum = Humanoid()",
	"    if hum then hum.WalkSpeed = v end",
	"end })",
	"",
	"OnRender(function()",
	"    if not enabled.get() then return end",
	"    -- runs every frame while this feature is loaded",
	"end)",
}, "\n")

-- ------------------------------------------------------------ settings
function Custom.settings()
	if settingsCache then
		return settingsCache
	end
	local data = nil
	pcall(function()
		if storage:file("settings.json") then
			local ok, decoded = pcall(JSON.decode, storage:read("settings.json"))
			if ok and type(decoded) == "table" then
				data = decoded
			end
		end
	end)
	data = data or {}
	for k, v in pairs(DEFAULT_SETTINGS) do
		if data[k] == nil then
			data[k] = v
		end
	end
	if type(data.autorun) ~= "table" then
		data.autorun = {}
	end
	settingsCache = data
	return data
end

function Custom.saveSettings()
	local s = Custom.settings()
	pcall(function()
		storage:write("settings.json", JSON.encode(s))
	end)
end

function Custom.setSetting(key, value)
	local s = Custom.settings()
	s[key] = value
	Custom.saveSettings()
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------ storage
---@return string[]
function Custom.list()
	local out = {}
	pcall(function()
		for _, filename in ipairs(storage:list()) do
			if type(filename) == "string" and filename:sub(-4) == ".lua" then
				out[#out + 1] = filename:sub(1, #filename - 4)
			end
		end
	end)
	table.sort(out)
	return out
end

---@param name string
---@return string?
function Custom.load(name)
	local source
	pcall(function()
		if storage:file(name .. ".lua") then
			source = storage:read(name .. ".lua")
		end
	end)
	return source
end

---@param name string
---@param source string
---@return boolean ok, string? err
function Custom.save(name, source)
	if type(name) ~= "string" or name:gsub("%s", "") == "" then
		return false, "invalid name"
	end
	-- Keep filenames flat/safe.
	name = name:gsub("[\\/:*?\"<>|]", "_")
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		storage:write(name .. ".lua", source or "")
	end)
	if not ok then
		return false, tostring(err)
	end
	return true
end

---@param name string
function Custom.delete(name)
	Custom.stop(name)
	pcall(function()
		if storage:file(name .. ".lua") then
			storage:delete(name .. ".lua")
		end
	end)
	local s = Custom.settings()
	if s.autorun[name] then
		s.autorun[name] = nil
		Custom.saveSettings()
	end
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------ execution
function Custom.setHost(panel)
	host = panel
end

function Custom.isRunning(name)
	return running[name] ~= nil
end

function Custom.runningNames()
	local out = {}
	for name in pairs(running) do
		out[#out + 1] = name
	end
	table.sort(out)
	return out
end

function Custom.stop(name)
	local inst = running[name]
	if inst then
		pcall(inst.destroy)
		running[name] = nil
	end
end

function Custom.stopAll()
	for name in pairs(running) do
		Custom.stop(name)
	end
end

---Run (or re-run) a feature's source. Cleans any previous instance of the same name first.
---@param name string
---@param source string
---@return boolean ok, string? err
function Custom.run(name, source)
	if not host then
		return false, "custom panel not ready"
	end

	Custom.stop(name)

	local chunk, compileErr = loadstring(source)
	if not chunk then
		local clean = tostring(compileErr):gsub('^%[string "[^"]*"%]:', "line ")
		return false, "syntax: " .. clean
	end

	local inst = CustomAPI.new(host, name)
	pcall(setfenv, chunk, inst.env)

	local ranOk, runErr = pcall(chunk)
	if not ranOk then
		pcall(inst.destroy)
		local clean = tostring(runErr):gsub('^%[string "[^"]*"%]:', "line ")
		return false, "runtime: " .. clean
	end

	running[name] = inst
	return true
end

function Custom.setAutorun(name, on)
	local s = Custom.settings()
	s.autorun[name] = on and true or nil
	Custom.saveSettings()
end

function Custom.isAutorun(name)
	return Custom.settings().autorun[name] == true
end

-- Run every feature flagged autorun (called by CustomTab once the host panel exists).
function Custom.autorunAll()
	local s = Custom.settings()
	for name in pairs(s.autorun) do
		local source = Custom.load(name)
		if source then
			local ok, err = Custom.run(name, source)
			if not ok then
				Logger.notify("[Custom] autorun '%s' failed: %s", tostring(name), tostring(err))
			end
		end
	end
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------ AI code generation
local function httpRequest()
	return (syn and syn.request)
		or (http and http.request)
		or http_request
		or request
		or (getgenv and getgenv().request)
end

local API_REFERENCE = table.concat({
	"You write Luau code for the Roblox game Deepwoken, run inside the 'luma' exploit menu's custom-feature sandbox.",
	"The following API is available as GLOBAL functions (also under the `Luma` table). Use ONLY this API for UI + lifecycle:",
	"",
	"UI (each returns a control object):",
	"  Toggle(text, function(value) end)  -- or Toggle(text, { default=bool, callback=fn }); control.get()/control.set(v)",
	"  Button(text, function() end)",
	"  Slider(text, { min=number, max=number, default=number, decimals=number, callback=function(value) end }); control.get()/set(v)",
	"  Dropdown(text, { values={...}, default=any, callback=function(value) end }); control.get()/set(v)/setValues(t)",
	"  Input(text, { default=string, callback=function(text) end }); control.get()/set(v)",
	"  Keybind(text, { default='E', callback=function(key) end })  -- fires callback when the bound key is pressed",
	"  Label(text); control.set(text)",
	"  Section(title) -> a sub-surface with the same UI methods",
	"  Window(title) -> a floating draggable window, a sub-surface with the same UI methods",
	"",
	"Lifecycle (auto-cleaned on reload):",
	"  OnRender(function(dt) end)   -- every RenderStepped frame",
	"  OnStep(function(dt) end)     -- every Heartbeat",
	"  OnInput(function(input, gameProcessed) end)",
	"  Interval(seconds, function() end)",
	"  OnCleanup(function() end)",
	"  Notify(text, seconds)",
	"",
	"Game helpers:",
	"  LocalPlayer, Character(), HRP(), Humanoid(), Mouse(), Camera()",
	"  GetTarget() -> { character, humanoid, root, du } (nearest aim target) ; GetTargets() -> list",
	"  Value(flag) -> read an existing luma toggle/option by flag; Toggles, Options, Services, Library",
	"  Standard Roblox globals (game, workspace, task, math, Vector3, CFrame, etc.) are available.",
	"",
	"Rules: output ONLY runnable Luau code, no markdown fences, no explanation. Do not call loadstring/HttpGet/setfenv.",
	"Guard every frame loop with a toggle. Keep it concise and correct.",
}, "\n")

local function extractCode(text)
	if type(text) ~= "string" then
		return nil
	end
	-- Prefer a fenced code block if the model added one.
	local fenced = text:match("```[%w]*\n(.-)```")
	if fenced and fenced:gsub("%s", "") ~= "" then
		return fenced
	end
	return text
end

---Generate feature code from a natural-language prompt. Runs async; cb(ok, codeOrErr).
---@param prompt string
---@param cb fun(ok: boolean, result: string)
function Custom.generate(prompt, cb)
	task.spawn(function()
		local s = Custom.settings()
		local req = httpRequest()
		if not req then
			return cb(false, "no HTTP request function on this executor")
		end
		if not s.apiKey or s.apiKey:gsub("%s", "") == "" then
			return cb(false, "set your API key first")
		end

		local provider = s.provider or "OpenAI"
		local model = (s.model and s.model:gsub("%s", "") ~= "" and s.model) or Custom.DEFAULT_MODELS[provider] or "gpt-4o-mini"

		local url, headers, body
		if provider == "Anthropic" then
			url = "https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages"
			headers = {
				["x-api-key"] = s.apiKey,
				["anthropic-version"] = "2023-06-01",
				["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
			}
			body = JSON.encode({
				model = model,
				max_tokens = 2048,
				system = API_REFERENCE,
				messages = { { role = "user", content = prompt } },
			})
		elseif provider == "Google" then
			url = "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/"
				.. model
				.. ":generateContent?key="
				.. s.apiKey
			headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json" }
			body = JSON.encode({
				system_instruction = { parts = { { text = API_REFERENCE } } },
				contents = { { role = "user", parts = { { text = prompt } } } },
			})
		else
			url = "https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions"
			headers = {
				["Authorization"] = "Bearer " .. s.apiKey,
				["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
			}
			body = JSON.encode({
				model = model,
				temperature = 0.4,
				messages = {
					{ role = "system", content = API_REFERENCE },
					{ role = "user", content = prompt },
				},
			})
		end

		local ok, res = pcall(req, {
			Url = url,
			Method = "POST",
			Headers = headers,
			Body = body,
		})
		if not ok or type(res) ~= "table" then
			return cb(false, "request failed: " .. tostring(res))
		end
		local status = res.StatusCode or res.Status or 0
		if type(res.Body) ~= "string" or res.Body == "" then
			return cb(false, "empty response (status " .. tostring(status) .. ")")
		end

		local decodeOk, decoded = pcall(JSON.decode, res.Body)
		if not decodeOk or type(decoded) ~= "table" then
			return cb(false, "bad JSON (status " .. tostring(status) .. ")")
		end

		-- API-level error?
		if decoded.error then
			local msg = type(decoded.error) == "table" and decoded.error.message or tostring(decoded.error)
			return cb(false, "api error: " .. tostring(msg))
		end

		local content
		if provider == "Anthropic" then
			content = decoded.content and decoded.content[1] and decoded.content[1].text
		elseif provider == "Google" then
			content = decoded.candidates
				and decoded.candidates[1]
				and decoded.candidates[1].content
				and decoded.candidates[1].content.parts
				and decoded.candidates[1].content.parts[1]
				and decoded.candidates[1].content.parts[1].text
		else
			content = decoded.choices and decoded.choices[1] and decoded.choices[1].message and decoded.choices[1].message.content
		end

		if type(content) ~= "string" or content:gsub("%s", "") == "" then
			return cb(false, "no code in response (status " .. tostring(status) .. ")")
		end

		return cb(true, extractCode(content))
	end)
end

return Custom
end)
__bundle_register("Features/Custom/CustomAPI", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
-- Builds the sandboxed API a user's custom-feature code runs against. new(hostBody, name) returns
-- { env, destroy }: `env` is the environment to setfenv the user chunk with (API helpers are available
-- as globals AND under the `Luma`/`api` table); `destroy()` tears down every widget, window, connection
-- and loop the feature created. Everything the feature makes is tracked so a reload leaves nothing behind.

---@module GUI.Library
local Library = require("GUI/Library")

---@module Features.Custom.CustomWidgets
local Widgets = require("Features/Custom/CustomWidgets")

---@module Utility.Configuration
local Configuration = require("Utility/Configuration")

---@module Features.Combat.Targeting
local Targeting = require("Features/Combat/Targeting")

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local CustomAPI = {}

local function normalizeOpts(arg)
	if type(arg) == "function" then
		return { callback = arg }
	end
	if type(arg) == "table" then
		return arg
	end
	return {}
end

---@param hostBody Instance where this feature's widgets are parented
---@param name string
---@return table
function CustomAPI.new(hostBody, name)
	local conns = {}
	local roots = {}
	local cleanupFns = {}
	local intervals = {}
	local destroyed = false

	local function track(control)
		if control and control.conns then
			for _, c in ipairs(control.conns) do
				conns[#conns + 1] = c
			end
		end
		return control
	end

	local function trackConn(c)
		conns[#conns + 1] = c
		return c
	end

	-- Section for this feature inside the host panel; all its root widgets live here.
	local rootSection = Widgets.section(hostBody, name or "Custom Feature")
	roots[#roots + 1] = rootSection.instance

	-- Builds a widget surface bound to a particular parent body (root section, a sub-section, or a window).
	local function buildSurface(parent)
		local surface = {}

		function surface.Label(text)
			return track(Widgets.label(parent, text))
		end

		function surface.Button(text, callback)
			return track(Widgets.button(parent, text, callback))
		end

		function surface.Toggle(text, arg)
			local o = normalizeOpts(arg)
			return track(Widgets.toggle(parent, text, o.default, o.callback))
		end

		function surface.Slider(text, arg)
			local o = normalizeOpts(arg)
			return track(Widgets.slider(parent, text, o.min, o.max, o.default, o.callback, o.decimals))
		end

		function surface.Dropdown(text, arg)
			local o = normalizeOpts(arg)
			return track(Widgets.dropdown(parent, text, o.values, o.default, o.callback))
		end

		function surface.Input(text, arg)
			local o = normalizeOpts(arg)
			return track(Widgets.input(parent, text, o.default, o.callback))
		end

		function surface.Keybind(text, arg)
			local o = normalizeOpts(arg)
			return track(Widgets.keybind(parent, text, o.default, o.callback))
		end

		function surface.Section(title)
			local sec = track(Widgets.section(parent, title))
			return buildSurface(sec.body)
		end

		return surface
	end

	local root = buildSurface(rootSection.body)

	-- Floating, draggable window parented to the menu ScreenGui. Returns a widget surface for its body.
	local function makeWindow(title)
		local gui = Library.ScreenGui
		local outer = Library:Create("Frame", {
			Name = "CustomWindow",
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
			Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(240, 60),
			AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
			BackgroundColor3 = Library.MainColor,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			ZIndex = 70,
			Parent = gui,
		})
		Library:Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6), Parent = outer })
		local os = Library:Create("UIStroke", { Color = Library.AccentColor, Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.2, Parent = outer })
		Library:AddToRegistry(outer, { BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor" })
		Library:AddToRegistry(os, { Color = "AccentColor" })
		pcall(function() Library:AddUIScale(outer) end)

		local bar = Library:Create("TextLabel", {
			BackgroundColor3 = Library.BackgroundColor,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24),
			Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
			Text = "  " .. tostring(title or "Window"),
			TextSize = 13,
			TextColor3 = Library.FontColor,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
			ZIndex = 71,
			Parent = outer,
		})
		Library:Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6), Parent = bar })
		Library:AddToRegistry(bar, { BackgroundColor3 = "BackgroundColor", TextColor3 = "FontColor" })
		pcall(function() Library:MakeDraggable(outer, 24) end)

		local body = Library:Create("Frame", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 28),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 0),
			AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
			ZIndex = 71,
			Parent = outer,
		})
		Library:Create("UIListLayout", { SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding = UDim.new(0, 4), Parent = body })
		Library:Create("UIPadding", { PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6), Parent = body })

		roots[#roots + 1] = outer
		return buildSurface(body)
	end

	-- ------------------------------------------------------------ lifecycle + helpers
	local api = {}

	-- widget methods (root surface)
	for k, v in pairs(root) do
		api[k] = v
	end

	function api.Window(title)
		return makeWindow(title)
	end

	function api.OnRender(fn)
		trackConn(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
			if not destroyed then
				local ok, err = pcall(fn, dt)
				if not ok then
					Logger.notify("[Custom] render error: %s", tostring(err))
				end
			end
		end))
	end

	function api.OnStep(fn)
		trackConn(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
			if not destroyed then
				pcall(fn, dt)
			end
		end))
	end

	function api.OnInput(fn)
		trackConn(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
			if not destroyed then
				pcall(fn, input, gameProcessed)
			end
		end))
	end

	function api.Interval(seconds, fn)
		seconds = tonumber(seconds) or 1
		local token = { running = true }
		intervals[#intervals + 1] = token
		task.spawn(function()
			while token.running and not destroyed do
				task.wait(seconds)
				if token.running and not destroyed then
					pcall(fn)
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	function api.OnCleanup(fn)
		cleanupFns[#cleanupFns + 1] = fn
	end

	function api.Notify(text, time)
		pcall(function()
			Library:Notify(tostring(text), time)
		end)
	end

	-- game helpers
	api.LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
	api.Services = {
		Players = Players,
		RunService = RunService,
		UserInputService = UserInputService,
		Workspace = Workspace,
		ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"),
		TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService"),
		VirtualInputManager = nil,
	}

	function api.Character()
		local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
		return lp and lp.Character
	end

	function api.HRP()
		local char = api.Character()
		return char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	end

	function api.Humanoid()
		local char = api.Character()
		return char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	end

	function api.Mouse()
		local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
		return lp and lp:GetMouse()
	end

	function api.Camera()
		return Workspace.CurrentCamera
	end

	-- Current best aim target (Targeting respects the user's targeting config), and the full list.
	function api.GetTarget()
		local ok, list = pcall(Targeting.best)
		if ok and list then
			return list[1]
		end
		return nil
	end

	function api.GetTargets()
		local ok, list = pcall(Targeting.best)
		if ok and list then
			return list
		end
		return {}
	end

	-- Read any existing luma control value by flag.
	function api.Value(flag)
		local v = Configuration.expectToggleValue(flag)
		if v ~= nil then
			return v
		end
		return Configuration.expectOptionValue(flag)
	end

	api.Toggles = getgenv().Toggles
	api.Options = getgenv().Options
	api.Library = Library

	-- ------------------------------------------------------------ sandbox env
	local base = getfenv(1)
	local gg = getgenv and getgenv() or nil
	local backing = {}
	local env = setmetatable(backing, {
		__index = function(_, key)
			local a = api[key]
			if a ~= nil then
				return a
			end
			local b = base[key]
			if b ~= nil then
				return b
			end
			if gg then
				return gg[key]
			end
			return nil
		end,
		__newindex = function(t, key, value)
			rawset(t, key, value)
		end,
	})
	-- Expose the whole surface as a table too, for users who prefer `Luma.Toggle(...)`.
	backing.Luma = api
	backing.api = api

	local function destroy()
		if destroyed then
			return
		end
		destroyed = true
		for _, token in ipairs(intervals) do
			token.running = false
		end
		for _, c in ipairs(conns) do
			pcall(function()
				c:Disconnect()
			end)
		end
		for _, fn in ipairs(cleanupFns) do
			pcall(fn)
		end
		for _, inst in ipairs(roots) do
			pcall(function()
				inst:Destroy()
			end)
		end
	end

	return { env = env, destroy = destroy, api = api }
end

return CustomAPI
end)
__bundle_register("Features/Custom/CustomWidgets", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
-- Self-managed UI widgets for user-built custom features. These are NOT YSL controls -- they're plain
-- styled frames we fully own, so a feature's whole UI can be torn down cleanly on reload (destroy the
-- frames + disconnect the returned connections). Each builder returns { instance, conns, ... }.

---@module GUI.Library
local Library = require("GUI/Library")

local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

local CustomWidgets = {}

local ROW_H = 26

local function corner(inst, radius)
	Library:Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius or 4), Parent = inst })
end

local function stroke(inst, colorKey, transparency)
	local s = Library:Create("UIStroke", {
		Color = Library[colorKey] or Library.OutlineColor,
		Thickness = 1,
		Transparency = transparency or 0.4,
		Parent = inst,
	})
	Library:AddToRegistry(s, { Color = colorKey or "OutlineColor" })
	return s
end

---@param parent Instance
---@param height number
---@return Frame
local function row(parent, height)
	local f = Library:Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.BackgroundColor,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.3,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height or ROW_H),
		Parent = parent,
	})
	corner(f, 4)
	Library:AddToRegistry(f, { BackgroundColor3 = "BackgroundColor" })
	return f
end

local function label(parent, text, size, xAlign, width)
	return Library:Create("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
		Text = text or "",
		TextSize = size or 13,
		TextColor3 = Library.FontColor,
		TextXAlignment = xAlign or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
		Size = width or UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, 0),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 0),
		Parent = parent,
	})
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- Label ----
function CustomWidgets.label(parent, text)
	local f = Library:Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		Parent = parent,
	})
	local l = Library:Create("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
		Text = tostring(text or ""),
		TextSize = 13,
		TextColor3 = Library.FontColor,
		TextWrapped = true,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 0),
		Parent = f,
	})
	Library:AddToRegistry(l, { TextColor3 = "FontColor" }, true)
	return {
		instance = f,
		conns = {},
