		set = function(t)
			l.Text = tostring(t or "")
		end,
	}
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- Button ----
function CustomWidgets.button(parent, text, callback)
	local f = Library:Create("TextButton", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.MainColor,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
		Text = tostring(text or "Button"),
		TextSize = 13,
		TextColor3 = Library.FontColor,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, ROW_H),
		Parent = parent,
	})
	corner(f, 4)
	stroke(f, "OutlineColor", 0.4)
	Library:AddToRegistry(f, { BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor", TextColor3 = "FontColor" }, true)

	f.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		f.TextColor3 = Library.AccentColor
	end)
	f.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		f.TextColor3 = Library.FontColor
	end)
	f.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if callback then
			pcall(callback)
		end
	end)

	return { instance = f, conns = {} }
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- Toggle ----
function CustomWidgets.toggle(parent, text, default, callback)
	local value = default and true or false
	local f = row(parent, ROW_H)

	label(f, tostring(text or "Toggle"), 13, Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, UDim2.new(1, -36, 1, 0))

	local boxOuter = Library:Create("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16),
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.MainColor,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = f,
	})
	corner(boxOuter, 3)
	stroke(boxOuter, "OutlineColor", 0.2)
	Library:AddToRegistry(boxOuter, { BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor" })

	local fill = Library:Create("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10),
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.AccentColor,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Visible = value,
		Parent = boxOuter,
	})
	corner(fill, 2)
	Library:AddToRegistry(fill, { BackgroundColor3 = "AccentColor" })

	local button = Library:Create("TextButton", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = "",
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		Parent = f,
	})

	local control
	local function apply(fireCallback)
		fill.Visible = value
		if fireCallback and callback then
			pcall(callback, value)
		end
	end

	button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		value = not value
		apply(true)
	end)

	control = {
		instance = f,
		conns = {},
		get = function()
			return value
		end,
		set = function(v, fire)
			value = v and true or false
			apply(fire ~= false)
		end,
	}
	return control
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- Slider ----
function CustomWidgets.slider(parent, text, min, max, default, callback, decimals)
	min = tonumber(min) or 0
	max = tonumber(max) or 100
	if max <= min then
		max = min + 1
	end
	decimals = tonumber(decimals) or 0
	local value = math.clamp(tonumber(default) or min, min, max)

	local f = Library:Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.BackgroundColor,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.3,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34),
		Parent = parent,
	})
	corner(f, 4)
	Library:AddToRegistry(f, { BackgroundColor3 = "BackgroundColor" })

	local nameLabel = label(f, tostring(text or "Slider"), 12, Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, 14))
	nameLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 2)

	local valueLabel = Library:Create("TextLabel", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 2),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(54, 14),
		Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
		TextSize = 12,
		TextColor3 = Library.AccentColor,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
		Text = "",
		Parent = f,
	})
	Library:AddToRegistry(valueLabel, { TextColor3 = "AccentColor" }, true)

	local track = Library:Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.MainColor,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 22),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 6),
		Parent = f,
	})
	corner(track, 3)
	Library:AddToRegistry(track, { BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor" })

	local fill = Library:Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.AccentColor,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1),
		Parent = track,
	})
	corner(fill, 3)
	Library:AddToRegistry(fill, { BackgroundColor3 = "AccentColor" })

	local function round(v)
		local m = 10 ^ decimals
		return math.floor(v * m + 0.5) / m
	end

	local control
	local function apply(fireCallback)
		local ratio = (value - min) / (max - min)
		fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(math.clamp(ratio, 0, 1), 1)
		valueLabel.Text = tostring(round(value))
		if fireCallback and callback then
			pcall(callback, value)
		end
	end

	local dragging = false
	local function setFromX(x)
		local rel = math.clamp((x - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X, 1), 0, 1)
		value = round(min + rel * (max - min))
		apply(true)
	end

	track.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			dragging = true
			setFromX(input.Position.X)
		end
	end)
	local c1 = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			dragging = false
		end
	end)
	local c2 = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if dragging and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
			setFromX(input.Position.X)
		end
	end)

	apply(false)

	control = {
		instance = f,
		conns = { c1, c2 },
		get = function()
			return value
		end,
		set = function(v, fire)
			value = math.clamp(round(tonumber(v) or min), min, max)
			apply(fire ~= false)
		end,
	}
	return control
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- Input ----
function CustomWidgets.input(parent, text, default, callback)
	local f = row(parent, ROW_H)
	local hasLabel = text ~= nil and tostring(text) ~= ""
	if hasLabel then
		label(f, tostring(text), 13, Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, UDim2.new(0.4, -8, 1, 0))
	end

	local boxHolder = Library:Create("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0.5, 0),
		Size = hasLabel and UDim2.new(0.6, -10, 1, -8) or UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -8),
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.MainColor,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Parent = f,
	})
	corner(boxHolder, 3)
	stroke(boxHolder, "OutlineColor", 0.3)
	Library:AddToRegistry(boxHolder, { BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor" })

	local tb = Library:Create("TextBox", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, 0),
		Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
		TextSize = 12,
		TextColor3 = Library.FontColor,
		PlaceholderText = "...",
		PlaceholderColor3 = Library.OutlineColor,
		Text = tostring(default or ""),
		ClearTextOnFocus = false,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		Parent = boxHolder,
	})
	Library:AddToRegistry(tb, { TextColor3 = "FontColor" }, true)

	tb.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		if callback then
			pcall(callback, tb.Text)
		end
	end)

	return {
		instance = f,
		conns = {},
		get = function()
			return tb.Text
		end,
		set = function(t)
			tb.Text = tostring(t or "")
		end,
	}
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- Keybind ----
function CustomWidgets.keybind(parent, text, default, callback)
	local key = default
	local listening = false
	local f = row(parent, ROW_H)

	label(f, tostring(text or "Keybind"), 13, Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, UDim2.new(1, -74, 1, 0))

	local keyBtn = Library:Create("TextButton", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(60, 18),
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.MainColor,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
		TextSize = 11,
		TextColor3 = Library.AccentColor,
		Text = key and tostring(key) or "None",
		Parent = f,
	})
	corner(keyBtn, 3)
	stroke(keyBtn, "OutlineColor", 0.3)
	Library:AddToRegistry(keyBtn, { BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor", TextColor3 = "AccentColor" })

	keyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		listening = true
		keyBtn.Text = "..."
	end)

	local conn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
		if listening and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
			listening = false
			if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Backspace or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
				key = nil
				keyBtn.Text = "None"
			else
				key = input.KeyCode.Name
				keyBtn.Text = key
			end
			return
		end
		if gameProcessed then
			return
		end
		if key and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and input.KeyCode.Name == key then
			if callback then
				pcall(callback, key)
			end
		end
	end)

	return {
		instance = f,
		conns = { conn },
		get = function()
			return key
		end,
		set = function(k)
			key = k
			keyBtn.Text = k and tostring(k) or "None"
		end,
	}
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- Dropdown ----
-- Collapsed row shows the selected value; clicking expands an inline option list below it.
function CustomWidgets.dropdown(parent, text, values, default, callback)
	values = values or {}
	local selected = default
	if selected == nil and values[1] ~= nil then
		selected = values[1]
	end

	local f = Library:Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, ROW_H),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		Parent = parent,
	})

	local header = row(f, ROW_H)
	label(header, tostring(text or "Dropdown"), 13, Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, UDim2.new(0.5, -8, 1, 0))

	local valueLabel = Library:Create("TextLabel", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -22, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0),
		Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
		TextSize = 12,
		TextColor3 = Library.AccentColor,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
		Text = selected ~= nil and tostring(selected) or "...",
		Parent = header,
	})
	Library:AddToRegistry(valueLabel, { TextColor3 = "AccentColor" }, true)

	local arrow = Library:Create("TextLabel", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12),
		Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
		TextSize = 12,
		TextColor3 = Library.FontColor,
		Text = "+",
		Parent = header,
	})

	local listHolder = Library:Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.MainColor,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, ROW_H + 2),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		Visible = false,
		Parent = f,
	})
	corner(listHolder, 4)
	stroke(listHolder, "OutlineColor", 0.3)
	Library:AddToRegistry(listHolder, { BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor" })
	Library:Create("UIListLayout", { SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = listHolder })
	Library:Create("UIPadding", {
		PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 2),
		PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 2),
		Parent = listHolder,
	})

	local open = false
	local function setOpen(v)
		open = v
		listHolder.Visible = v
		arrow.Text = v and "-" or "+"
	end

	local headerBtn = Library:Create("TextButton", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = "",
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		Parent = header,
	})
	headerBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		setOpen(not open)
	end)

	local control
	local function rebuild()
		for _, c in ipairs(listHolder:GetChildren()) do
			if c:IsA("TextButton") then
				c:Destroy()
			end
		end
		for i, v in ipairs(values) do
			local opt = Library:Create("TextButton", {
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				AutoButtonColor = false,
				Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
				Text = tostring(v),
				TextSize = 12,
				TextColor3 = Library.FontColor,
				TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
				Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 20),
				Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 0),
				LayoutOrder = i,
				Parent = listHolder,
			})
			opt.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
				opt.TextColor3 = Library.AccentColor
			end)
			opt.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
				opt.TextColor3 = Library.FontColor
			end)
			opt.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				selected = v
				valueLabel.Text = tostring(v)
				setOpen(false)
				if callback then
					pcall(callback, v)
				end
			end)
		end
	end
	rebuild()

	control = {
		instance = f,
		conns = {},
		get = function()
			return selected
		end,
		set = function(v, fire)
			selected = v
			valueLabel.Text = v ~= nil and tostring(v) or "..."
			if fire ~= false and callback then
				pcall(callback, v)
			end
		end,
		setValues = function(newValues)
			values = newValues or {}
			rebuild()
		end,
	}
	return control
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- Section ----
-- A labelled container with its own vertical body for nested widgets.
function CustomWidgets.section(parent, title)
	local f = Library:Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.MainColor,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.2,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		Parent = parent,
	})
	corner(f, 4)
	stroke(f, "OutlineColor", 0.5)
	Library:AddToRegistry(f, { BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor" })

	Library:Create("UIPadding", {
		PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6),
		PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6),
		PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6),
		PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6),
		Parent = f,
	})
	Library:Create("UIListLayout", {
		SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
		Padding = UDim.new(0, 4),
		Parent = f,
	})

	local head = Library:Create("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
		Text = tostring(title or "Section"),
		TextSize = 13,
		TextColor3 = Library.AccentColor,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16),
		LayoutOrder = 0,
		Parent = f,
	})
	Library:AddToRegistry(head, { TextColor3 = "AccentColor" }, true)

	local body = Library:Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		LayoutOrder = 1,
		Parent = f,
	})
	Library:Create("UIListLayout", {
		SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
		Padding = UDim.new(0, 4),
		Parent = body,
	})

	return { instance = f, body = body, conns = {} }
end

return CustomWidgets
end)
__bundle_register("Menu/CustomTab", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
---@module GUI.Icons
local Icons = require("GUI/Icons")

---@module GUI.Library
local Library = require("GUI/Library")

---@module GUI.SaveManager
local SaveManager = require("GUI/SaveManager")

---@module Menu.Objects.CodeEditor
local CodeEditor = require("Menu/Objects/CodeEditor")

---@module Menu.Objects.FloatingWindow
local FloatingWindow = require("Menu/Objects/FloatingWindow")

---@module Features.Custom.Custom
local Custom = require("Features/Custom/Custom")

---@module Features.Custom.AIChat
local AIChat = require("Features/Custom/AIChat")

local CustomTab = {}

local editor -- the code editor control { get, set, setEditable }
local editorWindow
local chatWindow
local statusLabel

local function setStatus(fmt, ...)
	local text = select("#", ...) > 0 and string.format(fmt, ...) or fmt
	pcall(function()
		statusLabel:SetText(text)
	end)
end

local function currentName()
	local name = Options.Custom_FeatureName and Options.Custom_FeatureName.Value or ""
	if type(name) ~= "string" or name:gsub("%s", "") == "" then
		return nil
	end
	return name
end

local function refreshList()
	if Options.Custom_FeatureList then
		Options.Custom_FeatureList:SetValues(Custom.list())
	end
end

-- ============================================================ Editor window (pure code editor)
local function buildEditorWindow()
	editorWindow = FloatingWindow.new({
		title = "Luma - Code Editor",
		size = UDim2.fromOffset(540, 560),
		position = UDim2.fromScale(0.35, 0.5),
	})
	editor = CodeEditor.create(editorWindow.body, 514, "-- write your feature here, or generate one from AI Chat", 205)
	editor.set(Custom.TEMPLATE)
end

-- ============================================================ Chat window (agentic AI)
local function buildChatWindow()
	chatWindow = FloatingWindow.new({
		title = "Luma - AI Chat",
		size = UDim2.fromOffset(430, 520),
		position = UDim2.fromScale(0.65, 0.5),
	})
	local body = chatWindow.body

	local transcript = Library:Create("ScrollingFrame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.BackgroundColor,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.4,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -64),
		CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
		AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		ScrollBarThickness = 4,
		ZIndex = 202,
		Parent = body,
	})
	Library:Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4), Parent = transcript })
	Library:AddToRegistry(transcript, { BackgroundColor3 = "BackgroundColor" })
	Library:Create("UIListLayout", { SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding = UDim.new(0, 4), Parent = transcript })
	Library:Create("UIPadding", {
		PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4),
		PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4),
		PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6),
		PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6),
		Parent = transcript,
	})

	local order = 0
	local function addMsg(role, text)
		order = order + 1
		local color = Library.FontColor
		local prefix = ""
		if role == "user" then
			color = Library.AccentColor
			prefix = "You: "
		elseif role == "assistant" then
			color = Library.FontColor
			prefix = "AI: "
		elseif role == "tool" then
			color = Library.OutlineColor
			prefix = "[tool] "
		elseif role == "toolresult" then
			color = Library.OutlineColor
			prefix = "[result] "
			if #text > 500 then
				text = text:sub(1, 500) .. " ... (" .. #text .. " chars)"
			end
		elseif role == "error" then
			color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 96, 96)
			prefix = "[error] "
		end

		local lbl = Library:Create("TextLabel", {
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Font = (role == "tool" or role == "toolresult") and Enum.Font.Code or Enum.Font.Gotham,
			Text = prefix .. tostring(text),
			TextSize = (role == "tool" or role == "toolresult") and 11 or 12,
			TextColor3 = color,
			TextWrapped = true,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
			TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
			AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
			LayoutOrder = order,
			ZIndex = 203,
			Parent = transcript,
		})
		task.defer(function()
			transcript.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, transcript.AbsoluteCanvasSize.Y))
		end)
		return lbl
	end

	addMsg("assistant", "Ask me to build a feature or explain the game. I can inspect the live game and decompile scripts. Set your provider/key in the Custom tab first.")

	local statusText = Library:Create("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 1, -62),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 12),
		Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
		Text = "",
		TextSize = 11,
		TextColor3 = Library.AccentColor,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		ZIndex = 202,
		Parent = body,
	})
	Library:AddToRegistry(statusText, { TextColor3 = "AccentColor" })

	local inputHolder = Library:Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.MainColor,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -46),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -66, 0, 28),
		ZIndex = 202,
		Parent = body,
	})
	Library:Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4), Parent = inputHolder })
	local is = Library:Create("UIStroke", { Color = Library.OutlineColor, Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.3, Parent = inputHolder })
	Library:AddToRegistry(inputHolder, { BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor" })
	Library:AddToRegistry(is, { Color = "OutlineColor" })

	local inputBox = Library:Create("TextBox", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -14, 1, 0),
		Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
		TextSize = 12,
		TextColor3 = Library.FontColor,
		PlaceholderText = "Describe a feature, or ask about the game...",
		PlaceholderColor3 = Library.OutlineColor,
		Text = "",
		ClearTextOnFocus = false,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
		ZIndex = 203,
		Parent = inputHolder,
	})
	Library:AddToRegistry(inputBox, { TextColor3 = "FontColor" })

	local sendBtn = Library:Create("TextButton", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -46),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(60, 28),
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.AccentColor,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
		Text = "Send",
		TextSize = 12,
		TextColor3 = Library.MainColor,
		ZIndex = 203,
		Parent = body,
	})
	Library:Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4), Parent = sendBtn })
	Library:AddToRegistry(sendBtn, { BackgroundColor3 = "AccentColor", TextColor3 = "MainColor" })

	local clearBtn = Library:Create("TextButton", {
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -16),
		Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -2, 0, 16),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
		Text = "Clear chat",
		TextSize = 11,
		TextColor3 = Library.OutlineColor,
		ZIndex = 203,
		Parent = body,
	})
	Library:AddToRegistry(clearBtn, { TextColor3 = "OutlineColor" })

	local toEditorBtn = Library:Create("TextButton", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -16),
		Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -2, 0, 16),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
		Text = "Send code to editor ->",
		TextSize = 11,
		TextColor3 = Library.AccentColor,
		ZIndex = 203,
		Parent = body,
	})
	Library:AddToRegistry(toEditorBtn, { TextColor3 = "AccentColor" })

	local busy = false
	local currentAssistant = nil

	local function scrollBottom()
		task.defer(function()
			transcript.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, transcript.AbsoluteCanvasSize.Y))
		end)
	end

	local function onEvent(kind, payload)
		if kind == "status" then
			statusText.Text = tostring(payload or "")
		elseif kind == "done" then
			busy = false
			currentAssistant = nil
			sendBtn.Text = "Send"
			statusText.Text = ""
		elseif kind == "user" then
			addMsg("user", payload)
		elseif kind == "assistant_begin" then
			currentAssistant = addMsg("assistant", "")
		elseif kind == "assistant_delta" then
			if currentAssistant then
				currentAssistant.Text = currentAssistant.Text .. tostring(payload)
				scrollBottom()
			end
		elseif kind == "assistant_end" then
			currentAssistant = nil
		elseif kind == "assistant" then
			addMsg("assistant", payload)
		elseif kind == "tool" then
			addMsg("tool", payload)
		elseif kind == "toolresult" then
			addMsg("toolresult", payload)
		elseif kind == "error" then
			addMsg("error", payload)
		end
	end

	local function doSend()
		if busy then
			AIChat.cancel()
			statusText.Text = "stopping..."
			return
		end
		local text = inputBox.Text
		if type(text) ~= "string" or text:gsub("%s", "") == "" then
			return
		end
		inputBox.Text = ""
		busy = true
		sendBtn.Text = "Stop"
		AIChat.send(text, onEvent)
	end

	sendBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(doSend)
	inputBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
		if enterPressed and not busy then
			doSend()
		end
	end)

	clearBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		AIChat.reset()
		for _, c in ipairs(transcript:GetChildren()) do
			if c:IsA("TextLabel") then
				c:Destroy()
			end
		end
		order = 0
		addMsg("assistant", "Chat cleared.")
	end)

	toEditorBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if AIChat.lastCode and editor then
			editor.set(AIChat.lastCode)
			editorWindow:Show()
			setStatus("Pulled AI code into the editor.")
		end
	end)
end

-- ============================================================ tab
function CustomTab.init(window)
	local tab = window:AddTab("Custom", "Custom", Icons.Settings)

	SaveManager:SetIgnoreIndexes({
		"Custom_FeatureName",
		"Custom_FeatureList",
		"Custom_AIKey",
		"Custom_AIModel",
		"Custom_AIProvider",
		"Custom_Autorun",
	})

	buildEditorWindow()
	buildChatWindow()

	-- --------------------------------- LEFT: windows + feature management
	local manageBox = tab:AddLeftGroupbox("Custom Features")

	manageBox
		:AddButton("Open Code Editor", function()
			editorWindow:Show()
		end)
		:AddButton("Open AI Chat", function()
			chatWindow:Show()
		end)

	manageBox:AddInput("Custom_FeatureName", { Text = "Feature name", Default = "" })
	manageBox:AddDropdown("Custom_FeatureList", { Text = "Saved features", Values = Custom.list(), AllowNull = true })

	manageBox
		:AddButton("Run / Reload", function()
			if not editor then
				return setStatus("Editor not ready.")
			end
			if not currentName() then
				Options.Custom_FeatureName:SetValue("Untitled")
			end
			local name = currentName() or "Untitled"
			local source = editor.get()
			Custom.save(name, source)
			refreshList()
			local ok, err = Custom.run(name, source)
			if ok then
				setStatus("Running '%s'.", name)
			else
				setStatus("Error: %s", tostring(err))
			end
		end)
		:AddButton("Stop", function()
			local name = currentName()
			if not name then
				return setStatus("Nothing to stop.")
			end
			Custom.stop(name)
			setStatus("Stopped '%s'.", name)
		end)

	manageBox
		:AddButton("Save", function()
			local name = currentName()
			if not name then
				return setStatus("Enter a feature name first.")
			end
			local ok, err = Custom.save(name, editor and editor.get() or "")
			if ok then
				refreshList()
				setStatus("Saved '%s'.", name)
			else
				setStatus("Save failed: %s", tostring(err))
			end
		end)
		:AddButton("Load", function()
			local sel = Options.Custom_FeatureList.Value
			if type(sel) ~= "string" or sel == "" then
				return setStatus("Select a feature to load.")
			end
			local source = Custom.load(sel)
			if not source then
				return setStatus("Could not read '%s'.", sel)
			end
			if editor then
				editor.set(source)
			end
			Options.Custom_FeatureName:SetValue(sel)
			if Toggles.Custom_Autorun then
				Toggles.Custom_Autorun:SetValue(Custom.isAutorun(sel))
			end
			editorWindow:Show()
			setStatus("Loaded '%s'.", sel)
		end)

	manageBox
		:AddButton("New", function()
			if editor then
				editor.set(Custom.TEMPLATE)
			end
			Options.Custom_FeatureName:SetValue("")
			setStatus("New feature.")
		end)
		:AddButton("Delete", function()
			local sel = Options.Custom_FeatureList.Value
			if type(sel) ~= "string" or sel == "" then
				return setStatus("Select a feature to delete.")
			end
			Custom.delete(sel)
			refreshList()
			Options.Custom_FeatureList:SetValue(nil)
			setStatus("Deleted '%s'.", sel)
		end)

	local autorunToggle = manageBox:AddToggle("Custom_Autorun", {
		Text = "Autorun this feature on load",
		Default = false,
	})
	autorunToggle:OnChanged(function(value)
		local name = currentName()
		if name then
			Custom.setAutorun(name, value)
		end
	end)

	statusLabel = manageBox:AddLabel("Idle.", true)

	-- --------------------------------- LEFT: AI settings
	local aiBox = tab:AddLeftGroupbox("AI Settings")
	local s = Custom.settings()

	aiBox:AddDropdown("Custom_AIProvider", { Text = "Provider", Values = Custom.PROVIDERS, Default = s.provider })
	aiBox:AddInput("Custom_AIModel", { Text = "Model", Default = s.model })
	aiBox:AddInput("Custom_AIKey", { Text = "API key", Default = s.apiKey })

	local aiReady = false
	Options.Custom_AIProvider:OnChanged(function(value)
		Custom.setSetting("provider", value)
		if aiReady then
			local model = Custom.DEFAULT_MODELS[value]
			if model then
				Options.Custom_AIModel:SetValue(model)
			end
		end
	end)
	Options.Custom_AIModel:OnChanged(function(value)
		Custom.setSetting("model", value)
	end)
	Options.Custom_AIKey:OnChanged(function(value)
		Custom.setSetting("apiKey", value)
	end)
	aiReady = true

	aiBox:AddLabel("Key is stored locally (workspace/Luma/Custom), never in configs.", true)

	-- --------------------------------- RIGHT: live controls host
	local controlsBox = tab:AddRightGroupbox("Custom Controls")

	local holder = Library:Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.MainColor,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 360),
		LayoutOrder = 9999,
		ZIndex = 5,
		Parent = controlsBox.Container,
	})
	Library:Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4), Parent = holder })
	Library:AddToRegistry(holder, { BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor" })
	local hstroke = Library:Create("UIStroke", { Color = Library.OutlineColor, Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.5, Parent = holder })
	Library:AddToRegistry(hstroke, { Color = "OutlineColor" })

	local host = Library:Create("ScrollingFrame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -12),
		CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
		AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		ScrollBarThickness = 4,
		ZIndex = 6,
		Parent = holder,
	})
	Library:Create("UIListLayout", { SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding = UDim.new(0, 6), Parent = host })
	Library:Create("UIPadding", { PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 4), Parent = host })

	local emptyHint = Library:Create("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40),
		Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
		Text = "Run a feature to see its controls here.",
		TextSize = 12,
		TextColor3 = Library.OutlineColor,
		TextWrapped = true,
		Parent = host,
	})
	Library:AddToRegistry(emptyHint, { TextColor3 = "OutlineColor" }, true)

	Custom.setHost(host)

	pcall(function()
		controlsBox:Resize()
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		Custom.autorunAll()
	end)
end

return CustomTab
end)
__bundle_register("Features/Custom/AIChat", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
-- Conversational AI agent for the Custom tab. Keeps a message transcript, calls the configured provider
-- (OpenAI / Anthropic / Google) as a multi-turn chat, and runs a ReAct-style tool loop: the model asks
-- for a tool by replying with a JSON object, we run it against the live game (GameTools) and feed the
-- result back, repeating until the model gives a final answer. Provider-agnostic (plain chat, no native
-- function-calling), so it works the same across all three.

---@module Utility.JSON
local JSON = require("Utility/JSON")

---@module Features.Custom.GameTools
local GameTools = require("Features/Custom/GameTools")

---@module Features.Custom.Custom
local Custom = require("Features/Custom/Custom")

local AIChat = {}

local MAX_STEPS = 10

AIChat.busy = false
AIChat.lastCode = nil
AIChat.messages = nil
AIChat.current = nil -- active run token (for interrupt)

-- Stop the in-flight run. Takes effect between steps / mid-reveal (a blocking request() can't be aborted).
function AIChat.cancel()
	if AIChat.current then
		AIChat.current.cancelled = true
	end
end

local function httpRequest()
	return (syn and syn.request)
		or (http and http.request)
		or http_request
		or request
		or (getgenv and getgenv().request)
end

local function systemPrompt()
	return table.concat({
		"You are an expert assistant embedded in 'luma', a menu for the Roblox game Deepwoken. You help the",
		"user understand the game and build custom features. You can inspect the LIVE game and decompile",
		"scripts via tools before answering, so prefer checking real data over guessing.",
		"",
		GameTools.API_REFERENCE,
		"",
		GameTools.TOOLS_DOC,
		"",
		"Be concise. When the user asks for a feature, inspect what you need, then give the final code in a",
		"single ```lua block using only the API above.",
	}, "\n")
end

function AIChat.reset()
	AIChat.messages = { { role = "system", content = systemPrompt() } }
	AIChat.lastCode = nil
end

local function ensureInit()
	if not AIChat.messages then
		AIChat.reset()
	end
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------ provider chat completion (synchronous)
local function chatComplete(messages)
	local s = Custom.settings()
	local req = httpRequest()
	if not req then
		return false, "no HTTP request function on this executor"
	end
	if not s.apiKey or s.apiKey:gsub("%s", "") == "" then
		return false, "set your API key in the Custom tab first"
	end

	local provider = s.provider or "OpenAI"
	local model = (s.model and s.model:gsub("%s", "") ~= "" and s.model) or Custom.DEFAULT_MODELS[provider] or "gpt-4o-mini"

	local url, headers, body
	if provider == "Anthropic" then
		local system = {}
		local msgs = {}
		for _, m in ipairs(messages) do
			if m.role == "system" then
				system[#system + 1] = m.content
			else
				msgs[#msgs + 1] = { role = m.role, content = m.content }
			end
		end
		url = "https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages"
		headers = {
			["x-api-key"] = s.apiKey,
			["anthropic-version"] = "2023-06-01",
			["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
		}
		body = JSON.encode({
			model = model,
			max_tokens = 4096,
			system = table.concat(system, "\n"),
			messages = msgs,
		})
	elseif provider == "Google" then
		local system = {}
		local contents = {}
		for _, m in ipairs(messages) do
			if m.role == "system" then
				system[#system + 1] = m.content
			else
				local role = (m.role == "assistant") and "model" or "user"
				contents[#contents + 1] = { role = role, parts = { { text = m.content } } }
			end
		end
		url = "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/" .. model .. ":generateContent?key=" .. s.apiKey
		headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json" }
		body = JSON.encode({
			system_instruction = { parts = { { text = table.concat(system, "\n") } } },
			contents = contents,
		})
	else
		url = "https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions"
		headers = {
			["Authorization"] = "Bearer " .. s.apiKey,
			["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
		}
		body = JSON.encode({
			model = model,
			temperature = 0.3,
			messages = messages,
		})
	end

	local ok, res = pcall(req, { Url = url, Method = "POST", Headers = headers, Body = body })
	if not ok or type(res) ~= "table" then
		return false, "request failed: " .. tostring(res)
	end
	local status = res.StatusCode or res.Status or 0
	if type(res.Body) ~= "string" or res.Body == "" then
		return false, "empty response (status " .. tostring(status) .. ")"
	end
	local decodeOk, decoded = pcall(JSON.decode, res.Body)
	if not decodeOk or type(decoded) ~= "table" then
		return false, "bad JSON (status " .. tostring(status) .. ")"
	end
	if decoded.error then
		local msg = type(decoded.error) == "table" and decoded.error.message or tostring(decoded.error)
		return false, "api error: " .. tostring(msg)
	end

	local content
	if provider == "Anthropic" then
		if decoded.content then
			local parts = {}
			for _, p in ipairs(decoded.content) do
				if p.type == "text" and p.text then
					parts[#parts + 1] = p.text
				end
			end
			content = table.concat(parts)
		end
	elseif provider == "Google" then
		local cand = decoded.candidates and decoded.candidates[1]
		if cand and cand.content and cand.content.parts then
			local parts = {}
			for _, p in ipairs(cand.content.parts) do
				if p.text then
					parts[#parts + 1] = p.text
				end
			end
			content = table.concat(parts)
		end
	else
		content = decoded.choices and decoded.choices[1] and decoded.choices[1].message and decoded.choices[1].message.content
	end

	if type(content) ~= "string" or content:gsub("%s", "") == "" then
		return false, "no content in response (status " .. tostring(status) .. ")"
	end
	return true, content
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------ parsing
local function stripFence(text)
	local inner = text:match("^%s*```[%w]*%s*\n(.-)\n?%s*```%s*$")
	return inner or text
end

local function parseToolCall(text)
	local t = stripFence(text)
	t = t:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
	if t:sub(1, 1) ~= "{" then
		return nil
	end
	local ok, decoded = pcall(JSON.decode, t)
	if ok and type(decoded) == "table" and type(decoded.tool) == "string" then
		return { tool = decoded.tool, args = type(decoded.args) == "table" and decoded.args or {} }
	end
	return nil
end

local function extractCode(text)
	local c = text:match("```lua%s*\n(.-)```") or text:match("```%s*\n(.-)```")
	if c and c:gsub("%s", "") ~= "" then
		return c
	end
	return nil
end
AIChat.extractCode = extractCode

-- Reveal the final answer progressively (typewriter). request() returns the whole body at once, so this
-- is a cosmetic reveal, interruptible via the run token. Chunk size scales with length (~160 ticks total).
local function streamOut(text, onEvent, token)
	AIChat.lastCode = extractCode(text) or AIChat.lastCode
	onEvent("assistant_begin")
	local n = #text
	local chunkSize = math.max(2, math.floor(n / 160))
	local i = 1
	while i <= n do
		if token.cancelled then
			onEvent("assistant_delta", text:sub(i)) -- reveal the remainder instantly on stop
			break
		end
		onEvent("assistant_delta", text:sub(i, i + chunkSize - 1))
		i = i + chunkSize
		task.wait(0.012)
	end
	onEvent("assistant_end")
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------ agent loop
---@param userText string
---@param onEvent fun(kind: string, text: string?)
-- kinds: user, assistant_begin, assistant_delta, assistant_end, tool, toolresult, error, status, done
function AIChat.send(userText, onEvent)
	ensureInit()
	if AIChat.busy then
		onEvent("error", "still working on the previous message...")
		return
	end
	if type(userText) ~= "string" or userText:gsub("%s", "") == "" then
		return
	end

	local token = { cancelled = false }
	AIChat.current = token
	AIChat.busy = true
	table.insert(AIChat.messages, { role = "user", content = userText })
	onEvent("user", userText)

	task.spawn(function()
		local steps = 0
		local finished = false
		while steps < MAX_STEPS do
			if token.cancelled then
				onEvent("status", "stopped.")
				finished = true
				break
			end
			steps = steps + 1
			onEvent("status", steps > 1 and ("thinking... (step " .. steps .. ")") or "thinking...")

			local ok, text = chatComplete(AIChat.messages)
			if token.cancelled then
				onEvent("status", "stopped.")
				finished = true
				break
			end
			if not ok then
				onEvent("error", text)
				finished = true
				break
			end

			table.insert(AIChat.messages, { role = "assistant", content = text })

			local call = parseToolCall(text)
			if call then
				local argsStr = ""
				pcall(function()
					argsStr = JSON.encode(call.args)
				end)
				onEvent("tool", call.tool .. " " .. argsStr)
				local result = GameTools.run(call.tool, call.args)
				onEvent("toolresult", result)
				table.insert(AIChat.messages, {
					role = "user",
					content = "TOOL RESULT (" .. call.tool .. "):\n" .. result,
				})
			else
				streamOut(text, onEvent, token)
				finished = true
				break
			end
		end

		if not finished then
			onEvent("error", "reached the tool-step limit; ask again or be more specific")
		end

		AIChat.busy = false
		AIChat.current = nil
		onEvent("done")
	end)
end

return AIChat
end)
__bundle_register("Features/Custom/GameTools", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
-- Tools the AI agent can call to inspect the live game and decompile scripts, plus the full luma custom
-- feature API reference it builds code against. Everything is read-only and bounded so a tool call can't
-- hang or dump unbounded output. GameTools.run(name, args) -> string result.

---@module Features.Custom.Custom
local Custom = require("Features/Custom/Custom")

local GameTools = {}

local Players = game:GetService("Players")

-- ------------------------------------------------------------ path resolution
-- Resolve a dot path from the DataModel, e.g. "game.Workspace.Live", "Players.LocalPlayer",
-- "Players.LocalPlayer.Character". Handles services + the LocalPlayer/Character property shortcuts.
local function resolvePath(path)
	if type(path) ~= "string" or path:gsub("%s", "") == "" then
		return game
	end
	path = path:gsub("/", ".")
	local cur = game
	local first = true
	for token in path:gmatch("[^%.]+") do
		if first and (token == "game" or token == "Game" or token == "DataModel") then
			cur = game
		elseif token == "LocalPlayer" then
			cur = Players.LocalPlayer
			if not cur then
				return nil, "LocalPlayer is nil"
			end
		elseif token == "Character" and typeof(cur) == "Instance" and cur:IsA("Player") then
			cur = cur.Character
			if not cur then
				return nil, "Character is nil"
			end
		else
			local nextInst = nil
			if cur == game then
				pcall(function()
					nextInst = game:GetService(token)
				end)
			end
			if not nextInst then
				nextInst = cur:FindFirstChild(token)
			end
			if not nextInst then
				return nil, "not found: '" .. token .. "' under " .. cur:GetFullName()
			end
			cur = nextInst
		end
		first = false
	end
	return cur
end

GameTools.resolvePath = resolvePath

-- ------------------------------------------------------------ tools
local function tExplore(path, depth)
	local inst, err = resolvePath(path)
	if not inst then
		return "Error: " .. tostring(err)
	end
	depth = math.clamp(tonumber(depth) or 1, 1, 3)
	local lines = { inst:GetFullName() .. "  [" .. inst.ClassName .. "]" }
	local count = 0
	local function walk(node, d, indent)
		if count >= 200 then
			return
		end
		local ok, children = pcall(function()
			return node:GetChildren()
		end)
		if not ok then
			return
		end
		for _, child in ipairs(children) do
			count = count + 1
			if count >= 200 then
				lines[#lines + 1] = indent .. "... (truncated)"
				return
			end
			lines[#lines + 1] = indent .. child.Name .. "  [" .. child.ClassName .. "]"
			if d > 1 then
				walk(child, d - 1, indent .. "  ")
			end
		end
	end
	walk(inst, depth, "  ")
	if count == 0 then
		lines[#lines + 1] = "  (no children)"
	end
	return table.concat(lines, "\n")
end

local PROP_CANDIDATES = {
	"Name", "ClassName", "Enabled", "Value", "Visible", "Transparency", "Anchored", "CanCollide",
	"Position", "Size", "Orientation", "CFrame", "Health", "MaxHealth", "WalkSpeed", "JumpPower",
	"HipHeight", "DisplayName", "Text", "PlaceholderText", "Color", "BrickColor", "Material",
	"Massless", "CollisionGroup", "MeshId", "TextureId", "SoundId", "Volume", "Playing", "TimePosition",
	"AssetId", "AnimationId", "PrimaryPart", "Adornee", "Active",
}

local function tProperties(path)
	local inst, err = resolvePath(path)
	if not inst then
		return "Error: " .. tostring(err)
	end
	local out = { inst:GetFullName() .. "  [" .. inst.ClassName .. "]" }
	for _, p in ipairs(PROP_CANDIDATES) do
		local ok, v = pcall(function()
			return inst[p]
		end)
		if ok and v ~= nil then
			out[#out + 1] = p .. " = " .. tostring(v)
		end
	end
	pcall(function()
		if inst.Parent then
			out[#out + 1] = "Parent = " .. inst.Parent:GetFullName()
		end
	end)
	local okA, attrs = pcall(function()
		return inst:GetAttributes()
	end)
	if okA and type(attrs) == "table" then
		for k, v in pairs(attrs) do
			out[#out + 1] = "@" .. tostring(k) .. " = " .. tostring(v)
		end
	end
	return table.concat(out, "\n")
end

local function matchClass(className, filter)
	if filter == nil or filter == "" then
		return true
	end
	return className == filter
end

local function tSearch(query, class, rootPath)
	local root, err = resolvePath(rootPath or "game")
	if not root then
		return "Error: " .. tostring(err)
	end
	query = tostring(query or ""):lower()
	local results = {}
	local scanned = 0
	local ok, descendants = pcall(function()
		return root:GetDescendants()
	end)
	if not ok then
		return "Error: could not enumerate descendants."
	end
	for _, d in ipairs(descendants) do
		scanned = scanned + 1
		if scanned > 60000 then
			break
		end
		if matchClass(d.ClassName, class) and (query == "" or d.Name:lower():find(query, 1, true)) then
			results[#results + 1] = d:GetFullName() .. "  [" .. d.ClassName .. "]"
			if #results >= 60 then
				results[#results + 1] = "... (more results omitted)"
				break
			end
		end
	end
	if #results == 0 then
		return "No matches (scanned " .. scanned .. " descendants)."
	end
	return table.concat(results, "\n")
end

local SCRIPT_CLASSES = { LocalScript = true, ModuleScript = true, Script = true }

local function tScripts(rootPath)
	local root, err = resolvePath(rootPath or "game")
	if not root then
		return "Error: " .. tostring(err)
	end
	local results = {}
	local scanned = 0
	local ok, descendants = pcall(function()
		return root:GetDescendants()
	end)
	if not ok then
		return "Error: could not enumerate descendants."
	end
	for _, d in ipairs(descendants) do
		scanned = scanned + 1
		if scanned > 60000 then
			break
		end
		if SCRIPT_CLASSES[d.ClassName] then
			results[#results + 1] = d:GetFullName() .. "  [" .. d.ClassName .. "]"
			if #results >= 80 then
				results[#results + 1] = "... (more omitted)"
				break
			end
		end
	end
	if #results == 0 then
		return "No scripts found."
	end
	return table.concat(results, "\n")
end

local function tDecompile(path)
	local inst, err = resolvePath(path)
	if not inst then
		return "Error: " .. tostring(err)
	end
	if not (typeof(inst) == "Instance" and inst:IsA("LuaSourceContainer")) then
		return "Not a script: " .. inst.ClassName
	end
	local dec = decompile or (getgenv and getgenv().decompile)
	if not dec then
		return "decompile() is not available on this executor."
	end
	local ok, src = pcall(dec, inst)
	if not ok or type(src) ~= "string" then
		return "Decompile failed: " .. tostring(src)
	end
	if #src > 9000 then
		src = src:sub(1, 9000) .. "\n-- [truncated; ask to decompile a nested module for more]"
	end
	return src
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------ dispatch
---@param name string
---@param args table?
---@return string
function GameTools.run(name, args)
	args = args or {}
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		if name == "explore" then
			return tExplore(args.path, args.depth)
		elseif name == "properties" then
			return tProperties(args.path)
		elseif name == "search" then
			return tSearch(args.query, args.class, args.root)
		elseif name == "scripts" then
			return tScripts(args.root)
		elseif name == "decompile" then
			return tDecompile(args.path)
		elseif name == "api_reference" then
			return GameTools.API_REFERENCE
		elseif name == "run_feature" then
			local fname = (type(args.name) == "string" and args.name:gsub("%s", "") ~= "") and args.name or "AI Test"
			if type(args.code) ~= "string" or args.code:gsub("%s", "") == "" then
				return "Error: no 'code' provided."
			end
			Custom.save(fname, args.code)
			local okRun, err = Custom.run(fname, args.code)
			if okRun then
				return "Ran '" .. fname .. "' successfully; its controls are now in the Custom Controls panel. "
					.. "Toggle them to verify behaviour, then give the user the final code in a ```lua block."
			end
			return "Run FAILED: " .. tostring(err) .. "\nFix the code and call run_feature again."
		elseif name == "stop_feature" then
			local fname = type(args.name) == "string" and args.name or ""
			Custom.stop(fname)
			return "Stopped '" .. fname .. "'."
		elseif name == "list_features" then
			local names = Custom.list()
			if #names == 0 then
				return "No saved features."
			end
			return table.concat(names, "\n")
		end
		return "Unknown tool: " .. tostring(name)
	end)
	if not ok then
		return "Tool error: " .. tostring(result)
	end
	return tostring(result)
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------ docs for the system prompt
GameTools.TOOLS_DOC = table.concat({
	"TOOLS -- to call one, reply with ONLY a JSON object (no prose, no code fences):",
	'  {"tool":"explore","args":{"path":"game.Workspace","depth":1}}   -- list children (depth 1-3)',
	'  {"tool":"properties","args":{"path":"game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid"}}',
	'  {"tool":"search","args":{"query":"ClientManager","class":"ModuleScript","root":"game"}}',
	'  {"tool":"scripts","args":{"root":"game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts"}}',
	'  {"tool":"decompile","args":{"path":"game.ReplicatedStorage.SomeModule"}}',
	'  {"tool":"api_reference","args":{}}   -- re-read the luma feature API',
	'  {"tool":"run_feature","args":{"name":"My Feature","code":"<full luau code>"}}  -- TEST your code live; returns success or the error',
	'  {"tool":"stop_feature","args":{"name":"My Feature"}}',
	'  {"tool":"list_features","args":{}}',
	"Paths are dot paths from the DataModel. Shortcuts: 'LocalPlayer' and '.Character' resolve correctly.",
	"After I return a tool result, keep going: call more tools or give your final answer. One tool per message.",
	"Prefer to TEST feature code with run_feature before finishing -- if it returns an error, fix it and retry.",
	"When you have enough info, reply normally. If you wrote a feature, put the final code in a ```lua block.",
}, "\n")

GameTools.API_REFERENCE = table.concat({
	"LUMA CUSTOM FEATURE API. User code runs in a sandbox inside the 'luma' Deepwoken exploit menu.",
	"These are available as GLOBAL functions (and under the `Luma` table). Use ONLY this API for UI + lifecycle.",
	"",
	"UI (each returns a control; controls have :get()/:set(v) where sensible, accessed as control.get()/control.set(v)):",
	"  Toggle(text, cb_or_opts)   -- cb_or_opts = function(value) OR { default=bool, callback=fn }",
	"  Button(text, function() end)",
	"  Slider(text, { min=number, max=number, default=number, decimals=number, callback=function(value) end })",
	"  Dropdown(text, { values={...}, default=any, callback=function(value) end })  -- control.setValues(t)",
	"  Input(text, { default=string, callback=function(text) end })  -- or Input(text, function(text) end)",
	"  Keybind(text, { default='E', callback=function(key) end })  -- callback fires when the key is pressed",
	"  Label(text)  -- control.set(text)",
	"  Section(title) -> sub-surface with all the same UI methods",
	"  Window(title) -> a floating draggable window; sub-surface with the same UI methods",
	"",
	"Lifecycle (ALL auto-disconnected when the feature is reloaded/stopped):",
	"  OnRender(function(dt) end)   -- RenderStepped (every frame)",
	"  OnStep(function(dt) end)     -- Heartbeat",
	"  OnInput(function(input, gameProcessed) end)  -- UserInputService.InputBegan",
	"  Interval(seconds, function() end)",
	"  OnCleanup(function() end)",
	"  Notify(text, seconds)",
	"",
	"Game helpers:",
	"  LocalPlayer, Character(), HRP(), Humanoid(), Mouse(), Camera()",
	"  GetTarget() -> nearest target { character, humanoid, root, du=distance } or nil ; GetTargets() -> list",
	"  Value(flag) -> read an existing luma toggle/option by its flag string",
	"  Toggles, Options -> raw luma control tables ; Services -> { Players, RunService, Workspace, ... } ; Library",
	"  Standard Roblox globals work: game, workspace, task, math, string, table, Vector3, CFrame, Instance, etc.",
	"",
	"Rules: guard every frame loop with a toggle so it only runs when enabled. Never call loadstring/HttpGet/",
	"setfenv/require inside a feature. Keep code correct and concise. Example:",
	"  local on = Toggle('Enabled', function(v) end)",
	"  Slider('Speed', { min=16, max=200, default=16, callback=function(v)",
	"    local h = Humanoid(); if h then h.WalkSpeed = v end",
	"  end })",
	"  OnRender(function() if not on.get() then return end end)",
}, "\n")

return GameTools
end)
__bundle_register("Menu/Objects/FloatingWindow", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
-- Reusable floating, draggable window (title bar + close button + body) parented to the menu ScreenGui.
-- new{ title, size, position } -> { frame, body, Show, Hide, Toggle, IsOpen, SetTitle }. Starts hidden.

---@module GUI.Library
local Library = require("GUI/Library")

local FloatingWindow = {}

local BAR_H = 26

---@param opts table
---@return table
function FloatingWindow.new(opts)
	opts = opts or {}
	local size = opts.size or UDim2.fromOffset(360, 300)
	local position = opts.position or UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)

	local outer = Library:Create("Frame", {
		Name = "LumaFloatingWindow",
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = position,
		Size = size,
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.MainColor,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Visible = false,
		ZIndex = 200,
		Parent = Library.ScreenGui,
	})
	Library:Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6), Parent = outer })
	local st = Library:Create("UIStroke", { Color = Library.AccentColor, Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.2, Parent = outer })
	Library:AddToRegistry(outer, { BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor" })
	Library:AddToRegistry(st, { Color = "AccentColor" })
	pcall(function()
		Library:AddUIScale(outer)
	end)

	local bar = Library:Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.BackgroundColor,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, BAR_H),
		ZIndex = 201,
		Parent = outer,
	})
	Library:Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6), Parent = bar })
	Library:AddToRegistry(bar, { BackgroundColor3 = "BackgroundColor" })

	-- cover the bar's bottom rounded corners so it sits flush on the body
	Library:Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.BackgroundColor,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -6),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 6),
		ZIndex = 201,
		Parent = bar,
	})

	local titleLabel = Library:Create("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0),
		Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
		Text = tostring(opts.title or "Window"),
		TextSize = 13,
		TextColor3 = Library.FontColor,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
		ZIndex = 202,
		Parent = bar,
	})
	Library:AddToRegistry(titleLabel, { TextColor3 = "FontColor" })

	local closeBtn = Library:Create("TextButton", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
		Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
		Text = "X",
		TextSize = 13,
		TextColor3 = Library.FontColor,
		ZIndex = 202,
		Parent = bar,
	})
	Library:AddToRegistry(closeBtn, { TextColor3 = "FontColor" })

	local body = Library:Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, BAR_H + 4),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -(BAR_H + 10)),
		ZIndex = 201,
		Parent = outer,
	})

	pcall(function()
		Library:MakeDraggable(outer, BAR_H)
	end)

	local window = {
		frame = outer,
		body = body,
	}

	function window:IsOpen()
		return outer.Visible
	end

	function window:Show()
		outer.Visible = true
		outer.ZIndex = 200
	end

	function window:Hide()
		outer.Visible = false
	end

	function window:Toggle()
		if outer.Visible then
			self:Hide()
		else
			self:Show()
		end
	end

	function window:SetTitle(t)
		titleLabel.Text = tostring(t or "")
	end

	closeBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		closeBtn.TextColor3 = Library.AccentColor
	end)
	closeBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		closeBtn.TextColor3 = Library.FontColor
	end)
	closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		window:Hide()
	end)

	return window
end

return FloatingWindow
end)
__bundle_register("Menu/Objects/CodeEditor", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
-- Reusable Lua code editor widget (line gutter, custom caret, selection highlight, syntax colouring).
-- Lifted from the timing builder's module editor so the Custom tab can offer the exact same editor.
-- create(host, height, placeholder) -> { get, set, setEditable } ; host is a groupbox/tab (has .Container + :Resize()).

---@module GUI.Library
local Library = require("GUI/Library")

local CodeEditor = {}

local LUA_KEYWORDS = {}
for _, kw in ipairs({
	"and", "break", "do", "else", "elseif", "end", "false", "for", "function", "goto",
	"if", "in", "local", "nil", "not", "or", "repeat", "return", "then", "true", "until",
	"while", "continue", "self",
}) do
	LUA_KEYWORDS[kw] = true
end

local HL_KEYWORD = "#f3a8d6"
local HL_STRING = "#a8e6b0"
local HL_NUMBER = "#f5c07a"
local HL_COMMENT = "#8b7fa0"

local function escapeRich(s)
	return (s:gsub("&", "&amp;"):gsub("<", "&lt;"):gsub(">", "&gt;"))
end

local function span(color, text)
	return '<font color="' .. color .. '">' .. escapeRich(text) .. "</font>"
end

local function highlightLua(code)
	local out = {}
	local i, n = 1, #code

	while i <= n do
		local c = code:sub(i, i)
		local two = code:sub(i, i + 1)

		if two == "--" then
			local eqs = code:sub(i + 2):match("^%[(=*)%[")
			if eqs then
				local close = "]" .. eqs .. "]"
				local endpos = code:find(close, i + 2, true)
				local chunk = endpos and code:sub(i, endpos + #close - 1) or code:sub(i)
				out[#out + 1] = span(HL_COMMENT, chunk)
				i = i + #chunk
			else
				local nl = code:find("\n", i, true)
				local chunk = nl and code:sub(i, nl - 1) or code:sub(i)
				out[#out + 1] = span(HL_COMMENT, chunk)
				i = i + #chunk
			end
		elseif c == '"' or c == "'" then
			local j = i + 1
			while j <= n do
				local cj = code:sub(j, j)
				if cj == "\\" then
					j = j + 2
				elseif cj == c or cj == "\n" then
					j = j + 1
					break
				else
					j = j + 1
				end
			end
			local chunk = code:sub(i, j - 1)
			out[#out + 1] = span(HL_STRING, chunk)
			i = i + #chunk
		elseif c == "[" and code:sub(i):match("^%[=*%[") then
			local eqs = code:sub(i):match("^%[(=*)%[")
			local close = "]" .. eqs .. "]"
			local endpos = code:find(close, i + #eqs + 2, true)
			local chunk = endpos and code:sub(i, endpos + #close - 1) or code:sub(i)
			out[#out + 1] = span(HL_STRING, chunk)
			i = i + #chunk
		elseif c:match("%d") or (c == "." and code:sub(i + 1, i + 1):match("%d")) then
			local num = code:sub(i):match("^0[xX]%x+")
				or code:sub(i):match("^%d*%.?%d+[eE][%+%-]?%d+")
				or code:sub(i):match("^%d*%.?%d+")
				or c
			out[#out + 1] = span(HL_NUMBER, num)
			i = i + #num
		elseif c:match("[%a_]") then
			local word = code:sub(i):match("^[%w_]+")
			if LUA_KEYWORDS[word] then
				out[#out + 1] = span(HL_KEYWORD, word)
			else
				out[#out + 1] = escapeRich(word)
			end
			i = i + #word
		else
			out[#out + 1] = escapeRich(c)
			i = i + 1
		end
	end

	return table.concat(out)
end

CodeEditor.highlight = highlightLua
CodeEditor.escape = escapeRich

---@param host table|Instance a groupbox/tab (needs .Container and :Resize()) OR a raw parent frame
---@param height number
---@param placeholder string?
---@return table
function CodeEditor.create(host, height, placeholder, zbase)
	local GUTTER_W = 34
	local PAD = 6
	local PLACEHOLDER = placeholder or "-- write your feature here"
	-- The ScreenGui uses Global ZIndexBehavior, so when the editor lives in a high-ZIndex floating window
	-- its elements must be lifted above the window's background (pass zbase ~205), else it renders hidden.
	local Z = zbase or 5

	-- Accept either a luma groupbox/tab (parent into .Container, call :Resize afterwards) or a plain frame.
	local isContainer = typeof(host) ~= "Instance"
	local parentInst = isContainer and host.Container or host

	local holder = Library:Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.MainColor,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, height),
		LayoutOrder = 9999,
		ZIndex = Z,
		Parent = parentInst,
	})

	Library:Create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4), Parent = holder })
	Library:AddToRegistry(holder, { BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor" })

	local stroke = Library:Create("UIStroke", {
		Color = Library.OutlineColor,
		Thickness = 1,
		Transparency = 0.5,
		Parent = holder,
	})
	Library:AddToRegistry(stroke, { Color = "OutlineColor" })

	local gutterHolder = Library:Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.BackgroundColor,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.4,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ClipsDescendants = true,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, PAD),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, GUTTER_W, 1, -PAD * 2),
		ZIndex = Z + 1,
		Parent = holder,
	})

	local gutter = Library:Create("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		Font = Enum.Font.Code,
		TextSize = 13,
		TextColor3 = Library.OutlineColor,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
		TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
		Text = "1",
		ZIndex = Z + 1,
		Parent = gutterHolder,
	})

	local scroll = Library:Create("ScrollingFrame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, GUTTER_W + 2, 0, PAD),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -(GUTTER_W + 2 + PAD), 1, -PAD * 2),
		CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
		AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,
		ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.XY,
		ScrollBarThickness = 4,
		ZIndex = Z + 1,
		Parent = holder,
	})

	local selectionFrames = {}

	local highlight = Library:Create("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,
		RichText = true,
		Font = Enum.Font.Code,
		TextSize = 13,
		TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 162),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
		Text = escapeRich(PLACEHOLDER),
		ZIndex = Z + 1,
		Parent = scroll,
	})

	local box = Library:Create("TextBox", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,
		MultiLine = true,
		ClearTextOnFocus = false,
		TextEditable = true,
		TextWrapped = false,
		Font = Enum.Font.Code,
		TextSize = 13,
		TextColor3 = Library.FontColor,
		TextTransparency = 1,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
		Text = "",
		PlaceholderText = "",
		ZIndex = Z + 2,
		Parent = scroll,
	})

	local TextService = game:GetService("TextService")
	local HUGE = Vector2.new(1e6, 1e6)
	local LINE_H = 15
	pcall(function()
		LINE_H = TextService:GetTextSize("Ag", 13, Enum.Font.Code, HUGE).Y
	end)

	local caret = Library:Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.FontColor,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, LINE_H),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 0),
		Visible = false,
		ZIndex = Z + 3,
		Parent = scroll,
	})
	Library:AddToRegistry(caret, { BackgroundColor3 = "FontColor" })

	local function updateCaret()
		if not box:IsFocused() then
			caret.Visible = false
			return
		end
		local pos = box.CursorPosition
		if pos < 1 then
			caret.Visible = false
			return
		end
		local before = box.Text:sub(1, pos - 1)
		local line = 0
		for _ in before:gmatch("\n") do
			line = line + 1
		end
		local seg = before:match("[^\n]*$") or ""
		local width = 0
		pcall(function()
			width = TextService:GetTextSize(seg, 13, Enum.Font.Code, HUGE).X
		end)
		caret.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2 + width, 0, line * LINE_H)
		caret.Visible = true
		caret.BackgroundTransparency = 0
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		while caret.Parent do
			task.wait(0.5)
			if box:IsFocused() and caret.Visible then
				caret.BackgroundTransparency = caret.BackgroundTransparency == 0 and 1 or 0
			end
		end
	end)

	local function renderSelection()
		for _, f in ipairs(selectionFrames) do
			f:Destroy()
		end
		selectionFrames = {}

		local sStart, cPos = box.SelectionStart, box.CursorPosition
		if sStart == -1 or cPos == -1 or sStart == cPos then
			return
		end
		local a = math.min(sStart, cPos)
		local b = math.max(sStart, cPos)

		local text = box.Text
		local pos = 1
		for line in (text .. "\n"):gmatch("(.-)\n") do
			local len = #line
			local lineEndPos = pos + len
			local selA = math.max(a, pos)
			local selB = math.min(b, lineEndPos + 1)
			if selB > selA then
				local col1 = math.min(selA - pos, len)
				local col2 = math.min(selB - pos, len)
				local x1, x2 = 0, 0
				pcall(function()
					x1 = TextService:GetTextSize(line:sub(1, col1), 13, Enum.Font.Code, HUGE).X
					x2 = TextService:GetTextSize(line:sub(1, col2), 13, Enum.Font.Code, HUGE).X
				end)
				local lineIdx = 0
				for _ in text:sub(1, pos - 1):gmatch("\n") do
					lineIdx = lineIdx + 1
				end
				local w = x2 - x1
				if b > lineEndPos then
					w = w + 4
				end
				local sel = Library:Create("Frame", {
					BackgroundColor3 = Library.AccentColor,
					BackgroundTransparency = 0.55,
					BorderSizePixel = 0,
					Position = UDim2.new(0, 2 + x1, 0, lineIdx * LINE_H),
					Size = UDim2.new(0, math.max(w, 2), 0, LINE_H),
					ZIndex = Z,
					Parent = scroll,
				})
				Library:AddToRegistry(sel, { BackgroundColor3 = "AccentColor" })
				selectionFrames[#selectionFrames + 1] = sel
			end
			pos = lineEndPos + 1
		end
	end

	box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CursorPosition"):Connect(function()
		updateCaret()
		renderSelection()
	end)
	box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("SelectionStart"):Connect(renderSelection)
	box.Focused:Connect(updateCaret)
	box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		caret.Visible = false
		renderSelection()
	end)

	local function refresh()
		local text = box.Text

		if text == "" then
			highlight.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 162)
			highlight.Text = escapeRich(PLACEHOLDER)
		else
			highlight.TextColor3 = Library.FontColor
			local ok, colored = pcall(highlightLua, text)
			highlight.Text = ok and colored or escapeRich(text)
		end

		local lines = 1
		for _ in text:gmatch("\n") do
			lines = lines + 1
		end
		local nums = {}
		for k = 1, lines do
			nums[k] = tostring(k)
		end
		gutter.Text = table.concat(nums, "\n")
	end

	box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
		refresh()
		updateCaret()
		renderSelection()
	end)
	scroll:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanvasPosition"):Connect(function()
		gutter.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, -scroll.CanvasPosition.Y)
	end)

	refresh()

	if isContainer and host.Resize then
		pcall(function()
			host:Resize()
		end)
	end

	return {
		get = function()
			return box.Text
		end,
		set = function(text)
			box.Text = text or ""
			refresh()
		end,
		setEditable = function(editable)
			box.TextEditable = editable and true or false
			if not editable then
				caret.Visible = false
			end
		end,
	}
end

return CodeEditor
end)
__bundle_register("GUI/SaveManager", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
return require("GUI/LinoriaAddons").SaveManager
end)

__bundle_register("Menu/UtilityTab", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
local UtilityTab = {}

---@module GUI.Icons
local Icons = require("GUI/Icons")

---@param window table
function UtilityTab.init(window)
	local GameTab = require("Menu/GameTab")
	local VisualsTab = require("Menu/VisualsTab")
	local ExploitTab = require("Menu/ExploitTab")

	local tab = window:AddTab("Utility", "Utility", Icons.Exploit)

	local tabbox = tab:AddFullTabbox()

	GameTab.initMovementSection(tabbox:AddTab("Movement"))

	local exploitsTab = tabbox:AddTab("Exploits")
	ExploitTab.initMobExploitsSection(exploitsTab)
	ExploitTab.initLocalCharacterExploitsSection(exploitsTab)
	VisualsTab.initMorphLoaderSection(exploitsTab)

	local realmOk = true
	pcall(function()
		local replicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
		local info = replicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Info", 5)
		local realmInfo = info and info:WaitForChild("RealmInfo", 5)
		local currentWorld = (realmInfo and require(realmInfo).CurrentWorld) or "N/A"
		if currentWorld == "Depths" or currentWorld == "Mission" or currentWorld == "Dungeon" then
			realmOk = false
		end
	end)
	if realmOk then
		ExploitTab.initTeleportsSection(tabbox)
	end

	GameTab.initPlayerMonitoringSection(tabbox:AddTab("Monitoring"))

	GameTab.initInfoSpoofingSection(tabbox:AddTab("Info"))

	local trackersTab = tabbox:AddTab("Trackers")
	VisualsTab.initVisualAssistanceSection(trackersTab)
	GameTab.initRevealAnimationsSection(trackersTab)

	local worldTab = tabbox:AddTab("World")
	VisualsTab.initWorldVisualsSection(worldTab)
	GameTab.initEffectRemovalsSection(worldTab)
	GameTab.initInstanceRemovalsSection(worldTab)
	VisualsTab.initVisualRemovalsSection(worldTab)
	GameTab.initTweeningSection(worldTab)
end

return UtilityTab
end)
__bundle_register("Menu/ExploitTab", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
local ExploitTab = {}

---@module GUI.Icons
local Icons = require("GUI/Icons")

---@module Features.Game.Teleport
local Teleport = require("Features/Game/Teleport")

---@param groupbox table
function ExploitTab.initMobExploitsSection(groupbox)

		Text = "Void Mobs",
		Default = false,
	})

	voidMobsToggle:AddKeyPicker("VoidMobsKeyBind", { Default = "N/A", SyncToggleState = true, Text = "Void Mobs" })
end

---@param groupbox table
function ExploitTab.initLocalCharacterExploitsSection(groupbox)
	local moveWhileKnockedToggle = groupbox:AddToggle("MoveWhileKnocked", {
		Text = "Move While Knocked",
		Default = false,
	})

	moveWhileKnockedToggle:AddKeyPicker(
		"MoveWhileKnockedKeyBind",

	)

		Default = false,

	flingToggle:AddKeyPicker("FlingKeyBind", { Default = "N/A", SyncToggleState = true, Text = "Fling" })

	local flingDepBox = groupbox:AddDependencyBox()

	flingDepBox:AddSlider("FlingPower", {
		Text = "Fling Power",
		Tooltip = "Downward velocity dumped onto your root every frame -- you stay pinned while anyone who touches you inherits the momentum and gets launched. 100000 matches the reference fling.",
		Default = 100000,
		Min = 1000,
		Max = 1000000,
		Rounding = 0,
		Suffix = " studs/s",
	})

	flingDepBox:SetupDependencies({

	})
end

---@param groupbox table
---@param text string
---@param dest string
local function addTeleportButton(groupbox, text, dest)
	groupbox:AddButton({
		Text = text,
		DoubleClick = true,
		DoubleClickText = "Use at your own risk?",
		Func = function()
			Teleport.start(dest)
		end,
	})
end

---@param tabbox table
function ExploitTab.initTeleportsSection(tabbox)
	local teleportsTab = tabbox:AddTab("Teleports")
	local guildDoorTab = teleportsTab

	local Locations = {
		[6032399813] = {
			["Etris"] = CFrame.new(1006, 412, -4412),
			["Minityrsa"] = CFrame.new(-1307, 442, -6573),
			["Lower Erisia"] = CFrame.new(79, 36, 220),
			["Upper Erisia"] = CFrame.new(1593, 872, 907),
			["Isle Of Vigils"] = CFrame.new(-1580, 350, 2658),
			["Warspot"] = CFrame.new(-4329, 985, 4180)
		},
		[6473861193] = {
			["Songseeker"] = CFrame.new(250, 432, -2870),
			["Summer Isle"] = CFrame.new(-2105, 414, -678),
			["Miner's Landing"] = CFrame.new(-3158, 509, 3199),
			["Starfield Veldt"] = CFrame.new(1546, 749, 4060),
			["The Entropy Catalyst"] = CFrame.new(1307, 980, 1980),
			["Moon's Eyrie"] = CFrame.new(-4945, 480, -13955),
			["Monkey's Paw"] = CFrame.new(-9357, 789, -6687),
			["Thresher's Nest"] = CFrame.new(-5499, 1040, -362),
			["Starswept Valley"] = CFrame.new(-5499, 1040, -362),
			["Great Hive"] = CFrame.new(-7391, 962, -313),
			["Metoer Isle"] = CFrame.new(-7884, 159, 1118),
			["Fort Merit"] = CFrame.new(-7612, 671, 3013),
			["Ferryman Island"] = CFrame.new(-4601, 158, 9527),
		}
	}

	local currentPlaceId = game.PlaceId
	local placeData = Locations[currentPlaceId]

	if placeData then
		local teleportList = {}
		for name, _ in pairs(placeData) do
			table.insert(teleportList, name)
		end

		local teleportsdropdown = teleportsTab:AddDropdown("Teleports", {
			Text = "Teleports List",
			Values = teleportList,
			Multi = false,
			AllowNull = true,
		})

		teleportsTab:AddButton("Teleport To Location", function()
			local selected = teleportsdropdown.Value
			local targetCFrame = placeData[selected]
			
			if targetCFrame then
				Teleport.Safetweenstart(targetCFrame, 190)
			end
		end)
	end

	if game.PlaceId == 6032399813 then
		addTeleportButton(teleportsTab, "Teleport to Eastern Luminant", "EasternLuminant")
		addTeleportButton(teleportsTab, "Teleport to Trial Of One", "TrialOfOne")
	end

	if game.PlaceId == 6473861193 then
		addTeleportButton(teleportsTab, "Teleport to Etrean Luminant", "EtreanLuminant")
	end

	addTeleportButton(teleportsTab, "Teleport to Depths", "Depths")
	addTeleportButton(teleportsTab, "Teleport to Voidheart", "Voidheart")

	teleportsTab:AddButton("Stop Teleporting", Teleport.stop and Teleport.Safetweenstop)

	guildDoorTab:AddDivider()

	guildDoorTab:AddInput("GuildDoorName", {
		Text = "Guild Door Name",
		Placeholder = "Exact or partial name of door.",
	})

	addTeleportButton(guildDoorTab, "Teleport To Guild Door", "GuildDoor")
end

return ExploitTab
end)
__bundle_register("Menu/VisualsTab", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
---@module GUI.Icons
local Icons = require("GUI/Icons")

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Configuration
local Configuration = require("Utility/Configuration")

---@module Features.Visuals.Visuals
local Visuals = require("Features/Visuals/Visuals")

---@module Utility.JSON
local JSON = require("Utility/JSON")

---@module Features.Visuals.Objects.BuilderData
local BuilderData = require("Features/Visuals/Objects/BuilderData")

local VisualsTab = {}

---@param groupbox table
function VisualsTab.initESPCustomization(groupbox)
	groupbox:AddSlider("FontSize", {
		Text = "ESP Font Size",
		Default = 16,
		Min = 4,
		Max = 24,
		Rounding = 0,
	})

	groupbox:AddSlider("ESPSplitLineLength", {
		Text = "ESP Split Line Length",
		Default = 30,
		Min = 10,
		Max = 100,
		Rounding = 0,
	})

	local fonts = {}

	for _, font in next, Enum.Font:GetEnumItems() do
		if font == Enum.Font.Unknown then
			continue
		end

		table.insert(fonts, font.Name)
	end

	groupbox:AddDropdown("Font", { Text = "ESP Fonts", Default = 1, Values = fonts })
end

---@param groupbox table
function VisualsTab.initESPOptimizations(groupbox)
	groupbox:AddToggle("ESPLimitUpdates", {
		Text = "ESP Limit Updates",
		Default = true,
	})

	local eluDepBox = groupbox:AddDependencyBox()

	eluDepBox:AddSlider("ESPRefreshRate", {
		Text = "ESP Refresh Rate",
		Suffix = "f",
		Default = 30,
		Min = 1,
		Max = 144,
		Rounding = 0,
	})

	eluDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ Toggles.ESPLimitUpdates, true },
	})

	groupbox:AddToggle("ESPSplitUpdates", {
		Text = "ESP Split Updates",
		Default = false,
	})

	local esuDepBox = groupbox:AddDependencyBox()

	esuDepBox:AddSlider("ESPSplitFrames", {
		Text = "ESP Split Frames",
		Suffix = "f",
		Default = 32,
		Min = 1,
		Max = 64,
		Rounding = 0,
	})

	esuDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ Toggles.ESPSplitUpdates, true },
	})

	groupbox:AddToggle("NoPersisentESP", {
		Text = "No Persistent ESP",
		Default = false,
	})
end

---@param groupbox table
function VisualsTab.initWorldVisualsSection(groupbox)
	groupbox:AddToggle("OptimizeGame", {
		Text = "Optimize Game",
		Tooltip = "Rate-limits Deepwoken's water (SeaClient) and world render (WorldClient) loops for higher FPS by re-driving the game's render connections.",
		Default = false,
	})

	groupbox:AddToggle("UseFastFlags", {
		Text = "Use Fast Flags For Session",
		Default = false,
	})

	Toggles.UseFastFlags:OnChanged(function(state)
		if not state then
			return
		end

		local success, content = pcall(readfile, "fflags.json")
		if not success then
			return Logger.longNotify("Failed to load fflags.json. Make sure the file exists in your workspace.")
		end
		local decodeSuccess, fflags = pcall(game:GetService("HttpService").JSONDecode, game:GetService("HttpService"), content)
		if not decodeSuccess then
			return Logger.longNotify("fflags.json is not a valid JSON format.")
		end
		for index, value in next, fflags do
			pcall(function()
				setfflag(index, tostring(value))
			end)
		end
		Logger.notify("Fast Flags applied successfully for this session server hop to see changes.")
	end)

	groupbox:AddToggle("ModifyFieldOfView", {
		Text = "Modify Field Of View",
		Default = false,
	})

	local fovDepBox = groupbox:AddDependencyBox()

	fovDepBox:AddSlider("FieldOfView", {
		Text = "Field Of View Slider",
		Default = 90,
		Min = 0,
		Max = 120,
		Suffix = "°",
		Rounding = 0,
	})

	fovDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ Toggles.ModifyFieldOfView, true },
	})

	local modifyAmbienceToggle = groupbox:AddToggle("ModifyAmbience", {
		Text = "Modify Ambience",
		Default = false,
	})

	modifyAmbienceToggle:AddColorPicker("AmbienceColor", {
		Default = Color3.fromHex("FFFFFF"),
	})

	local oacDepBox = groupbox:AddDependencyBox()

	oacDepBox:AddToggle("OriginalAmbienceColor", {
		Text = "Original Ambience Color",
		Default = false,
	})

	local umacDepBox = oacDepBox:AddDependencyBox()

	umacDepBox:AddSlider("OriginalAmbienceColorBrightness", {
		Text = "Original Ambience Brightness",
		Default = 0,
		Min = 0,
		Max = 255,
		Suffix = "+",
		Rounding = 0,
	})

	oacDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ Toggles.ModifyAmbience, true },
	})

	umacDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ Toggles.OriginalAmbienceColor, true },
	})
end

---@param groupbox table
function VisualsTab.initVisualAssistanceSection(groupbox)
	groupbox:AddToggle("ChainOfPerfectionTracker", {
		Text = "Chain Of Perfection Tracker",
		Default = false,
	})

	groupbox:AddToggle("SanityTracker", {
		Text = "Sanity Tracker",
		Default = false,
	})

	groupbox:AddToggle("MysteryMantraRevealer", {
		Text = "Mystery Mantra Revealer",
		Default = false,
	})

	groupbox:AddToggle("Roll2Revealer", {
		Text = "Roll 2 Revealer",
		Default = false,
	})

	local buildAssistanceToggle = groupbox:AddToggle("BuildAssistance", {
		Text = "Build Assistance",
		Default = false,
	})

	local buildAssistanceDepBox = groupbox:AddDependencyBox()

	local sdoToggle = buildAssistanceDepBox:AddToggle("ShrineDetectionOverride", {
		Text = "Shrine Detection Override",
		Default = true,
	})

	local sdoDepBox = buildAssistanceDepBox:AddDependencyBox()

	sdoDepBox:AddDropdown("ShrineOverrideState", {
		Text = "Shrine Override State",
		Default = 1,
		Values = { "Pre-Shrine", "Post-Shrine", "Must Shrine" },
	})

	sdoDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ sdoToggle, true },
	})

	buildAssistanceDepBox:AddToggle("AutoFavourCards", {
		Text = "Auto Favour Cards",
		Default = false,
	})

	buildAssistanceDepBox:AddInput("BuildAssistanceLink", {
		Text = "Build Assistance Link",
		Placeholder = "Enter your builder link here.",
		Finished = true,
		Callback = function(value)
			-- Empty box (e.g. restored by a config load) -> do nothing, silently. Only complain when
			-- there's actual text that failed to parse.
			if type(value) ~= "string" or value:gsub("%s+", "") == "" then
				return
			end

			local headers = {
				["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
				["Accept"] = "application/json",
				["Origin"] = "https://deepwoken.co",
				["Referer"] = "https://deepwoken.co/builder",
				["Sec-Fetch-Site"] = "same-origin",
				["Sec-Fetch-Mode"] = "cors",
				["Sec-Fetch-Dest"] = "empty",
			}

			local id = value:match("[?&]id=([%w%d]+)") or value:match("^%s*([%w%d]+)%s*$")
			if not id then
				return Logger.notify("Invalid builder link. Please provide a valid builder URL or builder id.")
			end

			local bresponse = request({
				Url = "https://new.deepwoken.co/api/proxy/builds/" .. id,
				Method = "GET",
				Headers = headers,
			})

			if not bresponse or not bresponse.Success or not bresponse.Body then
				return Logger.notify("Invalid response while fetching builder response.")
			end

			local bsuccess, bresult = pcall(JSON.decode, bresponse.Body)
			if not bsuccess or not bresult then
				return Logger.notify("JSON error '%s' while deserializing builder response.", bresult)
			end

			Logger.notify("Successfully fetched build data.")

			local dresponse = request({
				Url = "https://new.deepwoken.co/api/proxy/get?type=all",
				Method = "GET",
				Headers = headers,
			})

			if not dresponse or not dresponse.Success or not dresponse.Body then
				return Logger.notify("Invalid response while fetching data response.")
			end

			local dsuccess, dresult = pcall(JSON.decode, dresponse.Body)
			if not dsuccess or not dresult then
				return Logger.notify("JSON error '%s' while deserializing data response.", dresult)
			end

			Logger.notify("Successfully created BuilderData object.")

			Visuals.bdata = BuilderData.new(bresult, dresult)
		end,
	})

	buildAssistanceDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ buildAssistanceToggle, true },
	})
end
