--- Morph loader. Models live under the executor workspace at luma_morphs/Races and luma_morphs/Ornaments.
---@param groupbox table
function VisualsTab.initMorphLoaderSection(groupbox)
	local Players = game:GetService("Players")
	local lp = Players.LocalPlayer

	-- Executor file API resolution (Synapse Z / generic).
	local readCustomAsset = getcustomasset or getsynasset or (syn and syn.get_custom_asset)
	local listFiles = listfiles

	local FOLDERS = {
		Races = "Luma/luma_morphs/Races",
		Ornaments = "Luma/luma_morphs/Ornaments",
	}

	-- Create the morph folders on first execution, the same way the config / theme folders get made.
	do
		local function ensureFolder(path)
			if type(makefolder) ~= "function" then
				return
			end
			if type(isfolder) == "function" and isfolder(path) then
				return
			end
			pcall(makefolder, path)
		end
		ensureFolder("Luma")
		ensureFolder("Luma/luma_morphs")
		ensureFolder(FOLDERS.Races)
		ensureFolder(FOLDERS.Ornaments)
	end

	local HOLDER_NAME = "LumaMorph_Applied"
	local BACKUP_NAME = "LumaMorph_Backup"
	-- Per-morph baked forward nudge (studs, along facing).
	local MORPH_FORWARD = {
	}
	-- Morphs that ship a HideBody flag but look better worn OVER the body.
	local MORPH_NOHIDE = {
	}
	-- For hide-body morphs with gaps, keep these character limbs visible to plug the holes. morphName -> { limbName = true }.
	local MORPH_KEEP_VISIBLE = {
	}

	local currentCategory = "Races"
	local currentFile = nil
	local lastApplied = nil -- { category = , file = }
	local hideBodyEnabled = true
	local reapplyEnabled = false
	local hideToggle -- forward declared; assigned in UI section below
	local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
	local hideConn = nil
	local hiddenList = {}

	local function listMorphs(dir)
		if type(listFiles) ~= "function" then return {} end
		local ok, res = pcall(listFiles, dir)
		if not ok or type(res) ~= "table" then return {} end
		local out = {}
		for _, p in ipairs(res) do
			local path = tostring(p):gsub("\\", "/")
			if path:sub(-5):lower() == ".rbxm" then
				local name = path:match("([^/]+)%.rbxm$")
				if name then table.insert(out, name) end
			end
		end
		table.sort(out)
		return out
	end

	local function getChar()
		local c = lp.Character
		if not c then return nil end
		if not c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
			c:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
		end
		return c
	end

	-- Stop enforcing invisibility and restore everything we hid.
	local function stopHide()
		if hideConn then hideConn:Disconnect(); hideConn = nil end
		for _, p in ipairs(hiddenList) do
			if p and p.Parent then
				pcall(function()
					if p:IsA("BasePart") then
						p.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
					end
					if p:GetAttribute("LumaMorphHidden") ~= nil then
						p.Transparency = p:GetAttribute("LumaMorphHidden")
						p:SetAttribute("LumaMorphHidden", nil)
					end
				end)
			end
		end
		hiddenList = {}
	end

	-- Keep the given parts/decals invisible every frame (Deepwoken re-shows them otherwise).
	local function startHide(parts)
		stopHide()
		hiddenList = parts
		hideConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
			for _, p in ipairs(hiddenList) do
				if p and p.Parent then
					if p:IsA("BasePart") then
						p.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
					else
						p.Transparency = 1
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	local function clearMorphs()
		stopHide()
		local c = lp.Character
		if not c then return end
		-- Remove morph clothing clones we added.
		for _, p in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
			if p:GetAttribute("LumaMorphClothing") then p:Destroy() end
		end
		-- Restore any original clothing we backed up.
		local backup = c:FindFirstChild(BACKUP_NAME)
		if backup then
			for _, p in ipairs(backup:GetChildren()) do p.Parent = c end
			backup:Destroy()
		end
		local h = c:FindFirstChild(HOLDER_NAME)
		if h then h:Destroy() end
		-- Restore / remove any face decals we changed.
		for _, p in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
			if p:GetAttribute("LumaMorphFaceClone") then
				p:Destroy()
			elseif p:GetAttribute("LumaMorphFace") ~= nil then
				pcall(function() p.Texture = p:GetAttribute("LumaMorphFace") end)
				p:SetAttribute("LumaMorphFace", nil)
			end
		end
		-- Safety sweep for any leftover hidden decals from a prior run.
		for _, p in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
			if p:GetAttribute("LumaMorphHidden") ~= nil then
				pcall(function() p.Transparency = p:GetAttribute("LumaMorphHidden") end)
				p:SetAttribute("LumaMorphHidden", nil)
			end
		end
		-- Restore any real body limbs we recolored to a morph's skin tone.
		for _, p in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
			if p:GetAttribute("LumaMorphSkin") ~= nil then
				pcall(function() p.Color = p:GetAttribute("LumaMorphSkin") end)
				p:SetAttribute("LumaMorphSkin", nil)
			end
		end
	end

	-- Character limb parts (R15 first, R6 fallback) we can bind morph pieces to.
	local LIMB_NAMES = {
		"Head", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso",
		"LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand",
		"RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm", "RightHand",
		"LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot",
		"RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot",
		"Torso", "Left Arm", "Right Arm", "Left Leg", "Right Leg",
	}
	-- Names a morph tends to use for its torso/root anchor.
	local ANCHOR_NAMES = {
		"HumanoidRootPart", "UpperTorso", "Torso", "LowerTorso",
		"tt", "t", "lt", "Chestplate", "Body", "Root", "Middle",
	}

	local function collectLimbs(char)
		local limbs = {}
		for _, name in ipairs(LIMB_NAMES) do
			local p = char:FindFirstChild(name)
			if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then limbs[#limbs + 1] = p end
		end
		return limbs
	end

	local function findCharAnchor(char)
		return char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
			or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
			or char:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso")
			or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	end

	local function findMorphAnchor(model)
		for _, an in ipairs(ANCHOR_NAMES) do
			local p = model:FindFirstChild(an, true)
			if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then return p end
		end
		return model.PrimaryPart or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
	end

	local function nearestLimb(limbs, pos)
		local best, bestDist
		for _, l in ipairs(limbs) do
			local d = (l.Position - pos).Magnitude
			if not bestDist or d < bestDist then bestDist, best = d, l end
		end
		return best
	end

	-- Map a keyword in a morph part's name to a character limb, so morphs that bind by naming convention land on the intended limb instead of relying on raw nearest-position guessing.
	local LIMB_HINTS = {
		{ "uppertorso", { "UpperTorso" } },
		{ "lowertorso", { "LowerTorso" } },
		{ "torso", { "UpperTorso", "Torso", "LowerTorso" } },
		{ "chest", { "UpperTorso", "Torso" } },
		{ "head", { "Head" } }, { "face", { "Head" } }, { "hair", { "Head" } },
		{ "hat", { "Head" } }, { "horn", { "Head" } }, { "mask", { "Head" } }, { "helm", { "Head" } },
		-- Head/hair overlay meshes (ippo's "Shading") are head pieces; without a hint they'd fall to the torso and render on the chest.
		{ "shading", { "Head" } }, { "shade", { "Head" } },
		{ "lefthand", { "LeftHand", "Left Arm" } }, { "righthand", { "RightHand", "Right Arm" } },
		{ "leftlowerarm", { "LeftLowerArm", "Left Arm" } }, { "rightlowerarm", { "RightLowerArm", "Right Arm" } },
		{ "leftupperarm", { "LeftUpperArm", "Left Arm" } }, { "rightupperarm", { "RightUpperArm", "Right Arm" } },
		{ "leftarm", { "LeftUpperArm", "Left Arm" } }, { "rightarm", { "RightUpperArm", "Right Arm" } },
		{ "leftfoot", { "LeftFoot", "Left Leg" } }, { "rightfoot", { "RightFoot", "Right Leg" } },
		{ "leftlowerleg", { "LeftLowerLeg", "Left Leg" } }, { "rightlowerleg", { "RightLowerLeg", "Right Leg" } },
		{ "leftupperleg", { "LeftUpperLeg", "Left Leg" } }, { "rightupperleg", { "RightUpperLeg", "Right Leg" } },
		{ "leftleg", { "LeftUpperLeg", "Left Leg" } }, { "rightleg", { "RightUpperLeg", "Right Leg" } },
		{ "arm", { "UpperTorso", "Torso" } }, -- generic "arms equipment" binds to the torso anchor
	}
	local function limbFromName(char, name)
		local n = tostring(name):lower():gsub("[^%w]", "")
		for _, pair in ipairs(LIMB_HINTS) do
			if n:find(pair[1], 1, true) then
				for _, cand in ipairs(pair[2]) do
					local limb = char:FindFirstChild(cand)
					if limb and limb:IsA("BasePart") then
						return limb
					end
				end
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	-- The attachment a morph part welds by.
	local function bindingAttachment(part)
		local a = part:FindFirstChild("Offset") or part:FindFirstChild("JointOffset")
			or part:FindFirstChild("Attach")
		if a and a:IsA("Attachment") then
			return a
		end
		return nil
	end

	-- Per-morph, per-part vertical lift (studs) for pieces a morph anchors too low.
	local MORPH_PART_LIFT = {
		FifthGear = { ArmsEquipment_Torso = 1.3 },
	}
	local function partLift(morphName, partName)
		local base = tostring(morphName):gsub("%s*%(%d+%)%s*$", "")
		local t = MORPH_PART_LIFT[base]
		return (t and t[partName]) or 0
	end

	-- Per-morph skin tone.
	local MORPH_SKIN = {
		FifthGear = Color3.fromRGB(226, 178, 132), -- Gear 5 golden tan
	}
	-- Real body limbs to recolor (R6 + R15 names; whichever the character actually has).
	local SKIN_LIMBS = {
		"Torso", "Left Arm", "Right Arm", "Left Leg", "Right Leg",
		"UpperTorso", "LowerTorso",
		"LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand",
		"RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm", "RightHand",
		"LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot",
		"RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot",
	}

	-- Full R15 limb names.
	local R15_LIMB_SET = {
		Head = true, UpperTorso = true, LowerTorso = true,
		LeftUpperArm = true, LeftLowerArm = true, LeftHand = true,
		RightUpperArm = true, RightLowerArm = true, RightHand = true,
		LeftUpperLeg = true, LeftLowerLeg = true, LeftFoot = true,
		RightUpperLeg = true, RightLowerLeg = true, RightFoot = true,
	}

	-- ---- Per-morph overrides (keyed by base file name, trailing " (n)" copy-suffix ignored) ----
	local function morphBase(name) return tostring(name):gsub("%s*%(%d+%)%s*$", "") end
	-- Morphs whose "head" is a stiff extension of the body: weld the head region to the TORSO, not the neck, so it can't swing away from the body when a weapon draw / emote animation tilts the head.
	local MORPH_RIGID_HEAD = { TungTung = true }
	-- Redundant / overlapping pieces to drop outright.
	local MORPH_HIDE_PARTS = {
		Mudskipper = { MudskipperHatA = true },
	}
	-- Force a named morph part onto a named character limb, overriding the name-guessing.
	local MORPH_PART_LIMB = {}
	-- Corrective vertical lift (studs) for DRIVEN sub-parts that sit too low -- their joint offset drops them onto the chest.
	local MORPH_SUBPART_LIFT = { ippo = { MeshPart = 1.5 } }

	local function applyModel(model, hideBody, morphName)
		local char = getChar()
		if not char then return false, "no character" end
		if not char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return false, "no HumanoidRootPart" end

		local limbs = collectLimbs(char)
		if #limbs == 0 then return false, "no limbs on character" end

		local extraHide = {} -- specific parts (e.g. real hair) to hide even when not hiding the whole body
		local morphHasHair = false -- set true if the morph supplies its own hair

		local base = morphBase(morphName)

		-- NOTE: do NOT strip the morph's LocalScripts -- on these Deepwoken race morphs they apply the skin/hair/eye colouring from RaceData at runtime; removing them renders the morph flat black.

		-- Drop pieces this morph is known to double up on (prevents z-fighting overlaps).
		local hideParts = MORPH_HIDE_PARTS[base]
		if hideParts then
			for _, p in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
				if p:IsA("BasePart") and hideParts[p.Name] then
					pcall(function() p:Destroy() end)
				end
			end
		end

		-- Per-morph forced part->limb map, consulted before name guessing.
		local partLimbMap = MORPH_PART_LIMB[base]
		local function overrideLimb(partName)
			local nm = partLimbMap and partLimbMap[partName]
			if nm then
				local l = char:FindFirstChild(nm)
				if l and l:IsA("BasePart") then return l end
			end
			return nil
		end
		local rigidHead = MORPH_RIGID_HEAD[base]
		-- Records which character limb each morph part was bound to, so sub-parts of an assembly can inherit their parent's limb instead of falling to a nearest-position guess that can strand them on the chest.
		local partToLimb = {}

		-- Snapshot the real body NOW (before adding morph parts) so we can hide it wholesale, skipping any joint-filler limbs this morph wants kept visible.
		local keepVisible = MORPH_KEEP_VISIBLE[morphName] or {}
		local originalBits = {}
		for _, p in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
			local isPart = p:IsA("BasePart") and p.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart"
			local isDecal = p:IsA("Decal") or p:IsA("Texture")
			if isPart or isDecal then
				local ownerName = isPart and p.Name or (p.Parent and p.Parent.Name)
				if not (ownerName and keepVisible[ownerName]) then
					if isDecal and p:GetAttribute("LumaMorphHidden") == nil then
						p:SetAttribute("LumaMorphHidden", p.Transparency)
					end
					originalBits[#originalBits + 1] = p
				end
			end
		end

		local cAnchor = findCharAnchor(char)
		local mAnchor = findMorphAnchor(model)
		if not cAnchor or not mAnchor then return false, "could not find torso anchor" end

		-- Does this morph actually replace the body?
		local morphWantsHide = model:FindFirstChild("HideBody", true) ~= nil
		-- Only trust the anchor if it's a genuine torso part; otherwise recenter afterward.
		local TRUE_TORSO = {
			HumanoidRootPart = true, UpperTorso = true, Torso = true,
			LowerTorso = true, tt = true, t = true, lt = true,
		}
		local trustedAnchor = TRUE_TORSO[mAnchor.Name] == true

		-- Transform that maps morph-space onto the character.
		local T = cAnchor.CFrame * mAnchor.CFrame:Inverse()

		-- Parts already driven by the morph's own joints — don't re-weld those.
		local driven = {}
		for _, j in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
			if j:IsA("Weld") or j:IsA("Motor6D") or j:IsA("WeldConstraint")
				or j:IsA("Snap") or j:IsA("ManualWeld") then
				if j.Part1 then driven[j.Part1] = true end
			end
		end

		-- Rigidly slide the whole morph onto the character (keeps internal layout + joints).
		for _, p in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
			if p:IsA("BasePart") then
				p.CFrame = T * p.CFrame
			end
		end

		-- If we anchored off a non-torso part (e.g.
		if not trustedAnchor then
			local sum, cnt = Vector3.zero, 0
			for _, p in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
				if p:IsA("BasePart") then sum = sum + p.Position; cnt = cnt + 1 end
			end
			if cnt > 0 then
				local center = sum / cnt
				local delta = Vector3.new(cAnchor.Position.X - center.X, 0, cAnchor.Position.Z - center.Z)
				for _, p in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
					if p:IsA("BasePart") then p.CFrame = p.CFrame + delta end
				end
			end
		end

		-- Forward/back nudge along the character's facing: per-morph baked default.
		local fwdAmount = MORPH_FORWARD[morphName] or 0
		if fwdAmount ~= 0 then
			local fwd = cAnchor.CFrame.LookVector * fwdAmount
			for _, p in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
				if p:IsA("BasePart") then p.CFrame = p.CFrame + fwd end
			end
		end

		-- Full-body R15 rig morphs (e.g.
		local rigParts = {}
		for _, p in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
			if p:IsA("BasePart") and R15_LIMB_SET[p.Name] and char:FindFirstChild(p.Name) then
				rigParts[#rigParts + 1] = p
			end
		end
		if #rigParts >= 6 then
			-- Cut the morph's internal limb welds so each limb is free to follow its own bone.
			for _, w in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
				if (w:IsA("Weld") or w:IsA("Motor6D") or w:IsA("ManualWeld")
					or w:IsA("Snap") or w:IsA("WeldConstraint"))
					and w.Part1 and R15_LIMB_SET[w.Part1.Name] then
					w:Destroy()
				end
			end
			for _, p in ipairs(rigParts) do
				local targetName = p.Name
				local keepPos = false
				-- Rigid-head morphs: pin the head to the torso and KEEP its current offset above the body, so the log head rides the torso as one piece instead of pivoting on the neck when the character animates.
				if rigidHead and p.Name == "Head" then
					if char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") then targetName = "UpperTorso"
					elseif char:FindFirstChild("Torso") then targetName = "Torso" end
					keepPos = true
				end
				local real = char:FindFirstChild(targetName)
				if real and real:IsA("BasePart") then
					-- A standard R15 skinned part's CFrame IS its bone frame, so aligning it to the real limb's CFrame fits it exactly; the weld then locks it there.
					if not keepPos then p.CFrame = real.CFrame end
					p.Anchored = false; p.CanCollide = false; p.Massless = true
					local w = Instance.new("Weld")
					w.Name = "LumaMorphWeld"
					w.Part0 = real
					w.Part1 = p
					w.C0 = real.CFrame:Inverse() * p.CFrame
					w.C1 = CFrame.new()
					w.Parent = p
					driven[p] = true
				end
			end
			-- Hide the leftover bone-anchor Parts (la/t/h/...): they only carried the welds we just replaced and would otherwise render as stray blocks around the body.
			for _, p in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
				if p.ClassName == "Part" and not driven[p] then
					pcall(function()
						p.Transparency = 1; p.CanCollide = false; p.Massless = true
					end)
				end
			end
		end

		-- Cosmetic/clothing morphs: place Offset-attachment pieces on the right limb and swap the face decal.
		if not morphWantsHide then
			local headPart = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
			local torsoPart = char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
			for _, p in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
				if p:IsA("BasePart") and not driven[p] then
					local off = bindingAttachment(p)
					if off then
						local n = p.Name:lower()
						local isHair = n:find("hair") ~= nil
						-- Prefer the limb named in the part itself (e.g.
						local limb = overrideLimb(p.Name) or limbFromName(char, p.Name)
						if not limb then
							if isHair or n:find("head") or n:find("face")
								or n:find("hat") or n:find("horn") or n:find("mask") then
								limb = headPart
							else
								limb = torsoPart
							end
						end
						if limb then
							-- The Offset attachment marks where the piece meets the limb; align that attachment onto the limb so it sits right.
							p.CFrame = limb.CFrame * off.CFrame:Inverse()
							-- Per-morph vertical correction for pieces that anchor too low.
							local lift = partLift(morphName, p.Name)
							if lift ~= 0 then
								p.CFrame = p.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, lift, 0)
							end
							p.Anchored = false; p.CanCollide = false; p.Massless = true
							local w = Instance.new("Weld")
							w.Name = "LumaMorphWeld"
							w.Part0 = limb
							w.Part1 = p
							w.C0 = limb.CFrame:Inverse() * p.CFrame
							w.C1 = CFrame.new()
							w.Parent = p
							driven[p] = true
							partToLimb[p] = limb
							if isHair then morphHasHair = true end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			-- Face: Deepwoken renders the face as a Decal sitting on the FaceMount mesh -- NOT the block Head, and NOT the mesh's TextureID.
			local faces = model:FindFirstChild("Faces", true)
			local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
			if faces and head then
				local srcFace = faces:FindFirstChild("Base") or faces:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Decal")
				if srcFace and srcFace:IsA("Decal") then
					local faceTex = srcFace.Texture
					-- The face decal lives under the head (usually on FaceMount).
					local faceDecal
					for _, d in ipairs(head:GetDescendants()) do
						if d:IsA("Decal") then
							if d.Name:lower() == "face" then
								faceDecal = d
								break
							elseif not faceDecal then
								faceDecal = d
							end
						end
					end
					if faceDecal then
						if faceDecal:GetAttribute("LumaMorphFace") == nil then
							faceDecal:SetAttribute("LumaMorphFace", faceDecal.Texture)
						end
						faceDecal.Texture = faceTex
						-- Deepwoken layers separate overlay decals for the eyes/brows on top of the base face; the morph face already includes its own eyes, so hide every OTHER face decal/texture on the head so the real eyes don't show through.
						for _, d in ipairs(head:GetDescendants()) do
							if d ~= faceDecal
								and (d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture"))
								and not d:GetAttribute("LumaMorphFaceClone")
							then
								if d:GetAttribute("LumaMorphHidden") == nil then
									d:SetAttribute("LumaMorphHidden", d.Transparency)
								end
								d.Transparency = 1
							end
						end
					else
						-- No existing face decal: mount a fresh one on FaceMount's front (a Decal keeps alpha, so it blends instead of boxing solid).
						local mount = head:FindFirstChild("FaceMount") or head
						local nd = srcFace:Clone()
						nd.Name = "LumaMorphFace"
						nd.Face = Enum.NormalId.Front
						nd:SetAttribute("LumaMorphFaceClone", true)
						nd.Parent = mount
					end
				end
			end
		end

		-- Bind each free (non-driven) part to a limb so it sits right and articulates.
		local bound = 0
		for _, p in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
			if p:IsA("BasePart") and not driven[p] then
				local limb = overrideLimb(p.Name) or limbFromName(char, p.Name)
				if not limb then
					-- Inherit the limb an ancestor part was bound to: a strand parented under a hair clump belongs on the head with it, not wherever nearest-position lands.
					local anc = p.Parent
					while anc and anc ~= model do
						if partToLimb[anc] then limb = partToLimb[anc]; break end
						anc = anc.Parent
					end
				end
				limb = limb or nearestLimb(limbs, p.Position)
				if limb then
					p.Anchored = false
					p.CanCollide = false
					p.Massless = true
					local w = Instance.new("Weld")
					w.Name = "LumaMorphWeld"
					w.Part0 = limb
					w.Part1 = p
					w.C0 = limb.CFrame:Inverse() * p.CFrame
					w.C1 = CFrame.new()
					w.Parent = p
					partToLimb[p] = limb
					bound = bound + 1
				end
			end
		end

		-- Safety net: if every part looked driven, at least pin the anchor to the torso.
		if bound == 0 then
			mAnchor.Anchored = false
			local w = Instance.new("Weld")
			w.Name = "LumaMorphWeld"
			w.Part0 = cAnchor
			w.Part1 = mAnchor
			w.C0 = cAnchor.CFrame:Inverse() * mAnchor.CFrame
			w.Parent = mAnchor
		end

		-- Nothing anchored / colliding.
		for _, p in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
			if p:IsA("BasePart") then
				p.Anchored = false
				p.CanCollide = false
			end
		end

		-- Accessories self-weld under the Humanoid.
		for _, acc in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
			if acc:IsA("Accessory") then acc.Parent = char end
		end

		-- Turn effects on (they usually ship disabled).
		for _, fx in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
			if fx:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or fx:IsA("Trail") or fx:IsA("Beam")
				or fx:IsA("PointLight") or fx:IsA("SpotLight") or fx:IsA("SurfaceLight") then
				pcall(function() fx.Enabled = true end)
			end
		end

		-- Apply clothing morphs (Shirt / Pants / graphic) straight onto the character.
		local backup = char:FindFirstChild(BACKUP_NAME)
		for _, cl in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
			if cl:IsA("Shirt") or cl:IsA("Pants") or cl:IsA("ShirtGraphic") then
				if not backup then
					backup = Instance.new("Folder")
					backup.Name = BACKUP_NAME
					backup.Parent = char
				end
				local existing = char:FindFirstChildOfClass(cl.ClassName)
				if existing then existing.Parent = backup end
				local clone = cl:Clone()
				clone:SetAttribute("LumaMorphClothing", true)
				clone.Parent = char
				-- The carrier part (e.g.
				local carrier = cl.Parent
				if carrier and carrier:IsA("BasePart") then
					carrier.Transparency = 1
					carrier.CanCollide = false
				end
			end
		end

		-- Hide the player's real hair whenever the morph replaces the body or brings its own hair.
		if morphWantsHide or morphHasHair then
			for _, acc in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
				if acc:IsA("Accessory") then
					local handle = acc:FindFirstChild("Handle")
					local isHairAcc = false
					local ok, at = pcall(function() return acc.AccessoryType end)
					if ok and at == Enum.AccessoryType.Hair then isHairAcc = true end
					if handle and handle:FindFirstChild("HairAttachment") then isHairAcc = true end
					if isHairAcc and handle then extraHide[#extraHide + 1] = handle end
				end
			end
		end

		-- Wear-over morph that brings its OWN head: the real Deepwoken head keeps rendering straight through the morph one -- the "two heads" bug.
		if hideBody and not morphWantsHide then
			local morphReplacesHead = false
			for _, p in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
				-- Exact "Head" only: a piece merely CONTAINING "head" is an accessory, not a head replacement -- hiding the real head for those would leave the character headless.
				if p:IsA("BasePart") and p.Name:lower() == "head" then
					morphReplacesHead = true
					break
				end
			end
			if morphReplacesHead then
				local realHead = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
				if realHead then
					extraHide[#extraHide + 1] = realHead
					for _, d in ipairs(realHead:GetChildren()) do
						if d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture") then
							extraHide[#extraHide + 1] = d
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		-- Hide the real body ONLY for morphs that truly replace it (e.g.
		if hideBody and morphWantsHide and not MORPH_NOHIDE[morphName] then
			startHide(originalBits)
		elseif #extraHide > 0 then
			startHide(extraHide)
		else
			stopHide()
		end

		-- Skin tone: recolor the real body limbs to the morph's skin so exposed arms/legs/hands match instead of showing the player's own pale skin.
		local skin = MORPH_SKIN[(tostring(morphName):gsub("%s*%(%d+%)%s*$", ""))]
		if skin then
			for _, name in ipairs(SKIN_LIMBS) do
				local part = char:FindFirstChild(name)
				if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
					if part:GetAttribute("LumaMorphSkin") == nil then
						part:SetAttribute("LumaMorphSkin", part.Color)
					end
					part.Color = skin
				end
			end

			-- The FACE mesh isn't in SKIN_LIMBS: recolor it to skin so the face isn't left white, and clear its Deepwoken face TextureID so the old eyes/skin stop bleeding through the morph face's transparent parts.
			local skinHead = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
			local faceMount = skinHead and skinHead:FindFirstChild("FaceMount")
			if faceMount and faceMount:IsA("MeshPart") then
				if faceMount:GetAttribute("LumaMorphSkin") == nil then
					faceMount:SetAttribute("LumaMorphSkin", faceMount.Color)
				end
				faceMount.Color = skin
				if faceMount:GetAttribute("LumaMorphFaceTex") == nil then
					faceMount:SetAttribute("LumaMorphFaceTex", faceMount.TextureID)
				end
				faceMount.TextureID = ""
			end
		end

		-- Corrective lift for driven sub-parts that hang too low.
		local subLift = MORPH_SUBPART_LIFT[base]
		if subLift then
			for _, j in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
				if (j:IsA("Motor6D") or j:IsA("Weld")) and j.Part1 and subLift[j.Part1.Name] then
					j.C0 = CFrame.new(0, subLift[j.Part1.Name], 0) * j.C0
				end
			end
		end

		local holder = char:FindFirstChild(HOLDER_NAME)
		if not holder then
			holder = Instance.new("Folder")
			holder.Name = HOLDER_NAME
			holder.Parent = char
		end
		model.Parent = holder
		return true
	end

	local function loadMorph(category, fileName)
		if not fileName or fileName == "" then
			return Logger.notify("Pick a morph from the dropdown first.")
		end
		if type(readCustomAsset) ~= "function" then
			return Logger.notify("Executor has no getcustomasset — can't load local models.")
		end
		local path = (FOLDERS[category] or FOLDERS.Races) .. "/" .. fileName .. ".rbxm"
		local ok, asset = pcall(readCustomAsset, path)
		if not ok or not asset then
			return Logger.notify("getcustomasset failed: " .. path)
		end
		local ok2, objs = pcall(function() return game:GetObjects(asset) end)
		if not ok2 or type(objs) ~= "table" or not objs[1] then
			return Logger.notify("Failed to deserialize: " .. tostring(objs))
		end
		local model = objs[1]
		if not model:IsA("Model") then
			local m = Instance.new("Model")
			model.Parent = m
			model = m
		end


		-- Force the morph's meshes/textures to download BEFORE we place anything.
		pcall(function()
			local cp = game:GetService("ContentProvider")
			local assets = { model }
			for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
				if d:IsA("MeshPart") or d:IsA("SpecialMesh") or d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture") then
					assets[#assets + 1] = d
				end
			end
			cp:PreloadAsync(assets)
		end)

		clearMorphs() -- replace any previously applied morph
		local okA, err = applyModel(model, hideBodyEnabled, fileName)
		if okA then
			lastApplied = { category = category, file = fileName }

		else
			if model then model:Destroy() end
			Logger.notify("Apply error: " .. tostring(err))
		end
	end

	-- ===== UI =====
	local catDropdown = groupbox:AddDropdown("LumaMorph_Category", {
		Text = "Category",
		Values = { "Races", "Ornaments" },
		AllowNull = false,
		Default = "Races",
	})

	local morphDropdown = groupbox:AddDropdown("LumaMorph_File", {
		Text = "Morph",
		Values = listMorphs(FOLDERS[currentCategory]),
		AllowNull = true,
		Default = nil,
	})

	catDropdown:OnChanged(function(v)
		currentCategory = v
		morphDropdown:SetValues(listMorphs(FOLDERS[v]))
		morphDropdown:Display()
	end)

	morphDropdown:OnChanged(function(v)
		currentFile = v
	end)

	groupbox:AddButton("Load Morph", function()
		loadMorph(currentCategory, currentFile)
	end)

	groupbox:AddButton("Clear Morph", function()
		clearMorphs()
		lastApplied = nil
		Logger.notify("Cleared applied morphs.")
	end)

	groupbox:AddButton("Refresh List", function()
		morphDropdown:SetValues(listMorphs(FOLDERS[currentCategory]))
		morphDropdown:Display()
	end)

	hideToggle = groupbox:AddToggle("LumaMorph_HideBody", {
		Text = "Hide real body (for HideBody morphs)",
		Default = true,
	})
	hideToggle:OnChanged(function(v)
		hideBodyEnabled = v
	end)

	local reapplyToggle = groupbox:AddToggle("LumaMorph_Reapply", {
		Text = "Reapply on respawn",
		Default = false,
	})
	reapplyToggle:OnChanged(function(v)
		reapplyEnabled = v
	end)

	lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
		if reapplyEnabled and lastApplied then
			task.wait(1.5)
			loadMorph(lastApplied.category, lastApplied.file)
		end
	end)
end

---@param window table
---@param groupbox table
function VisualsTab.initProximitySection(groupbox)
	-- On-screen list of nearby players (name + distance), ported from project-rain's proximity list.
	-- NOTE: distinct flags from GameTab's "PlayerProximity" NOTIFICATION feature -- reusing those collided.
	groupbox:AddToggle("ProximityList", {
		Text = "Player Proximity List",
		Tooltip = "Show an on-screen list of nearby players with their distance (updates live).",
		Default = false,
	})

	local depBox = groupbox:AddDependencyBox()

	depBox:AddSlider("ProximityListRange", {
		Text = "Range",
		Min = 100,
		Max = 5000,
		Default = 1000,
		Rounding = 0,
		Suffix = " studs",
	})

	depBox:AddToggle("ProximityListNotify", {
		Text = "Notify On Enter / Leave",
		Default = true,
	})

	depBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ Toggles.ProximityList, true },
	})
end

---@param groupbox table
function VisualsTab.initTargetHUDSection(groupbox)
	groupbox:AddToggle("TargetHUD", {
		Text = "Target HUD",
		Tooltip = "Show a draggable panel with the current target's name, level, distance, health, posture, blood and sanity.",
		Default = false,
	})

	groupbox:AddLabel("Drag the panel to reposition it.")
end

function VisualsTab.init(window)
	local tab = window:AddTab("ESP", "Visuals", Icons.Visuals)

	VisualsTab.initESPCustomization(tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("ESP Customization"))
	VisualsTab.initESPOptimizations(tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("ESP Optimizations"))
	VisualsTab.initProximitySection(tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Player Proximity"))
	VisualsTab.initTargetHUDSection(tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Target HUD"))

	VisualsTab.addPlayerESP(
		VisualsTab.addEntityESP(VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("Player", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Player ESP")))
	)

	VisualsTab.addFilterESP(
		VisualsTab.addEntityESP(VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("Mob", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Mob ESP")))
	)

	VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("NPC", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("NPC ESP"))
	VisualsTab.addChestESP(VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("Chest", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Chest ESP")))
	VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("BagDrop", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Bag ESP"))
	VisualsTab.addFilterESP(VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("AreaMarker", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Area Marker ESP")))
	VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("VOIBoundaryESP", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("VOI Boundary ESP"))
	VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("VOIWeaponESP", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("VOI Weapon ESP"))
	VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("ShopESP", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Shop ESP"))
	VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("JobBoard", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Job Board ESP"))
	VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("Artifact", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Artifact ESP"))
	VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("WindrunnerOrb", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Windrunner Orb ESP"))
	VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("Whirlpool", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Whirlpool ESP"))
	VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("ExplosiveBarrel", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Explosive Barrel ESP"))
	VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("MinistryCacheIndicator", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Ministry Cache ESP"))
	VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("OwlFeathers", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Owl Feathers ESP"))
	VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("GuildDoor", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Guild Door ESP"))
	VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("GuildBanner", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Guild Banner ESP"))
	VisualsTab.addObeliskESP(VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("Obelisk", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Obelisk ESP")))
	VisualsTab.addBoneAltarESP(VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("BoneAltar", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Bone Altar ESP")))
	VisualsTab.addFilterESP(VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("Ingredient", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Ingredient ESP")))
	VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("BoneSpear", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Bone Spear ESP"))
	VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("ArmorBrick", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Armor Brick ESP"))
	VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("BellMeteor", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Bell Meteor ESP"))
	VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("RareObelisk", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Rare Obelisk ESP"))
	VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("HealBrick", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Heal Brick ESP"))
	VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("MantraObelisk", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Mantra Obelisk ESP"))
	VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("BRWeapon", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("BR Weapon ESP"))
	VisualsTab.initBaseESPSection("BellKey", tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Bell Key ESP"))
end

return VisualsTab
end)

		NoUI = true,
		Text = "Offset Up (1500)",
		Callback = function()
			local localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer
			local character = localPlayer and localPlayer.Character
			if not character then
				return
			end

			character:TranslateBy(Vector3.new(0, 1500, 0))
		end,
	})

	local ttbToggle = groupbox:AddToggle("TweenToBack", {

		Max = 30,
		Suffix = "studs",
		Rounding = 0,
	})

	ttbDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ Toggles.TweenToBack, true },
	})

	local infJumpToggle = groupbox:AddToggle("InfiniteJump", {
		Text = "Infinite Jump",
		Default = false,
	})

	infJumpToggle:AddKeyPicker(
		"InfiniteJumpKeybind",
		{ Default = "N/A", SyncToggleState = true, Text = "Infinite Jump" }
	)

	local infiniteJumpDepBox = groupbox:AddDependencyBox()

	infiniteJumpDepBox:AddSlider("InfiniteJumpBoost", {
		Text = "Infinite Jump Boost",
		Default = 50,
		Min = 0,
		Max = 500,
		Suffix = "/s",
		Rounding = 0,
	})

	infiniteJumpDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ Toggles.InfiniteJump, true },
	})

	groupbox:AddToggle("AutoSprint", {
		Text = "Auto Sprint",
		Default = false,
	})

	local asDepBox = groupbox:AddDependencyBox()

	asDepBox:AddToggle("AutoSprintOnCrouch", {
		Text = "Auto Sprint On Crouch",
		Default = false,
	})

	asDepBox:AddToggle("AutoSprintDelay", {
		Text = "Auto Sprint Delay",
		Default = false,
	})

	local asdDepBox = asDepBox:AddDependencyBox()

	asdDepBox:AddSlider("AutoSprintDelayTime", {
		Text = "Auto Sprint Delay Time",
		Default = 0.2,
		Min = 0,
		Max = 5,
		Suffix = "s",
		Rounding = 2,
	})

	asdDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ Toggles.AutoSprintDelay, true },
	})

	asDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ Toggles.AutoSprint, true },
	})

	groupbox:AddButton({
		Text = "Respawn Character",
		DoubleClick = true,
		Func = function()
			local character = players.LocalPlayer.Character
			if not character then
				return
			end

			local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not humanoidRootPart then
				return
			end

			character:PivotTo(humanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 10000000, 0))
		end,
	})

	local function dealSelfFallDamage()
		local remote = KeyHandling.getRemote("FallDamage")
		if not remote then
			return Logger.notify("Failed to find fall damage remote.")
		end

		remote:FireServer(Configuration.expectOptionValue("FallDamageAmount") or 0.0, false)
	end

	groupbox:AddButton({
		Text = "Deal Fall Damage To Self",
		DoubleClick = true,
		Func = dealSelfFallDamage,
	})

	local dealFallDamageLabel = groupbox:AddLabel("Deal Fall Damage Keybind")

	dealFallDamageLabel:AddKeyPicker("DealFallDamageKeybind", {
		Default = "N/A",
		Mode = "Toggle",
		NoUI = true,
		Text = "Deal Fall Damage To Self",
		Callback = function()
			dealSelfFallDamage()
		end,
	})

	groupbox:AddSlider("FallDamageAmount", {
		Text = "Fall Damage Amount",
		Default = 50,
		Min = 0,
		Max = 1000,
		Suffix = "hp",
		Rounding = 0,
	})
end

---@param groupbox table
function GameTab.initPlayerMonitoringSection(groupbox)
	groupbox:AddToggle("NotifyMod", {
		Text = "Mod Notifications",
		Default = true,
	})

	local nmDepBox = groupbox:AddDependencyBox()

	nmDepBox:AddToggle("NotifyModSound", {
		Text = "Mod Notification Sound",
		Default = false,
	})

	local nmbDepBox = nmDepBox:AddDependencyBox()

	nmbDepBox:AddSlider("NotifyModSoundVolume", {
		Text = "Sound Volume",
		Default = 10,
		Min = 0,
		Max = 20,
		Suffix = "v",
		Rounding = 2,
	})

	nmbDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ Toggles.NotifyModSound, true },
	})

	nmDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ Toggles.NotifyMod, true },
	})

	groupbox:AddToggle("NotifyVoidWalker", {
		Text = "Void Walker Notifications",
		Default = false,
	})

	local niToggle = groupbox:AddToggle("NotifyItems", {
		Text = "Item Notifications",
		Default = false,
	})

	local niDepBox = groupbox:AddDependencyBox()

	local notifyItemsList = niDepBox:AddDropdown("NotifyItemsList", {
		Text = "Item List",
		Default = {},
		SaveValues = true,
		Multi = true,
		Values = {},
	})

	local itemLabel = niDepBox:AddInput("NotifyItemsLabel", {
		Text = "Item Name",
		Placeholder = "Partial or exact item name.",
	})

	niDepBox:AddButton("Add Name To Filter", function()
		local itemLabelValue = itemLabel.Value

		if #itemLabelValue <= 0 then
			return Logger.notify("Please enter a valid item name.")
		end

		local notifyItemsListValues = notifyItemsList.Values

		if not table.find(notifyItemsListValues, itemLabelValue) then
			table.insert(notifyItemsListValues, itemLabelValue)
		end

		notifyItemsList:SetValues(notifyItemsListValues)
		notifyItemsList:SetValue({})
		notifyItemsList:Display()
	end)

	niDepBox:AddButton("Remove Selected Names", function()
		local notifyItemsListValues = notifyItemsList.Values
		local selectedNotifyItems = notifyItemsList.Value

		for selectedNotifyItem, _ in next, selectedNotifyItems do
			local selectedIndex = table.find(notifyItemsListValues, selectedNotifyItem)
			if not selectedIndex then
				return Logger.notify("The selected item name %s does not exist in the list", selectedNotifyItem)
			end

			table.remove(notifyItemsListValues, selectedIndex)
		end

		notifyItemsList:SetValues(notifyItemsListValues)
		notifyItemsList:SetValue({})
		notifyItemsList:Display()
	end)

	niDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ niToggle, true },
	})

	groupbox:AddToggle("PlayerSpectating", {
		Text = "Player List Spectating",
		Default = false,
	})

	groupbox:AddToggle("BuildStealer", {
		Text = "Build Stealer",
		Default = false,
	})

	groupbox:AddToggle("HairStealer", {
		Text = "Hair Stealer",
		Default = false,
	})

	groupbox:AddToggle("ShowHiddenPlayers", {
		Text = "Show Hidden Players",
		Default = false,
	})

	groupbox:AddToggle("ShowRobloxChat", {
		Text = "Show Roblox Chat",
		Default = false,
	})

	groupbox:AddToggle("ShowOwnership", {
		Text = "Show Network Ownership",
		Default = false,
	})

	groupbox:AddToggle("PlayerProximity", {
		Text = "Player Proximity Notifications",
		Default = false,
	})

	local ppDepBox = groupbox:AddDependencyBox()

	ppDepBox:AddSlider("PlayerProximityRange", {
		Text = "Player Proximity Distance",
		Default = 1000,
		Min = 50,
		Max = 2500,
		Suffix = "studs",
		Rounding = 0,
	})

	ppDepBox:AddToggle("PlayerProximityVW", {
		Text = "Only Allow Voidwalkers",
		Default = false,
	})

	ppDepBox:AddToggle("PlayerProximityBeep", {
		Text = "Play Beep Sound",
		Default = false,
	})

	local ppbDepBox = ppDepBox:AddDependencyBox()

	ppbDepBox:AddSlider("PlayerProximityBeepVolume", {
		Text = "Beep Sound Volume",
		Default = 0.1,
		Min = 0,
		Max = 10,
		Suffix = "v",
		Rounding = 2,
	})

	ppbDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ Toggles.PlayerProximityBeep, true },
	})

	ppDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ Toggles.PlayerProximity, true },
	})

	local freecamToggle = groupbox:AddToggle("Freecam", {
		Text = "Freecam",
		Default = false,
	})

	freecamToggle:AddKeyPicker("FreecamKeybind", { Default = "N/A", SyncToggleState = true, Text = "Freecam" })

	local freecamDepBox = groupbox:AddDependencyBox()

	freecamDepBox:AddSlider("FreecamSpeed", {
		Text = "Freecam Speed",
		Default = 60,
		Min = 5,
		Max = 400,
		Suffix = "/s",
		Rounding = 0,
	})

	freecamDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ Toggles.Freecam, true },
	})

	groupbox:AddToggle("InfiniteZoom", {
		Text = "Infinite Zoom Out",
		Default = false,
	}):AddKeyPicker("InfiniteZoomKeybind", { Default = "N/A", SyncToggleState = true, Text = "Infinite Zoom Out" })
end

---@param groupbox table
function GameTab.initInfoSpoofingSection(groupbox)
	local hasEverBeenEnabled = true

	groupbox:AddToggle("InfoSpoofing", {
		Text = "Enable Info Spoofing",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(value)
			if value or hasEverBeenEnabled then
				Spoofing.rics()
			end

			if not value then
				return
			end

			Spoofing.sss(Configuration.expectOptionValue("SpoofedSlotString"))
			Spoofing.sds(Configuration.expectOptionValue("SpoofedDateString"))
			Spoofing.sgv(Configuration.expectOptionValue("SpoofedGameVersion"))

			hasEverBeenEnabled = true
		end,
	})

	local isDepBox = groupbox:AddDependencyBox()

	isDepBox:AddToggle("SpoofOtherPlayers", {
		Text = "Spoof Other Players",
		Default = false,
		Callback = Spoofing.rics,
	})

	isDepBox:AddToggle("HideDeathInformation", {
		Text = "Hide Death Information",
		Default = false,
	})

	isDepBox:AddInput("SpoofedSlotString", {
		Text = "Spoofed Slot String",
		Default = "1234567890:Z|1 [Lv. 1]",
		Finished = true,
		Callback = Spoofing.sss,
	})

	isDepBox:AddInput("SpoofedDateString", {
		Text = "Spoofed Date String",
		Default = "Linoria, 1970 CE",
		Finished = true,
		Callback = Spoofing.sds,
	})

	isDepBox:AddInput("SpoofedGameVersion", {
		Text = "Spoofed Game Version",
		Default = "pv_JAN_01_00:00z",
		Finished = true,
		Callback = Spoofing.sgv,
	})

	local function refreshHandler()
		if not Configuration.expectToggleValue("InfoSpoofing") then
			return
		end

		Spoofing.rics()
	end

	isDepBox:AddInput("SpoofedFirstName", {
		Text = "Spoofed First Name",
		Default = "W Spoof",
		Finished = true,
		Callback = refreshHandler,
	})

	isDepBox:AddInput("SpoofedLastName", {
		Text = "Spoofed Last Name",
		Default = "On Top",
		Finished = true,
		Callback = refreshHandler,
	})

	isDepBox:AddInput("SpoofedGuildName", {
		Text = "Spoofed Guild Name",
		Default = "discord.gg/lyc",
		Finished = true,
		Callback = refreshHandler,
	})

	isDepBox:AddInput("SpoofedServerName", {
		Text = "Spoofed Server Name",
		Default = "W Spoof",
		Finished = true,
		Callback = refreshHandler,
	})

	isDepBox:AddInput("SpoofedServerRegion", {
		Text = "Spoofed Server Region",
		Default = "discord.gg/lyc",
		Finished = true,
		Callback = refreshHandler,
	})

	isDepBox:AddInput("SpoofedServerAge", {
		Text = "Spoofed Server Age",
		Default = "???",
		Finished = true,
		Callback = refreshHandler,
	})

	isDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ Toggles.InfoSpoofing, true },
	})
end

__bundle_register("Features/Game/FakeVoid", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
local FakeVoid = {}

local Signal = require("Utility/Signal")

local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

local Configuration = require("Utility/Configuration")

local Maid = require("Utility/Maid")

local runService = game:GetService("RunService")
local players = game:GetService("Players")

local fakeVoidMaid = Maid.new()

local preSimulation = Signal.new(runService.PreSimulation)
local workspaceDescendantAdded = Signal.new(workspace.DescendantAdded)
local workspaceDescendantRemoving = Signal.new(workspace.DescendantRemoving)

local TRIGGER_HEIGHT = 100
local DEPTHS_ROOF_Y = -600
local DEPTHS_RAY_LENGTH = 1000
local ROOF_OFFSET = 3
local TELEPORT_COOLDOWN = 1.0

local killPlanes = {}

local lastTeleport = 0
local initialized = false

local function isKillPlane(instance)
	return instance:IsA("BasePart") and (instance.Name == "KillPlane" or instance.Name:match("Chasm") ~= nil)
end

local function getBounds(part)
	local cframe = part.CFrame
	if cframe.Y ~= cframe.Y or math.abs(cframe.Y) == math.huge then
		return nil
	end

	local size = part.Size
	local right, up, look = cframe.RightVector, cframe.UpVector, cframe.LookVector
	local extents = Vector3.new(
		math.abs(right.X) * size.X + math.abs(up.X) * size.Y + math.abs(look.X) * size.Z,
		math.abs(right.Y) * size.X + math.abs(up.Y) * size.Y + math.abs(look.Y) * size.Z,
		math.abs(right.Z) * size.X + math.abs(up.Z) * size.Y + math.abs(look.Z) * size.Z
	) / 2

	return { min = cframe.Position - extents, max = cframe.Position + extents }
end

local function onWorkspaceDescendantAdded(descendant)
	if not isKillPlane(descendant) then
		return
	end

	killPlanes[descendant] = getBounds(descendant) or false
end

local function onWorkspaceDescendantRemoving(descendant)
	killPlanes[descendant] = nil
end

local function getKillPlaneY(position)
	local fpdh = workspace:GetAttribute("OriginalFPDH") or workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
	local best = (typeof(fpdh) == "number" and fpdh == fpdh) and fpdh or nil

	for part, cached in next, killPlanes do
		local data = getBounds(part) or cached
		if not data then
			continue
		end

		killPlanes[part] = data

		if position.X < data.min.X or position.X > data.max.X then
			continue
		end

		if position.Z < data.min.Z or position.Z > data.max.Z then
			continue
		end

		if not best or data.max.Y > best then
			best = data.max.Y
		end
	end

	return best
end

local function getDepthsRoofY(position)
	local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
	if not map then
		return DEPTHS_ROOF_Y
	end

	local params = RaycastParams.new()
	params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { map }
	params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include

	local result = workspace:Raycast(
		Vector3.new(position.X, DEPTHS_ROOF_Y, position.Z),
		Vector3.new(0, -DEPTHS_RAY_LENGTH, 0),
		params
	)

	return result and result.Position.Y + ROOF_OFFSET or DEPTHS_ROOF_Y
end

local function updateFakeVoid()
	if not Configuration.expectToggleValue("FakeVoid") then
		return
	end

	if os.clock() - lastTeleport <= TELEPORT_COOLDOWN then
		return
	end

	local character = players.LocalPlayer and players.LocalPlayer.Character
	local rootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not rootPart then
		return
	end

	local position = rootPart.Position

	local killPlaneY = getKillPlaneY(position)

	if not killPlaneY or position.Y - killPlaneY > TRIGGER_HEIGHT then
		return
	end

	local targetY = getDepthsRoofY(position)
	if targetY - killPlaneY <= TRIGGER_HEIGHT then
		return
	end

	lastTeleport = os.clock()

	Logger.warn(
		"(FakeVoid) Teleporting from Y %.1f to Y %.1f (kill Y %.1f).",
		position.Y,
		targetY,
		killPlaneY
	)

	rootPart.CFrame = rootPart.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, targetY - position.Y, 0)
	rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)
end

function FakeVoid.init()
	if initialized then
		return
	end
	initialized = true

	for _, descendant in next, workspace:GetDescendants() do
		onWorkspaceDescendantAdded(descendant)
	end

	fakeVoidMaid:add(
		workspaceDescendantAdded:connect("FakeVoid_WorkspaceDescendantAdded", onWorkspaceDescendantAdded)
	)
	fakeVoidMaid:add(
		workspaceDescendantRemoving:connect("FakeVoid_WorkspaceDescendantRemoving", onWorkspaceDescendantRemoving)
	)
	fakeVoidMaid:add(preSimulation:connect("FakeVoid_PreSimulation", updateFakeVoid))

	if not LPH_OBFUSCATED or shared.Luma.verbose then
		local count = 0

		for _ in next, killPlanes do
			count = count + 1
		end

		Logger.warn("FakeVoid initialized with %d kill planes.", count)
	end
end

function FakeVoid.detach()
	-- Guard: a startup toggle callback (config load firing OnChanged(false) before FakeVoid was ever
	-- started) must NOT run a spurious detach. The ungated Logger.warn below leaked the "[Luma Recode]"
	-- fingerprint into LogService for release users (where every other log is gated off) and got them
	-- flagged by the anticheat's log scan.
	if not initialized then
		return
	end
	initialized = false

	fakeVoidMaid:clean()
	table.clear(killPlanes)

	if not LPH_OBFUSCATED or (shared.Luma and shared.Luma.verbose) then
		Logger.warn("FakeVoid detached.")
	end
end

return FakeVoid
end)
__bundle_register("Menu/LumaTab", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
local LumaTab = {}

---@module GUI.Icons
local Icons = require("GUI/Icons")

---@module GUI.ThemeManager
local ThemeManager = require("GUI/ThemeManager")

---@module GUI.SaveManager
local SaveManager = require("GUI/SaveManager")

---@module GUI.Library
local Library = require("GUI/Linoria")

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.SpotifyClient
local SpotifyClient = require("Utility/SpotifyClient")

---@module GUI.SpotifyWidget
local SpotifyWidget = require("GUI/SpotifyWidget")

---@param groupbox table
function LumaTab.initCheatSettingsSection(groupbox)
	groupbox:AddButton("Toggle Silent Mode", function()
		if not isfile or not delfile or not writefile then
			return
		end

		shared.Luma.silent = not shared.Luma.silent

		if not shared.Luma.silent then
			Logger.notify("Silent mode was disabled.")
		end

		if isfile("smarker.txt") then
			delfile("smarker.txt")
		else
			writefile(
				"smarker.txt",
				"Hello, if you're reading this, that means you have CM1 (Deepwoken) silent mode turned on. Deleting this file will turn it off."
			)
		end
	end)

	groupbox:AddButton("Toggle Player Scanning", function()
		if not isfile or not delfile or not writefile then
			return
		end

		shared.Luma.dpscanning = not shared.Luma.dpscanning

		if shared.Luma.dpscanning then
			Logger.notify("Player scanning was disabled.")
		else
			Logger.notify("Player scanning was enabled.")
		end

		if isfile("dpscanning.txt") then
			delfile("dpscanning.txt")
		else
			writefile(
				"dpscanning.txt",
				"Hello, if you're reading this, that means you have CM1 (Deepwoken) player scanning turned off. Deleting this file will turn it on."
			)
		end
	end)

	groupbox:AddButton("Toggle Bloxstrap RPC", function()
		if not isfile or not delfile or not writefile then
			return
		end

		shared.Luma.norpc = not shared.Luma.norpc

		if not shared.Luma.norpc then
			Logger.notify("Bloxstrap RPC was enabled.")
		else
			Logger.notify("Bloxstrap RPC was disabled.")
		end

		if isfile("norpc.txt") then
			delfile("norpc.txt")
		else
			writefile(
				"norpc.txt",
				"Hello, if you're reading this, that means you have CM1 (Deepwoken) Bloxstrap RPC turned off. Deleting this file will turn it on."
			)
		end
	end)

	groupbox:AddButton("Toggle Debug Logs", function()
		if not isfile or not delfile or not writefile then
			return
		end

		shared.Luma.verbose = not shared.Luma.verbose

		if shared.Luma.verbose then
			Logger.notify("Debug logs were enabled.")
		else
			Logger.notify("Debug logs were disabled.")
		end

		if isfile("verbose.txt") then
			delfile("verbose.txt")
		else
			writefile(
				"verbose.txt",
				"Hello, if you're reading this, that means you have CM1 (Deepwoken) debug logs turned on. Deleting this file will turn it off."
			)
		end
	end)

	groupbox:AddButton("Unload CM1", function()
		shared.Luma.detach()
	end)
end

function LumaTab.initSpotifySection(groupbox)
	groupbox:AddButton("Copy Auth URL", function()
		setclipboard(SpotifyClient.getAuthUrl())
		Logger.notify("Auth URL copied. Open it in a browser, log in, click Allow, then copy the URL it redirects to (even if the page fails to load).")
	end)

	local redirectUrlInput = groupbox:AddInput("SpotifyRedirectUrl", {
		Text = "Redirect URL",
		Placeholder = "Paste the full URL you were redirected to here.",
	})

	groupbox:AddButton("Authorize", function()
		local ok, err = SpotifyClient.completeAuth(redirectUrlInput.Value)
		if ok then
			Logger.notify("Spotify authorized successfully.")
		else
			Logger.notify("Spotify authorization failed: " .. tostring(err))
		end
	end)

	SpotifyWidget.init(Library)

	local showWidgetToggle = groupbox:AddToggle("ShowSpotifyWidget", {
		Text = "Show Spotify Widget",
		Default = false,
	})

	showWidgetToggle:OnChanged(function(state)
		SpotifyWidget.setVisible(state)
	end)
end

---@param groupbox table
function LumaTab.initUISettingsSection(groupbox)
	groupbox:AddSlider("NotificationScale", {
		Text = "Notification Scale",
		Min = 50,
		Max = 300,
		Default = 100,
		Rounding = 0,
		Suffix = "%",
	})

	groupbox:AddSlider("QuickNotificationSpeed", {
		Text = "Quick Notification Speed",
		Min = 0.1,
		Max = 2.0,
		Default = 0.5,
		Rounding = 2,
		Suffix = "s",
	})

	local menuBindLabel = groupbox:AddLabel("Menu Bind")

	menuBindLabel:AddKeyPicker("MenuKeybind", { Default = "LeftAlt", NoUI = true, Text = "Menu Keybind" })

	local keybindFrameLabel = groupbox:AddLabel("Keybind List Bind")

	keybindFrameLabel:AddKeyPicker("KeybindList", {
		Default = "N/A",
		Mode = "Toggle",
		NoUI = true,
		Text = "Keybind List",
		Callback = function(Value)
			Library.KeybindFrame.Visible = Value
		end,
	})

	-- The keybind list's KeyPicker:Update() reads Toggles.OnlyShowEnabledKeybinds to decide whether to hide
	-- bindings whose feature is currently off. YSL defines this toggle in its own settings module, which luma
	-- replaces -- so create it here and re-run every KeyPicker's Update() on change to refresh the list.
	local onlyEnabledKeybinds = groupbox:AddToggle("OnlyShowEnabledKeybinds", {
		Text = "Only Show Enabled Keybinds",
		Tooltip = "Hide keybinds from the on-screen list unless their feature is currently toggled on.",
		Default = false,
	})
	onlyEnabledKeybinds:OnChanged(function()
		for _, option in pairs(Options) do
			if option.Type == "KeyPicker" and option.Update then
				option:Update()
			end
		end
	end)

	local watermarkFrameLabel = groupbox:AddLabel("Watermark Bind")

	watermarkFrameLabel:AddKeyPicker("Watermark", {
		Default = "N/A",
		Mode = "Toggle",
		NoUI = true,
		Text = "Watermark",
		Callback = function(Value)
			Library:SetWatermarkVisibility(Value)
		end,
	})

	groupbox:AddToggle("HideUIInMenus", {
		Text = "Hide UI In Menus",
		Tooltip = "Hide the keybind list and watermark while the inventory or other in-game menus are open.",
		Default = false,
	})


end

function LumaTab.init(window)
	local tab = window:AddTab("Settings")

	local tabbox = tab:AddFullTabbox()

	LumaTab.initCheatSettingsSection(tabbox:AddTab("Cheat"))
	LumaTab.initUISettingsSection(tabbox:AddTab("Interface"))

	local debugTab = tabbox:AddTab("Debug")
	local CombatTab = require("Menu/CombatTab")
	local GameTab = require("Menu/GameTab")
	local AutomationTab = require("Menu/AutomationTab")
	if not LRM_UserNote then
		CombatTab.initDebuggingSection(debugTab)
	end
	GameTab.initDebuggingSection(debugTab)
	if not LRM_UserNote then
		AutomationTab.initDebuggingSection(debugTab)
	end

	ThemeManager:ApplyToGroupbox(tabbox:AddTab("Theme"))

	if not LPH_OBFUSCATED then
		LumaTab.initSpotifySection(tabbox:AddTab("Spotify"))
	end
end

return LumaTab
end)
__bundle_register("Menu/AutomationTab", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
local AutomationTab = {}

---@module GUI.Icons

local AutoAuthority = require("Features/Automation/AutoAuthority")

---@module Features.Automation.AutoDuke
local AutoDuke = require("Features/Automation/AutoDuke")

local AutoBuilderWindow = require("Menu/AutoBuilderWindow")

---@module GUI.SaveManager
local SaveManager = require("GUI/SaveManager")

---@module Features.Automation.Objects.AutoLog
local AutoLog = require("Features/Automation/Objects/AutoLog")

---Add a per-automation "Auto Log Distance" slider (0-2000; 0 = off) to a section. The value is saved per
---automation in the AutoLog store (NOT in luma config saves): ignored by SaveManager, loaded from disk as
---the slider's default, and written back on every change.
---@param groupbox table
---@param flag string unique Options key for the slider
---@param storeKey string automation id the distance is saved under
local function addAutoLogSlider(groupbox, flag, storeKey)
	local slider = groupbox:AddSlider(flag, {
		Text = "Auto Log Distance",
		Tooltip = "Server-hop when another player comes within this many studs (0 = off). Saved per automation, not in configs.",
		Min = 0,
		Max = 2000,
		Rounding = 0,
		Default = AutoLog.get(storeKey),
		Suffix = " studs",
	})
	SaveManager:SetIgnoreIndexes({ flag })
	slider:OnChanged(function(value)
		AutoLog.set(storeKey, value)
	end)
	return slider
end

---Per-automation tween (movement) speed slider: every tween in that automation uses this value. Default
---100, range 50-300. Saved in configs like any normal option.
---@param groupbox table
---@param flag string unique Options key for the slider
local function addTweenSpeedSlider(groupbox, flag)
	return groupbox:AddSlider(flag, {
		Text = "Tween Speed",
		Tooltip = "Movement (tween) speed for this automation, in studs/second.",
		Default = 100,
		Min = 50,
		Max = 300,
		Rounding = 0,
		Suffix = " studs/s",
	})
end

---@param groupbox table
function AutomationTab.initDebuggingSection(groupbox)
	groupbox:AddButton("Start Deflect", function()
		QueuedBlocking.invoke(QueuedBlocking.BLOCK_TYPE_DEFLECT, "StartDeflect", nil)
	end)

	groupbox:AddButton("Start Deflect 0.1s", function()
		QueuedBlocking.invoke(QueuedBlocking.BLOCK_TYPE_DEFLECT, "StartDeflect", 0.1)
	end)

	groupbox:AddButton("Start Block 0.3s", function()
		QueuedBlocking.invoke(QueuedBlocking.BLOCK_TYPE_NORMAL, "StartBlock", 0.3)
	end)

	groupbox:AddButton("Start Block", function()
		QueuedBlocking.invoke(QueuedBlocking.BLOCK_TYPE_NORMAL, "StartBlock", nil)
	end)

	groupbox:AddButton("Stop All Block", function()
		QueuedBlocking.empty()
	end)

	groupbox:AddButton("Raw Start Block", function()
		local blockRemote = KeyHandling.getRemote("Block")
		if not blockRemote then
			return
		end

		blockRemote:FireServer()
	end)

	groupbox:AddButton("Raw Stop Block", function()
		local unblockRemote = KeyHandling.getRemote("Unblock")
		if not unblockRemote then
			return
		end

		unblockRemote:FireServer()
	end)

	groupbox:AddButton("Test Tween", function()
		local positions = {
			CFrame.new(2414.70, 442.28, -5595.58),
			CFrame.new(2424.78, 408.30, -5501.38),
			CFrame.new(2429.86, 408.30, -5495.80),
			CFrame.new(2507.99, 414.60, -5560.85),
			CFrame.new(600.33, 983.29, -7643.66),
			CFrame.new(669.22, 979.31, -7668.61),
			CFrame.new(2960.38, 404.16, -5604.14),
		}

		for idx, position in next, positions do
			Tweening.goal(string.format("TweenToTest_%i", idx), position, true)
		end
	end)

	groupbox:AddButton("Cancel Tween", function()
		for idx = 1, 7 do
			Tweening.stop(string.format("TweenToTest_%i", idx))
		end
	end)
end

---@param groupbox table
function AutomationTab.initAutoLootSection(groupbox)
	groupbox:AddToggle("AutoLoot", {
		Text = "Auto Loot",
		Tooltip = "Automatically loot items from choice prompts with filtering options.",
		Default = false,
	}):AddKeyPicker("AutoLootKeybind", {
		Default = "N/A",
		SyncToggleState = true,
		Text = "Auto Loot",
	})

	local autoLootDepBox = groupbox:AddDependencyBox()

	autoLootDepBox:AddToggle("AutoLootAll", {
		Text = "Loot All Items",
		Tooltip = "Loot all items from choice prompts. This will ignore filtering options.",
		Default = false,
	})

	local autoLootAllDepBox = autoLootDepBox:AddDependencyBox()

	autoLootAllDepBox:AddSlider("AutoLootStarsMin", {
		Text = "Minimum Stars",
		Tooltip = "The minimum number of stars an item must have to be looted.",
		Min = 0,
		Max = 4,
		Rounding = 0,
		Default = 0,
	})

	autoLootAllDepBox:AddSlider("AutoLootStarsMax", {
		Text = "Maximum Stars",
		Tooltip = "The maximum number of stars an item can have to be looted.",
		Min = 0,
		Max = 4,
		Rounding = 0,
		Default = 4,
	})

	local itemNameList = autoLootAllDepBox:AddDropdown("ItemNameList", {
		Text = "Item Name List",
		Values = {},
		SaveValues = true,
		Multi = true,
		AllowNull = true,
	})

	local itemNameInput = autoLootAllDepBox:AddInput("ItemNameInput", {
		Text = "Item Name Input",
		Placeholder = "Exact or partial names.",
	})

	autoLootAllDepBox:AddButton("Add Item Name To Filter", function()
		local itemName = itemNameInput.Value
		if #itemName <= 0 then
			return Logger.notify("Please enter a valid item name.")
		end

		local values = itemNameList.Values
		if not table.find(values, itemName) then
			table.insert(values, itemName)
		end

		itemNameList:SetValues(values)
		itemNameList:SetValue({})
		itemNameList:Display()
	end)

	autoLootAllDepBox:AddButton("Remove Selected Item Names", function()
		local values = itemNameList.Values
		local value = itemNameList.Value

		for selected, _ in next, value do
			local index = table.find(values, selected)
			if not index then
				continue
			end

			table.remove(values, index)
		end

		itemNameList:SetValues(values)
		itemNameList:SetValue({})
		itemNameList:Display()
	end)

	autoLootAllDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ Toggles.AutoLootAll, false },
	})

	autoLootDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ Toggles.AutoLoot, true },
	})
end

---@param groupbox table
function AutomationTab.initAutoBuilderSection(groupbox)
	-- The tab only holds the link box + load button. The loaded build's stats, talents and mantras
	-- (preshrine on the left, postshrine on the right) live in a separate window that Load opens.
	local linkInput = groupbox:AddInput("AutoBuilderLink", {
		Text = "Builder Link",
		Placeholder = "deepwoken.co/builder?id=...",
	})

	groupbox:AddButton("Load Build", function()
		local build = AutoBuilder.load(linkInput.Value)
		local classified = AutoBuilder.classified

		if build and classified then
			AutoBuilderWindow.show(build, classified)
		end
	end)

	-- A Mantra Hand always contains a special "Roll 2" card that grants 2 Talents instead of a Mantra.
	-- With this on, the builder always takes Roll 2 on mantra hands rather than picking a mantra.
	groupbox:AddToggle("AutoBuilderOnlyRoll2", {
		Text = "Only Roll 2 For Mantras",
		Default = false,
		Tooltip = "On a Mantra Hand, always take the 'Roll 2' card (2 Talents instead of a Mantra). Talent choices are unaffected.",
	})

	-- Start/Stop button. Reads "Start Preshrine"/"Start Postshrine" per the selected target (double-
	-- click to confirm), and flips to "Stop" once picking is running.
	local startButton
	local function startLabel()
		return AutoBuilder.target == "post" and "Start Postshrine" or "Start Preshrine"
	end

	local function applyState()
		if AutoBuilder.picking then
			startButton.Text = "Stop"
			startButton.Label.Text = "Stop"
			startButton.DoubleClick = false
			startButton.Func = AutoBuilder.stopPicking
		else
			local label = startLabel()
			startButton.Text = label
			startButton.Label.Text = label
			startButton.DoubleClick = true
			startButton.Func = AutoBuilder.startPicking
		end
	end

	startButton = groupbox:AddButton({
		Text = startLabel(),
		DoubleClick = true,
		Func = AutoBuilder.startPicking,
	})

	-- Keep the button in sync with target changes (from the window) and start/stop (incl. a hop
	-- resume that flips picking on without this button being pressed).
	AutoBuilder.onStateChanged = applyState

	-- Reflect any state that a server-hop resume may have already set before the menu was built.
	applyState()
end

---@param groupbox table
function AutomationTab.initAutoProgSection(groupbox)
	-- Like Auto Builder (a link box + Load that opens the preshrine/postshrine window), but the start
	-- buttons run the full progression: the builder invests points + picks talents toward the spread
	-- while the chosen farm earns the points. Shrine manually between preshrine and postshrine.
	local linkInput = groupbox:AddInput("AutoProgLink", {
		Text = "Builder Link",
		Placeholder = "deepwoken.co/builder?id=...",
	})

	groupbox:AddButton("Load Build", function()
		local build = AutoBuilder.load(linkInput.Value)
		local classified = AutoBuilder.classified

		if build and classified then
			AutoBuilderWindow.show(build, classified)
		end
	end)

	groupbox:AddDropdown("AutoProgFarmMethod", {
		Text = "Method",
		Values = { "Ferryman", "Saramed" },
		Default = "Ferryman",
	})

	groupbox:AddButton({
		Text = "Start Preshrine",
		DoubleClick = true,
		Func = AutoProg.startPreshrine,
	})

	groupbox:AddButton({
		Text = "Start Postshrine",
		DoubleClick = true,
		Func = AutoProg.startPostshrine,
	})

	groupbox:AddButton({
		Text = "Stop",
		Func = AutoProg.stop,
	})
end

---@param groupbox table
function AutomationTab.initAutoTitusSection(groupbox)
	groupbox:AddButton({
		Text = "Start",
		DoubleClick = true,
		Func = function()
			-- start() self-guards on the merit hub / Titus dungeon and notifies if we're not there.
			AutoTitus.start()
		end,
	})

	addAutoLogSlider(groupbox, "TitusAutoLogDist", "auto_titus")
	addTweenSpeedSlider(groupbox, "TitusTweenSpeed")

	-- Relic names to bank when we hold >= 25 of them (leave empty to never bank).
	local bankList = groupbox:AddDropdown("TitusBankItems", {
		Text = "Bank Items (>=25)",
		-- Pre-populated with the real relic item names (exact Backpack Tool names, same list PR banks) so
		-- they always match countItem(). The old empty list forced you to hand-type a name, and any typo /
		-- wrong case / missing apostrophe silently never matched -- so it never banked. Use the input +
		-- "Add Bank Item" below only for relics outside this standard set.
		Values = { "Moonseye Tome", "Idol of Yun'Shul", "Armorer's Needle", "Smith's Alloy" },
		Default = { "Moonseye Tome", "Idol of Yun'Shul", "Armorer's Needle", "Smith's Alloy" },
		SaveValues = true,
		Multi = true,
		AllowNull = true,
	})

	local bankInput = groupbox:AddInput("TitusBankItemInput", {
		Text = "Bank Item Name",
		Placeholder = "Exact relic name.",
	})

	groupbox:AddButton("Add Bank Item", function()
		local itemName = bankInput.Value
		if #itemName <= 0 then
			return Logger.notify("Please enter a valid relic name.")
		end
		-- Snapshot the current selection so adding a custom relic doesn't wipe what's already ticked.
		local selected = {}
		for selectedName in next, bankList.Value do
			selected[selectedName] = true
		end

		local values = bankList.Values
		if not table.find(values, itemName) then
			table.insert(values, itemName)
		end
		bankList:SetValues(values)

		-- Keep the previous selection and also tick the item we just added.
		selected[itemName] = true
		bankList:SetValue(selected)
		bankList:Display()
	end)

	groupbox:AddButton("Remove Selected Bank Items", function()
		local values = bankList.Values
		for selected, _ in next, bankList.Value do
			local index = table.find(values, selected)
			if index then
				table.remove(values, index)
			end
		end
		bankList:SetValues(values)
		bankList:SetValue({})
		bankList:Display()
	end)
end

---@param groupbox table
function AutomationTab.initEchoFarmSection(groupbox)
	-- Titus echo farm (Fort Merit detainment). Start is a double-click: shows "Start", then
	-- "THIS WILL WIPE YOUR CURRENT CHARACTER" after the first click; clicking again confirms and starts.
	groupbox:AddButton({
		Text = "Start",
		DoubleClick = true,
		DoubleClickText = "THIS WILL WIPE YOUR CURRENT CHARACTER",
		Func = function()
			EchoFarm.invoke("Titus")
		end,
	})
	-- Distance governs the Titus farm's player-proximity server-hop (0 = never hop on nearby players).
	addAutoLogSlider(groupbox, "EchoFarmAutoLogDist", "echo_farm")
	addTweenSpeedSlider(groupbox, "EchoFarmTweenSpeed")
	-- Stop via the on-screen overlay's Stop button.
end

	AutomationTab.initAutoVoISection(tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Auto VoI"))
	AutomationTab.initAutoTitusSection(tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Auto Titus"))
	AutomationTab.initEchoFarmSection(tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Echo Farm"))
	AutomationTab.initAutoLayer2Section(tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Auto L2"))
	AutomationTab.initAutoAuthoritySection(tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Auto Authority"))
	AutomationTab.initAutoDukeSection(tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Auto Duke"))
	AutomationTab.initAutoMooneyrieSection(tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Auto Mooneyrie"))
	AutomationTab.initAutoNotesSection(tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Auto Notes"))
	AutomationTab.initAutoSaramedSection(tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Auto Saramed"))
	AutomationTab.initAutoBuilderSection(tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Auto Builder"))
	AutomationTab.initAutoProgSection(tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Auto Prog"))
	AutomationTab.initAutoLootSection(tab:AddDynamicGroupbox("Auto Loot"))
end

return AutomationTab
end)
__bundle_register("Menu/CombatTab", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
local CombatTab = {}

---@module GUI.Icons
local Icons = require("GUI/Icons")

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Features.Combat.Defense
local Defense = require("Features/Combat/Defense")

---@module Utility.Configuration
local Configuration = require("Utility/Configuration")

---@module Utility.Environment
local Environment = require("Utility/Environment")

---@module GUI.Library
local Library = require("GUI/Library")

---@module Game.Timings.SaveManager
local SaveManager = require("Game/Timings/SaveManager")

local RhythmSpoofer = require("Features/Combat/RhythmSpoofer")

local ForceAutoRotate = require("Features/Combat/ForceAutoRotate")

local AutoDustlunge = require("Features/Combat/AutoDustlunge")

-- Minimal modal confirm popup (luma's Library has no dialog primitive): dims the screen, shows a title +
-- message with Confirm / Cancel, and calls back with a boolean. Parented on top of Library.ScreenGui.
local function confirmDialog(titleText, bodyText, onResult)
	local backdrop = Library:Create("Frame", {
		Name = "LumaConfirmDialog",
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.45,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0),
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		ZIndex = 999990,
		Active = true,
		Parent = Library.ScreenGui,
	})

	local dialog = Library:Create("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(360, 160),
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.MainColor,
		BorderColor3 = Library.OutlineColor,
		BorderMode = Enum.BorderMode.Inset,
		ZIndex = 999991,
		Active = true,
		Parent = backdrop,
	})
	Library:AddToRegistry(dialog, { BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor", BorderColor3 = "OutlineColor" })

	Library:Create("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 12),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 22),
		Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
		Text = titleText,
		TextColor3 = Library.AccentColor,
		TextSize = 18,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
		ZIndex = 999992,
		Parent = dialog,
	})

	Library:Create("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 44),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 58),
		Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
		Text = bodyText,
		TextColor3 = Library.FontColor,
		TextSize = 15,
		TextWrapped = true,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
		TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
		ZIndex = 999992,
		Parent = dialog,
	})

	local done = false
	local function finish(result)
		if done then
			return
		end
		done = true
		pcall(function()
			backdrop:Destroy()
		end)
		Library:SafeCallback(onResult, result)
	end

	local function makeButton(text, xScale, isAccent)
		return Library:Create("TextButton", {
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1),
			Position = UDim2.new(xScale, 0, 1, -14),
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(150, 32),
			BackgroundColor3 = isAccent and Library.AccentColor or Library.MainColor,
			BorderColor3 = Library.OutlineColor,
			BorderMode = Enum.BorderMode.Inset,
			Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
			Text = text,
			TextColor3 = Library.FontColor,
			TextSize = 15,
			AutoButtonColor = true,
			ZIndex = 999993,
			Parent = dialog,
		})
	end

	makeButton("Confirm", 0.28, true).MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		finish(true)
	end)
	makeButton("Cancel", 0.72, false).MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		finish(false)
	end)
end

---@param groupbox table
function CombatTab.initCombatWhitelistSection(groupbox)
	groupbox:AddToggle("PlayerListWhitelisting", {
		Text = "Player List Whitelisting",
		Default = false,
	})

	local usernameList = groupbox:AddDropdown("UsernameList", {
		Text = "Username List",
		Values = {},
		SaveValues = true,
		Multi = true,
		AllowNull = true,
	})

	local usernameInput = groupbox:AddInput("UsernameInput", {
		Text = "Username Input",
		Placeholder = "Display name or username.",
	})
	groupbox:AddButton("Add Username To Whitelist", function()
		local username = usernameInput.Value
		if #username <= 0 then
			return Logger.longNotify("Please enter a valid username.")
		end

		local values = usernameList.Values
		if not table.find(values, username) then
			table.insert(values, username)
		end

		usernameList:SetValues(values)
		usernameList:SetValue({})
		usernameList:Display()
	end)

	groupbox:AddButton("Remove Selected Usernames", function()
		local values = usernameList.Values
		local value = usernameList.Value

		for selected, _ in next, value do
			local index = table.find(values, selected)
			if not index then
				continue
			end

			table.remove(values, index)
		end

		usernameList:SetValues(values)
		usernameList:SetValue({})
		usernameList:Display()

	end)
end
