---@param detectionBox table
---@param thresholdsBox table
function CombatTab.initAntiBreakerSection(detectionBox, thresholdsBox)
	local toggle = detectionBox:AddToggle("AntiBreakerEnabled", {
		Text = "Anti-AP Breaker",
		Default = false,
	})

	toggle:AddKeyPicker(
		"AntiBreakerKeybind",
		{ Default = "N/A", SyncToggleState = true, Text = "Anti-AP Breaker" }
	)

	-- Confirm before enabling: Anti-AP Breaker degrades your AP, so require explicit acceptance. We snap
	-- the toggle back off immediately and only re-enable it if the user picks Confirm.
	local programmatic = false
	local dialogOpen = false
	toggle:OnChanged(function(state)
		if programmatic or not state then
			return
		end
		programmatic = true
		toggle:SetValue(false)
		programmatic = false
		if dialogOpen then
			return
		end
		dialogOpen = true
		confirmDialog("Anti-AP Breaker", "THIS WILL MAKE AP SIGNIFICANTLY WORSE", function(confirmed)
			dialogOpen = false
			if confirmed then
				programmatic = true
				toggle:SetValue(true)
				programmatic = false
			end
		end)
	end)

	-- Notifies you when a nearby player is flagged as trying to AP-break you (ported from Mira).
	detectionBox:AddToggle("AntiBreakerNotify", {
		Text = "Ap Break Notifications",
		Default = false,
	})
end

---@param callback function
---@return function
local function timingOverrideCallback(callback)
	return function()
		local overrideData = Library.OverrideData
		if not overrideData then
			return false
		end

		local selectedTo = Configuration.expectOptionValue("TimingOverrideList")
		if not selectedTo or #selectedTo == 0 then
			return false
		end

		local override = overrideData[selectedTo]
		if not override then
			return false
		end

		callback(override)
	end
end

local function refreshTimingOverrideList()
	local overrideData = Library.OverrideData
	if not overrideData then
		return
	end

	local timingList = Options["TimingOverrideList"]
	if not timingList then
		return
	end

	local values = {}

	for rname, _ in next, overrideData do
		values[#values + 1] = rname
	end

	timingList:SetValues(values)
end

---@param groupbox table
function CombatTab:initTimingsSection(groupbox)
	self.tol = groupbox:AddDropdown("TimingOverrideList", {
		Text = "Timing Override List",
		Values = {},
		SaveValues = false,
		Multi = false,
		AllowNull = true,
		Searchable = true,
		Callback = function()
			self.dashInsteadOfParryRate:SetRawValue(0)
			self.failureRate:SetRawValue(0)
			self.ignoreAnimationEndRate:SetRawValue(0)
			self.reactToFeintsRate:SetRawValue(0)

			local overrideData = Library.OverrideData
			if not overrideData then
				return false
			end

			local selectedTo = Configuration.expectOptionValue("TimingOverrideList")
			if not selectedTo or #selectedTo == 0 then
				return false
			end

			local override = overrideData[selectedTo]
			if not override then
				return false
			end

			self.dashInsteadOfParryRate:SetRawValue(override.dipr or 0)
			self.failureRate:SetRawValue(override.fr or 0)
			self.ignoreAnimationEndRate:SetRawValue(override.iaer or 0)
			self.reactToFeintsRate:SetRawValue(override.rtfr or 0)
		end,
	})

	groupbox:AddDivider()

	local names = {}
	local plist = { SaveManager.as, SaveManager.es, SaveManager.ss, SaveManager.ps }

	---@todo: There should be a specific method for this; I mean as far as I'm aware THERE IS ONE. But, I'm too lazy.
	for _, pair in next, plist do
		for _, timing in next, pair:list() do
			names[#names + 1] = timing.name
		end
	end

	table.sort(names)

	self.ti = groupbox:AddDropdown("TimingOverrideTimingList", {
		Text = "Timing List",
		Values = names,
		Multi = false,
		AllowNull = true,
		Searchable = true,
	})

	groupbox:AddButton("Add Timing Override", function()
		if Library.OverrideData[self.ti.Value] then
			return Logger.notify("A timing override with that name already exists.")
		end

		local override = {
			fr = self.failureRate.Value,
			dipr = self.dashInsteadOfParryRate.Value,
			iaer = self.ignoreAnimationEndRate.Value,
			rtfr = self.reactToFeintsRate.Value,
		}

		Library.OverrideData[self.ti.Value] = override

		refreshTimingOverrideList()

		self.tol:SetValue(self.ti.Value)
		self.tol:Display()
	end)

	groupbox:AddButton("Remove Selected Override", function()
		Library.OverrideData[self.tol.Value] = nil

		refreshTimingOverrideList()

		self.tol:SetValue(nil)
		self.tol:Display()
	end)

	refreshTimingOverrideList()
end

---@param groupbox table
function CombatTab:initProbabilitiesSection(groupbox)
	self.failureRate = groupbox:AddSlider("TP_FailureRate", {
		Text = "Failure Rate",
		Min = 0,
		Max = 100,
		Default = 0,
		Suffix = "%",
		Rounding = 2,
		Callback = timingOverrideCallback(function(override)
			override.fr = self.failureRate.Value
		end),
	})

	self.dashInsteadOfParryRate = groupbox:AddSlider("TP_DashInsteadOfParryRate", {
		Text = "Dash Instead Of Parry Rate",
		Min = 0,
		Max = 100,
		Default = 0,
		Suffix = "%",
		Rounding = 2,
		Callback = timingOverrideCallback(function(override)
			override.dipr = self.dashInsteadOfParryRate.Value
		end),
	})

	self.ignoreAnimationEndRate = groupbox:AddSlider("TP_IgnoreAnimationEndRate", {
		Text = "Ignore Animation End Rate",
		Min = 0,
		Max = 100,
		Default = 0,
		Suffix = "%",
		Rounding = 2,
		Callback = timingOverrideCallback(function(override)
			override.iaer = self.ignoreAnimationEndRate.Value
		end),
	})

	self.reactToFeintsRate = groupbox:AddSlider("TP_ReactToFeintsRate", {
		Text = "React To Feints Rate",
		Min = 0,
		Max = 100,
		Default = 0,
		Suffix = "%",
		Rounding = 2,
		Callback = timingOverrideCallback(function(override)
			override.rtfr = self.reactToFeintsRate.Value
		end),
	})
end

		Text = "Greycast Probability",
		Default = 0,
		Min = 0,
		Max = 100,
		Suffix = "%",
		Rounding = 0,
	})

	autoMixupDepBox:AddSlider("AutoMixupFeintIntoMantraDelay", {
		Text = "Greycast Delay",
		Default = 0.08,
		Min = 0,
		Max = 0.5,
		Suffix = "s",
		Rounding = 3,
	})

	local autoMixupMantras = autoMixupDepBox:AddDropdown("AutoMixupMantras", {
		Text = "Mantras",
		Values = {},
		Default = {},
		Multi = true,
		AllowNull = true,
		SaveValues = true,
	})

	autoMixupDepBox:AddButton("Load Mantras", function()
		local player = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
		if not player then
			return
		end

		local backpack = player:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
		if not backpack then
			return
		end

		local mantras = {}
		for _, mantra in pairs(backpack:GetChildren()) do
			if mantra:IsA("Tool") and mantra.Name:find("Mantra:") and not mantra.Name:find("RecalledMantra:") then
				local defaultName = mantra:GetAttribute("DefaultName")
				if defaultName and not table.find(mantras, defaultName) then
					table.insert(mantras, defaultName)
				end
			end
		end

		autoMixupMantras:SetValues(mantras)
		autoMixupMantras:SetValue({})
		autoMixupMantras:Display()
	end)

	autoMixupDepBox:AddSlider("AutoMixupRollBehindProbability", {
		Text = "Roll Cancel Behind Back Probability",
		Default = 0,
		Min = 0,
		Max = 100,
		Suffix = "%",
		Rounding = 0,
	})

	autoMixupDepBox:AddSlider("AutoMixupRollBehindLockOnIntensity", {
		Text = "Lock On Intensity",
		Default = 50,
		Min = 0,
		Max = 100,
		Suffix = "%",
		Rounding = 0,
	})

	autoMixupDepBox:AddToggle("AutoMixupRollBehindPlayersOnly", {
		Text = "Roll Behind Players Only",
		Default = false,
	})

	autoMixupDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ autoMixupToggle, true },
	})
end

		Text = "Slide Cast Mantras",
		Default = false,
	})

	local slideCastMantrasDepBox = groupbox:AddDependencyBox()

	local slideCastMantrasList = slideCastMantrasDepBox:AddDropdown("SlideCastMantrasList", {
		Text = "Trigger Mantras",
		Values = {},
		Default = {},
		Multi = true,
		AllowNull = true,
		SaveValues = true,
	})

	slideCastMantrasDepBox:AddButton("Load Slidecast Mantras", function()
		local player = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
		if not player then
			return
		end

		local backpack = player:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
		if not backpack then
			return
		end

		local mantras = {}
		for _, mantra in pairs(backpack:GetChildren()) do
			if mantra:IsA("Tool") and mantra.Name:find("Mantra:") and not mantra.Name:find("RecalledMantra:") then
				local defaultName = mantra:GetAttribute("DefaultName")
				if defaultName and not table.find(mantras, defaultName) then
					table.insert(mantras, defaultName)
				end
			end
		end

		slideCastMantrasList:SetValues(mantras)
		slideCastMantrasList:SetValue({})
		slideCastMantrasList:Display()
	end)

	slideCastMantrasDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ slideCastMantrasToggle, true },
	})

	local autoAirCounterToggle = groupbox:AddToggle("AutoAirCounter", {
		Text = "Auto Air Counter",
		Default = false,
	})

	autoAirCounterToggle:AddKeyPicker("AutoAirCounterKeybind", { Default = "N/A", SyncToggleState = true, Text = "Auto Air Counter" })

	local autoAirCounterDepBox = groupbox:AddDependencyBox()

	autoAirCounterDepBox:AddToggle("AutoAirCounterAllyCheck", {
		Text = "Ally Check",
		Default = false,
	})

	autoAirCounterDepBox:AddToggle("AutoAirCounterOnGround", {
		Text = "Air Counter On Ground",
		Default = false,
	})

	autoAirCounterDepBox:AddSlider("AutoAirCounterRange", {
		Text = "Air Counter Range",
		Default = 60,
		Min = 1,
		Max = 100,
		Suffix = " studs",
		Rounding = 0,
	})

	autoAirCounterDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ autoAirCounterToggle, true },
	})

	local autoDustlungeToggle = groupbox:AddToggle("AutoDustlunge", {
		Text = "Auto Assassination",
		Default = false,
	})

	autoDustlungeToggle:AddKeyPicker("AutoDustlungeKeybind", {
		Default = "N/A",
		SyncToggleState = true,
		Text = "Auto Assassination",
	})

	autoDustlungeToggle:AddKeyPicker("AutoDustlungeHoldBind", {
		Default = "V",
		Mode = "Hold",
		Modes = { "Hold" },
		Text = "Auto Assassination Bind (hold)",
	})

	AutoDustlunge.setHoldKeyPicker(Options.AutoDustlungeHoldBind)

	autoDustlungeToggle:OnChanged(function(state)
		if state then
			AutoDustlunge.enable()
		else
			AutoDustlunge.disable()
		end
	end)

	local autoDustlungeDepBox = groupbox:AddDependencyBox()

	autoDustlungeDepBox:AddToggle("AutoDustlungeDebug", {
		Text = "Show Hitbox",
		Default = false,
	})

	autoDustlungeDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ autoDustlungeToggle, true },
	})
end

---@param groupbox table
function CombatTab.initApBreakerSection(groupbox)
	local ExpApBreaker = require("Features/Combat/ExpApBreaker")

	local apBreakerToggle = groupbox:AddToggle("ExpApBreaker", {
		Text = "AP Breaker",
		Default = false,
	})

	apBreakerToggle:AddKeyPicker("APBreakerKeybind", { Default = "N/A", SyncToggleState = true, Text = "AP Breaker" })

	apBreakerToggle:OnChanged(function(state)
		if not ExpApBreaker then
			return
		end
		if state then
			ExpApBreaker:Start()
		else
			ExpApBreaker:Stop()
		end
	end)

	local apbDepBox = groupbox:AddDependencyBox()

	apbDepBox:AddDropdown("ExpApBreakerModes", {
		Text = "Breakers",
		Values = {
			"Priority Mask",
			"Fade Time Flood",
			"Monster Flood",
			"Mutate",
		},
		Default = {},
		Multi = true,
		AllowNull = true,
		Callback = function()
			if ExpApBreaker then
				ExpApBreaker.refresh()
			end
		end,
	})

	apbDepBox:AddSlider("ExpApBreakerAggressive2Intensity", {
		Text = "Flood Intensity",
		Default = 200,
		Min = 1,
		Max = 200,
		Rounding = 0,
	})

	apbDepBox:AddToggle("ExpApBreakerMaskVisual", {
		Text = "Mask Visual",
		Default = true,
	})

	apbDepBox:AddToggle("ApBreakerDontHitAnimLimit", {
		Text = "Don't hit animation limit",
		Default = true,
	})

	apbDepBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ apBreakerToggle, true },
	})

	-- Blatant Flood: standalone, independent of the AP Breaker toggle above. Floods every known
	-- timing and blanks your real attack animation ids to nothing so the enemy can't parry them.
	local blatantFlood = groupbox:AddToggle("BlatantFlood", {
		Text = "Blatant Flood",
		Default = false,
	})

	blatantFlood:AddKeyPicker(
		"BlatantFloodKeybind",
		{ Default = "N/A", SyncToggleState = true, Text = "Blatant Flood" }
	)

	blatantFlood:OnChanged(function(state)
		if not ExpApBreaker then
			return
		end
		if state then
			ExpApBreaker:StartBlatantFlood()
		else
			ExpApBreaker:StopBlatantFlood()
		end
	end)

	local blatantFloodBox = groupbox:AddDependencyBox()

	-- Flood rate (plays/sec). Higher = harder to read but each play replicates to the server, so
	-- pushing this too high spikes YOUR OWN ping. 40-80 buries the real swing without flooding.
	blatantFloodBox:AddSlider("BlatantFloodRate", {
		Text = "Blatant Flood Rate",
		Default = 60,
		Min = 1,
		Max = 300,
		Rounding = 0,
		Suffix = "/s",
	})

	blatantFloodBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ blatantFlood, true },
	})
end

__bundle_register("Features/Combat/SilentAim", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
-- Silent Aim (ported from Project Rain).
--
-- Deepwoken resolves where your attacks/abilities go by asking the CLIENT two things:
--   1. Requests.GetCameraToMouse (RemoteFunction) -- "what CFrame is your camera->mouse aim?"
--   2. Requests.UpdateMouse (RemoteEvent, fired every frame) -- "where is your mouse pointing in world?"
-- We override (1) to hand back a CFrame aimed at the target under the FOV, and we fire (2) ourselves with
-- the target's predicted position. The game's own UpdateMouse is suppressed in Game/Hooking.lua's namecall
-- hook while this is on, so only our redirected position reaches the server.
--
-- When the toggle is off (or no target is in the FOV) the GetCameraToMouse override returns the exact same
-- value the stock client returns -- CFrame.lookAt(camera, real mouse hit) -- so it is behaviourally
-- identical to vanilla and safe to leave installed.

---@module Utility.Configuration
local Configuration = require("Utility/Configuration")

---@module Game.Latency
local Latency = require("Game/Latency")

---@module Game.PlayerScanning
local PlayerScanning = require("Game/PlayerScanning")

---@module GUI.Library
local Library = require("GUI/Library")

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")

local SilentAim = {}

local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local TARGET_REFRESH = 1 / 8

local running = false
local installed = false
local renderConn = nil
local characterConn = nil
local currentTarget = nil
local lastTargetRefresh = 0
local lastSentPosition = nil

local fovCircle = nil
local outlineCircle = nil

local cachedEffectModule = nil

---@return any
local function getEffectModule()
	if cachedEffectModule then
		return cachedEffectModule
	end
	local effectReplicator = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("EffectReplicator")
	cachedEffectModule = effectReplicator and require(effectReplicator) or nil
	return cachedEffectModule
end

---@return boolean
local function hasCameraLock()
	local module = getEffectModule()
	if not module then
		return false
	end
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return module:HasEffect("CameraLock")
	end)
	return (ok and result) and true or false
end

---Strip NaN out of numbers/Vectors/CFrames so a bad target position can't desync us or trip a kick.
---@param value any
---@return any
local function safeNAN(value)
	if value == value then
		return value
	end

	if typeof(value) == "Vector3" then
		return Vector3.new(safeNAN(value.X), safeNAN(value.Y), safeNAN(value.Z))
	elseif typeof(value) == "CFrame" then
		local position = safeNAN(value.Position)
		local rotation = value.Rotation
		if rotation ~= rotation then
			rotation = CFrame.identity
		end
		return CFrame.new(position) * rotation
	elseif typeof(value) == "number" then
		return value == value and value or 0
	end

	return value
end

---@param entity Model
---@return BasePart?
local function getPrimary(entity)
	return entity.PrimaryPart or entity:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

---Lead a target by one round-trip, scaled by the prediction slider.
---@param target Model
---@return Vector3?
local function predictedPosition(target)
	local primary = getPrimary(target)
	if not primary then
		return nil
	end
	local prediction = (Configuration.expectOptionValue("SilentAimPrediction") or 0) / 100
	local lead = primary.AssemblyLinearVelocity * (Latency.rtt() or 0) * prediction
	return primary.Position + lead
end

---The CFrame handed back to the server for GetCameraToMouse, aimed at the target.
---@param target Model
---@return CFrame?
local function getGCTM(target)
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	local aimPos = predictedPosition(target)
	if not camera or not aimPos then
		return nil
	end
	return safeNAN(CFrame.lookAt(camera.CFrame.Position, aimPos))
end

---Pick the entity in workspace.Live whose box falls inside the FOV circle around the cursor.
---@return Model?
local function getTarget()
	local live = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")
	if not live then
		return nil
	end

	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	local mouse = localPlayer and localPlayer:GetMouse()
	if not camera or not mouse then
		return nil
	end

	local targets = Configuration.expectOptionValue("SilentAimTargets") or {}
	local fovRadius = Configuration.expectOptionValue("SilentAimFOV") or 90

	local mouseX = mouse.X
	local mouseY = mouse.Y + GuiService:GetGuiInset().Y
	local character = localPlayer and localPlayer.Character

	for _, entity in next, live:GetChildren() do
		local isMob = entity.Name:sub(1, 1) == "."
		local isPlayer = not isMob

		if isMob and not targets.Mobs then
			continue
		end
		if isPlayer and not targets.Players then
			continue
		end
		if entity == character then
			continue
		end

		local primary = getPrimary(entity)
		if not primary then
			continue
		end

		if isPlayer then
			local player = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(entity)
			if player and PlayerScanning.isAlly(player) and not targets.Guildmates then
				continue
			end
		end

		-- 8 corners of a rough character box projected to screen; first corner inside the FOV wins.
		local size = Vector3.new(4, 5, 6)
		local base = primary.CFrame - (size / 2)
		local mins = base.Position
		local maxs = (base + size).Position

		local points = {
			Vector3.new(mins.X, mins.Y, mins.Z),
			Vector3.new(mins.X, maxs.Y, mins.Z),
			Vector3.new(maxs.X, maxs.Y, mins.Z),
			Vector3.new(maxs.X, mins.Y, mins.Z),
			Vector3.new(maxs.X, maxs.Y, maxs.Z),
			Vector3.new(mins.X, maxs.Y, maxs.Z),
			Vector3.new(mins.X, mins.Y, maxs.Z),
			Vector3.new(maxs.X, mins.Y, maxs.Z),
		}

		for _, point in next, points do
			local screenPoint, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(point)
			if
				onScreen
				and screenPoint.X > mouseX - fovRadius
				and screenPoint.X < mouseX + fovRadius
				and screenPoint.Y > mouseY - fovRadius
				and screenPoint.Y < mouseY + fovRadius
			then
				return entity
			end
		end
	end

	return nil
end

---Install (or re-install after a respawn) the GetCameraToMouse override. Transparent passthrough when the
---toggle is off or no target is found.
local function installCameraHook()
	local requests = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Requests")
	local getCameraToMouse = requests and requests:FindFirstChild("GetCameraToMouse")
	if not getCameraToMouse then
		return false
	end

	getCameraToMouse.OnClientInvoke = function()
		if Configuration.expectToggleValue("SilentAim") then
			local target = getTarget()
			if target then
				local cf = getGCTM(target)
				if cf then
					return cf, hasCameraLock()
				end
			end
		end

		-- Vanilla behaviour: aim at the real mouse hit (matches the stock client's handler exactly).
		local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		local mouse = localPlayer and localPlayer:GetMouse()
		local origin = camera and camera.CFrame.Position or Vector3.zero
		local hit = (mouse and mouse.Hit and mouse.Hit.Position) or origin
		return safeNAN(CFrame.lookAt(origin, hit)), hasCameraLock()
	end

	return true
end

local function ensureDrawings()
	if fovCircle then
		return
	end
	if not (Drawing and Drawing.new) then
		return
	end

	local ok = pcall(function()
		fovCircle = Drawing.new("Circle")
		fovCircle.Visible = false
		fovCircle.ZIndex = 2
		fovCircle.NumSides = 100
		fovCircle.Thickness = 1
		fovCircle.Filled = true
		fovCircle.Transparency = 0.3

		outlineCircle = Drawing.new("Circle")
		outlineCircle.Visible = false
		outlineCircle.ZIndex = 1
		outlineCircle.NumSides = 100
		outlineCircle.Thickness = 2
		outlineCircle.Filled = false
	end)

	if not ok then
		fovCircle = nil
		outlineCircle = nil
	end
end

local function destroyDrawings()
	for _, drawing in next, { fovCircle, outlineCircle } do
		pcall(function()
			drawing.Visible = false
			drawing:Remove()
		end)
	end
	fovCircle = nil
	outlineCircle = nil
end

local function onRender()
	local mouse = localPlayer and localPlayer:GetMouse()
	if not mouse then
		return
	end

	-- FOV circle (optional; only on executors with a Drawing library).
	ensureDrawings()
	if fovCircle and outlineCircle then
		local showFov = Configuration.expectToggleValue("SilentAimShowFOV")
		local radius = Configuration.expectOptionValue("SilentAimFOV") or 90
		local position = Vector2.new(mouse.X, mouse.Y + GuiService:GetGuiInset().Y)

		fovCircle.Visible = showFov
		outlineCircle.Visible = showFov
		fovCircle.Radius = radius
		outlineCircle.Radius = radius
		fovCircle.Filled = Configuration.expectToggleValue("SilentAimFillFOV")
		fovCircle.Transparency = 1 - (Configuration.expectOptionValue("SilentAimFOVTransparency") or 0.3)
		fovCircle.Position = position
		outlineCircle.Position = position
	end

	if tick() - lastTargetRefresh > TARGET_REFRESH then
		currentTarget = getTarget()
		lastTargetRefresh = tick()
	end

	local aimPos = mouse.Hit and mouse.Hit.Position
	local accent = Library.AccentColor or Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80)

	if currentTarget and getPrimary(currentTarget) then
		if fovCircle then
			fovCircle.Color = accent
		end
		aimPos = predictedPosition(currentTarget) or aimPos
	elseif fovCircle then
		fovCircle.Color = Library:GetDarkerColor(accent)
	end

	if not aimPos then
		return
	end

	-- Redirect UpdateMouse (the game's own fire is dropped by the namecall hook while Silent Aim is on).
	if not lastSentPosition or (aimPos - lastSentPosition).Magnitude > 0.1 then
		local requests = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Requests")
		local updateMouse = requests and requests:FindFirstChild("UpdateMouse")
		if updateMouse then
			local packet = buffer.create(24)
			buffer.writef64(packet, 0, aimPos.X)
			buffer.writef64(packet, 8, aimPos.Y)
			buffer.writef64(packet, 16, aimPos.Z)
			pcall(function()
				updateMouse:FireServer(packet)
			end)
		end
		lastSentPosition = aimPos
	end
end

function SilentAim.enable()
	if running then
		return
	end
	running = true
	currentTarget = nil
	lastSentPosition = nil

	-- Install the OnClientInvoke override once the character + Requests exist.
	task.spawn(function()
		local timeout = tick() + 30
		repeat
			task.wait()
		until (localPlayer.Character and ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Requests")) or tick() > timeout

		if not running then
			return
		end

		local ok = pcall(installCameraHook)
		installed = installed or ok
	end)

	-- The game re-sets GetCameraToMouse.OnClientInvoke on respawn, so re-install after each CharacterAdded.
	if characterConn then
		pcall(function()
			characterConn:Disconnect()
		end)
	end
	characterConn = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
		if not running then
			return
		end
		task.spawn(function()
			task.wait(1)
			if running then
				pcall(installCameraHook)
			end
		end)
	end)

	if renderConn then
		pcall(function()
			renderConn:Disconnect()
		end)
	end
	renderConn = RunService.PreRender:Connect(function()
		if not running then
			return
		end
		pcall(onRender)
	end)
end

function SilentAim.disable()
	running = false
	currentTarget = nil
	lastSentPosition = nil

	if renderConn then
		pcall(function()
			renderConn:Disconnect()
		end)
		renderConn = nil
	end
	if characterConn then
		pcall(function()
			characterConn:Disconnect()
		end)
		characterConn = nil
	end

	destroyDrawings()

	-- The GetCameraToMouse override stays installed but is a transparent passthrough while the toggle is
	-- off (returns the real mouse aim), so leaving it is identical to the stock client.
end

return SilentAim
end)
__bundle_register("Features/Combat/AutoDustlunge", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
local KeyHandling = require("Game/KeyHandling")

local InputClient = require("Game/InputClient")

local Latency = require("Game/Latency")

local Configuration = require("Utility/Configuration")

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

local AutoDustlunge = {}

local HITBOX_SIZE = Vector3.new(2, 6, 10)
local HITBOX_SHIFT_BACK = -5
local TICK_INTERVAL = 1 / 5

local holdKeyPicker = nil
local running = false
local simulationPart = nil

function AutoDustlunge.setHoldKeyPicker(keyPicker)
	holdKeyPicker = keyPicker
end

local function cleanupSimulation()
	if simulationPart then
		simulationPart:Destroy()
		simulationPart = nil
	end
end

local function getEffectModule()
	local effectReplicator = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("EffectReplicator")
	return effectReplicator and require(effectReplicator)
end

local function setSprinting(state)
	local character = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
	local characterHandler = character and character:FindFirstChild("CharacterHandler")
	local requests = characterHandler and characterHandler:FindFirstChild("Requests")
	local sprintRemote = requests and requests:FindFirstChild("ServerSprint")
	if sprintRemote then
		sprintRemote:FireServer(state)
	end
end

local function runTick()
	local effectReplicatorModule = getEffectModule()
	if not effectReplicatorModule or effectReplicatorModule:FindEffect("GrabCD") then
		cleanupSimulation()
		return
	end

	local character = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
	local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not root then
		return
	end

	local usedCFrame = root.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, HITBOX_SHIFT_BACK)

	if not simulationPart or simulationPart.Parent ~= workspace then
		cleanupSimulation()
		simulationPart = Instance.new("Part")
		simulationPart.Name = "AutoDustlungeHitbox"
		simulationPart.Anchored = true
		simulationPart.CanCollide = false
		simulationPart.CanQuery = false
		simulationPart.CanTouch = false
		simulationPart.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField
		simulationPart.CastShadow = false
	end

	simulationPart.Transparency = Configuration.expectToggleValue("AutoDustlungeDebug") and 0.5 or 1.0
	simulationPart.Shape = Enum.PartType.Block
	simulationPart.Size = HITBOX_SIZE
	simulationPart.CFrame = usedCFrame
	simulationPart.Parent = workspace

	local live = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")
	local instances = {}
	if live then
		for _, child in next, live:GetChildren() do
			if child ~= character then
				table.insert(instances, child)
			end
		end
	end

	if #instances <= 0 then
		return
	end

	local params = OverlapParams.new()
	params.FilterDescendantsInstances = instances
	params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include

	local ok, parts = pcall(function()
		return workspace:GetPartsInPart(simulationPart, params)
	end)

	if not ok or not parts or #parts <= 0 then
		return
	end

	local targetCharacter = parts[1]:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Model")
	local targetHumanoid = targetCharacter and targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
	local targetRoot = targetCharacter and targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not targetHumanoid or not targetRoot then
		return
	end

	local facingDot = (targetRoot.Position - root.Position):Dot(root.CFrame.LookVector)
	if facingDot <= 0 then
		return
	end

	local sprinting = effectReplicatorModule:FindEffect("Sprinting") ~= nil
	if sprinting then
		setSprinting(false)
		task.wait(1 / 20)
	end

	local scriptCrouched = false
	if not effectReplicatorModule:FindEffect("Crouching") then
		InputClient.crouch()
		scriptCrouched = true
	end

	local start = tick()
	local pingWindow = (Latency.rtt() or 0) * 2
	repeat
		task.wait()
	until effectReplicatorModule:FindEffect("Crouching") or tick() - start > pingWindow

	if tick() - start > pingWindow then
		if scriptCrouched then
			InputClient.uncrouch()
		end
		if sprinting then
			setSprinting(true)
		end
		return
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		local remote = KeyHandling.getRemote("LeftClick")
		if not remote then
			return
		end

		remote:FireServer(false, Players.LocalPlayer:GetMouse().Hit, {
			S = false,
			NOAERIALS = true,
			Space = false,
			Right = UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2),
			W = false,
			Left = true,
			ctrl = false,
		})
	end)

	task.wait(1 / 20)

	if scriptCrouched then
		InputClient.uncrouch()
	else
		local vim = Instance.new("VirtualInputManager")
		vim:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl, false, game)
		vim:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl, false, game)
	end

	if sprinting then
		setSprinting(true)
	end
end

local function loop()
	while running do
		if not holdKeyPicker or not holdKeyPicker:GetState() then
			cleanupSimulation()
		else
			local ok, err = pcall(runTick)
			if not ok then
				cleanupSimulation()
			end
		end

		task.wait(TICK_INTERVAL)
	end
end

function AutoDustlunge.enable()
	if running then
		return
	end

	cleanupSimulation()
	running = true
	task.spawn(loop)
end

function AutoDustlunge.disable()
	running = false
	cleanupSimulation()
end

return AutoDustlunge
end)
__bundle_register("Features/Combat/ForceAutoRotate", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
local ForceAutoRotate = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

local hooked = false
local active = false
local originalNewIndex = nil

-- Parallel-hook guard (see Game/Hooking.lua): on executors without parallel_hooks_allowed, install via a
-- read-only metatable swap so this __newindex hook can't be dispatched from an Actor parallel phase, which
-- is an illegal cross-phase op the engine Kernel panics on (RSL - panic: ...Kernel).
local parallelHooksAllowed = parallel_hooks_allowed
local hookmetamethod = (not parallelHooksAllowed)
	and function(target, method, hook)
		local metatable = getrawmetatable(target)
		setreadonly(metatable, false)
		local original = metatable[method]
		metatable[method] = hook
		setreadonly(metatable, true)
		return original
	end
	or hookmetamethod

local function installHook()
	if hooked then
		return true
	end

	if type(hookmetamethod) ~= "function" then
		return false
	end

	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		originalNewIndex = hookmetamethod(game, "__newindex", function(self, key, value)
			if active
				and key == "AutoRotate"
				and value == false
				and typeof(self) == "Instance"
				and self:IsA("Humanoid")
			then
				return
			end

			return originalNewIndex(self, key, value)
		end)
	end)

	if not ok then
		Logger.longNotify("Force Auto Rotate failed to hook: " .. tostring(result))
		return false
	end

	hooked = true
	return true
end


function ForceAutoRotate.init()
	if not installHook() then
		return
	end
	active = true
end

function ForceAutoRotate.detach()
	active = false
end

return ForceAutoRotate
end)
__bundle_register("Features/Combat/RhythmSpoofer", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
local RhythmSpoofer = {}

---@module Utility.Maid
local Maid = require("Utility/Maid")

---@module Utility.Configuration
local Configuration = require("Utility/Configuration")

local players = game:GetService("Players")
local tweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
local runService = game:GetService("RunService")
local userInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local replicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local debris = game:GetService("Debris")
local soundService = game:GetService("SoundService")

local localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer

local rhythmMaid = Maid.new()

local CONFIG = {
	FALLBACK_KEY = Enum.KeyCode.G,
	MIN_DURATION = 4.0,
	DETECTION_RANGE = 1000,
	ANIMATION_ID = "rbxassetid://4746910224",
	SOUND_ID = "rbxassetid://4748402358",
	SOUND_VOLUME = 0.8,
	FADE_IN_DURATION = 1.0,
	FADE_OUT_DURATION = 1.0,
	SATURATION_NORMAL = 0,
	SATURATION_ACTIVE = -1.0,
	TINT_NORMAL = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
	TINT_ACTIVE = Color3.fromRGB(217, 217, 217),
	SONAR_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(35, 55, 70),
	SONAR_START_SIZE = 80,
	SONAR_END_SIZE = 200,
}

local rhythmActive = false
local rhythmStartTime = nil
local pendingStop = false
local rhythmCC = nil
local currentAnimTrack = nil
local currentSound = nil
local reverbEffect = nil
local volumeEffect = nil
local reverbLoop = nil
local pulseLoop = nil
local debounce = false
local entityTimestamps = {}
local rhythmKeybind = nil
local effectModule = nil

local function getCharacter()
	return localPlayer and localPlayer.Character
end

local function getHRP()
	local character = getCharacter()
	return character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

local function getHumanoid()
	local character = getCharacter()
	return character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end

local function hasRhythmTalent()
	local passives = localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:GetAttribute("ssv_Passives")
	if passives and passives:find("Murmur: Rhythm") then return true end
	local backpack = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
	return backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild("Talent:Murmur: Rhythm")
end

local function getEffectModule()
	if effectModule then return effectModule end
	local effectReplicator = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("EffectReplicator")
	if effectReplicator then
		local success, result = pcall(require, effectReplicator)
		if success then
			effectModule = result
		end
	end
	return effectModule
end

local function isCrouching()
	local em = getEffectModule()
	if not em then return false end
	local clientCrouch = em:FindEffect("ClientCrouch")
	local serverCrouch = em:FindEffect("Crouching")
	return (clientCrouch ~= nil) or (serverCrouch ~= nil)
end

local function canUseRhythm()
	if not isCrouching() then
		return false
	end
	local em = getEffectModule()
	if em and em:FindEffect("Stun") then
		return false
	end
	return true
end

local function getRhythmKeybind()
	if rhythmKeybind then return rhythmKeybind end

	local keybindsModule = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("KeyBinds")
	if keybindsModule then
		local success, keybinds = pcall(require, keybindsModule)
		if success and keybinds and keybinds.GetBinding then
			local binding = keybinds.GetBinding("Rhythm")
			if binding and type(binding) == "table" then
				for _, keyName in ipairs(binding) do
					local ok, keyCode = pcall(function()
						return Enum.KeyCode[keyName]
					end)
					if ok and keyCode then
						rhythmKeybind = keyCode
						return rhythmKeybind
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	rhythmKeybind = CONFIG.FALLBACK_KEY
	return rhythmKeybind
end

local function getThrown()
	local thrown = workspace:FindFirstChild("Thrown")
	if not thrown then
		thrown = Instance.new("Folder")
		thrown.Name = "Thrown"
		thrown.Parent = workspace
	end
	return thrown
end

local function setupRhythmCC()
	rhythmCC = lighting:FindFirstChild("Rhythm")
	if not rhythmCC then
		rhythmCC = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
		rhythmCC.Name = "Rhythm"
		rhythmCC.Brightness = 0
		rhythmCC.Contrast = 0
		rhythmCC.Saturation = 0
		rhythmCC.TintColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		rhythmCC.Enabled = false
		rhythmCC.Parent = lighting
	end
	return rhythmCC
end

local function activateLighting()
	if not rhythmCC then setupRhythmCC() end

	rhythmCC.Saturation = CONFIG.SATURATION_NORMAL
	rhythmCC.TintColor = CONFIG.TINT_NORMAL
	rhythmCC.Enabled = true

	tweenService:Create(rhythmCC,
		TweenInfo.new(CONFIG.FADE_IN_DURATION, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear),
		{ Saturation = CONFIG.SATURATION_ACTIVE, TintColor = CONFIG.TINT_ACTIVE }
	):Play()
end

local function deactivateLighting()
	if not rhythmCC then return end

	tweenService:Create(rhythmCC,
		TweenInfo.new(CONFIG.FADE_OUT_DURATION, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear),
		{ Saturation = CONFIG.SATURATION_NORMAL, TintColor = CONFIG.TINT_NORMAL }
	):Play()
end

local function startAmbientOverride()
	local em = getEffectModule()
	if not em then return end

	if reverbEffect then
		pcall(function() reverbEffect:Remove() end)
		reverbEffect = nil
	end
	if volumeEffect then
		pcall(function() volumeEffect:Remove() end)
		volumeEffect = nil
	end
	if reverbLoop then
		pcall(function() reverbLoop:Disconnect() end)
		reverbLoop = nil
	end

	task.wait()

	pcall(function()
		reverbEffect = em:CreateEffect("ReverbOverride", {})
	end)

	pcall(function()
		volumeEffect = em:CreateEffect("AmbientVolume", { Value = 1 })
	end)

	reverbLoop = runService.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
		if rhythmActive then
			if soundService.AmbientReverb ~= Enum.ReverbType.Alley then
				soundService.AmbientReverb = Enum.ReverbType.Alley
			end
		end
	end))

	local numberValue = Instance.new("NumberValue")
	numberValue.Value = 1
	numberValue.Changed:Connect(function()
		if volumeEffect then
			volumeEffect.Value = numberValue.Value
		end
	end)

	tweenService:Create(numberValue, TweenInfo.new(1), { Value = 0 }):Play()

	task.delay(1.5, function()
		numberValue:Destroy()
	end)
end

local function stopAmbientOverride(immediate)
	if reverbLoop then
		pcall(function() reverbLoop:Disconnect() end)
		reverbLoop = nil
	end

	local currentVolumeEffect = volumeEffect
	local currentReverbEffect = reverbEffect
	volumeEffect = nil
	reverbEffect = nil

	if currentReverbEffect then
		pcall(function() currentReverbEffect:Remove() end)
	end

	soundService.AmbientReverb = Enum.ReverbType.NoReverb

	if not currentVolumeEffect then return end

	if immediate then
		pcall(function() currentVolumeEffect:Remove() end)
		return
	end

	local success, numberValue = pcall(function()
		local nv = Instance.new("NumberValue")
		nv.Value = 0
		return nv
	end)

	if success and numberValue then
		local conn
		conn = numberValue.Changed:Connect(function()
			pcall(function()
				currentVolumeEffect.Value = numberValue.Value
			end)
		end)

		tweenService:Create(numberValue, TweenInfo.new(1), { Value = 1 }):Play()

		task.delay(1.1, function()
			pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
			pcall(function() numberValue:Destroy() end)
			pcall(function() currentVolumeEffect:Remove() end)
		end)
	else
		pcall(function() currentVolumeEffect:Remove() end)
	end
end

local function playAnimation()
	local humanoid = getHumanoid()
	if not humanoid then return end

	local animator = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
	if not animator then return end

	local listenAnim = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Assets")
		and replicatedStorage.Assets:FindFirstChild("Anims")
		and replicatedStorage.Assets.Anims:FindFirstChild("Powers")
		and replicatedStorage.Assets.Anims.Powers:FindFirstChild("Listen")

	if listenAnim then
		currentAnimTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(listenAnim)
	else
		local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
		anim.AnimationId = CONFIG.ANIMATION_ID
		currentAnimTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
	end

	currentAnimTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
	currentAnimTrack:Play()
end

local function stopAnimation()
	if currentAnimTrack then
		currentAnimTrack:Stop()
		currentAnimTrack = nil
	end
end

local function playSound()
	local rhythmSound = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Sounds")
		and replicatedStorage.Sounds:FindFirstChild("Rhythm")

	if rhythmSound then
		currentSound = rhythmSound:Clone()
	else
		currentSound = Instance.new("Sound")
		currentSound.SoundId = CONFIG.SOUND_ID
		currentSound.Volume = CONFIG.SOUND_VOLUME
	end

	currentSound.Name = "Rhythm"
	currentSound.Parent = workspace
	currentSound:Play()

	debris:AddItem(currentSound, 3)
end

local function stopSound()
	if currentSound then
		currentSound:Stop()
		currentSound:Destroy()
		currentSound = nil
	end
end

local function createExpandingSonar()
	local hrp = getHRP()
	if not hrp then return end

	local part
	local sonarAsset = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Assets")
		and replicatedStorage.Assets:FindFirstChild("Effects")
		and replicatedStorage.Assets.Effects:FindFirstChild("Sonar")

	if sonarAsset then
		part = sonarAsset:Clone()
	else
		part = Instance.new("Part")
		part.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
		part.Color = CONFIG.SONAR_COLOR
		part.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField
		part.Anchored = true
		part.CanCollide = false
		part.CastShadow = false
	end

	part.Name = "Sonar"
	part.Transparency = 1
	part.Size = Vector3.new(CONFIG.SONAR_START_SIZE, CONFIG.SONAR_START_SIZE, CONFIG.SONAR_START_SIZE)
	part.CFrame = hrp.CFrame
	part.Parent = getThrown()

	tweenService:Create(part,
		TweenInfo.new(3, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
		{ Size = Vector3.new(CONFIG.SONAR_END_SIZE, CONFIG.SONAR_END_SIZE, CONFIG.SONAR_END_SIZE) }
	):Play()

	tweenService:Create(part,
		TweenInfo.new(1, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, 0, true),
		{ Transparency = 0 }
	):Play()

	debris:AddItem(part, 4)
end

local function detectEntities()
	local hrp = getHRP()
	if not hrp then return end

	local character = getCharacter()
	local myPos = hrp.Position
	local range = CONFIG.DETECTION_RANGE

	local liveFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")
	if not liveFolder then return end

	local clientEffectDirect = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Requests")
		and replicatedStorage.Requests:FindFirstChild("ClientEffectDirect")
	if not clientEffectDirect then return end

	for _, entity in ipairs(liveFolder:GetChildren()) do
		local entityHRP = entity:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if entityHRP and entity ~= character and not entity:HasTag("InTacet") then
			if not entityTimestamps[entity.Name] then
				entityTimestamps[entity.Name] = tick()
			elseif tick() - entityTimestamps[entity.Name] >= 1 then
				entityTimestamps[entity.Name] = tick()
			else
				continue
			end

			local effectiveRange = range
			if entity:HasTag("Disguised") then
				effectiveRange = effectiveRange * 0.1
			end

			local dist = (myPos - entityHRP.Position).Magnitude
			if dist < effectiveRange then
				local color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)

				local hum = entity:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if hum and not entity:HasTag("NoVisibleDamage") then
					local healthPercent = hum.Health / hum.MaxHealth
					if healthPercent < 0.33 then
						color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
					elseif healthPercent < 0.66 then
						color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 179, 0)
					end
				end

				clientEffectDirect:Fire("sonarsmall", {
					pos = entityHRP.Position,
					col = color
				})
			end
		end
	end
end

local function canDeactivate()
	if not rhythmStartTime then return true end
	return (tick() - rhythmStartTime) >= CONFIG.MIN_DURATION
end

local function stopRhythm()
	if not rhythmActive then return end

	rhythmActive = false
	rhythmStartTime = nil
	pendingStop = false
	debounce = true

	stopAnimation()
	currentSound = nil
	deactivateLighting()
	stopAmbientOverride()

	if pulseLoop then
		task.cancel(pulseLoop)
		pulseLoop = nil
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		task.wait(0.2)
		debounce = false
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		task.wait(1)
		if rhythmCC then
			rhythmCC.Enabled = false
		end
	end)
end

local function startRhythm()
	if debounce then return end
	if rhythmActive then return end

	rhythmActive = true
	debounce = true
	rhythmStartTime = tick()
	pendingStop = false

	playAnimation()

	task.wait(0.1)
	playSound()

	task.wait(0.2)

	if not isCrouching() then
		rhythmActive = false
		rhythmStartTime = nil
		pendingStop = false
		stopAnimation()

		if currentSound then
			tweenService:Create(currentSound, TweenInfo.new(0.2), { Volume = 0 }):Play()
			task.wait(0.2)
			stopSound()
		end

		debounce = false
		return
	end

	setupRhythmCC()
	activateLighting()
	startAmbientOverride()

	entityTimestamps = {}
	detectEntities()

	pulseLoop = task.spawn(function()
		while rhythmActive do
			task.wait(0.2)
			detectEntities()
		end
	end)

	for _ = 1, 10 do
		task.spawn(createExpandingSonar)
		task.wait(0.1)
	end

	task.wait(0.5)

	task.spawn(function()
		task.wait(0.2)
		debounce = false
	end)
end

local function onKeyPress(input, gameProcessed)
	if gameProcessed then return end
	if not Configuration.expectToggleValue("RhythmSpoofer") then return end
	if hasRhythmTalent() then return end

	local keybind = getRhythmKeybind()
	if input.KeyCode ~= keybind then return end

	if rhythmActive then
		if canDeactivate() then
			stopRhythm()
		else
			pendingStop = true
		end
	else
		if canUseRhythm() then
			startRhythm()
		end
	end
end

local monitorRhythmCheck = LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
	if not rhythmActive then return end
	if not Configuration.expectToggleValue("RhythmSpoofer") then
		stopRhythm()
		return
	end

	if canDeactivate() then
		local canUse = canUseRhythm()
		if pendingStop or not canUse then
			stopRhythm()
		end
	end
end)

function RhythmSpoofer.init()
	rhythmMaid:add(userInputService.InputBegan:Connect(onKeyPress))
	rhythmMaid:add(runService.Heartbeat:Connect(monitorRhythmCheck))

	rhythmMaid:add(localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
		currentAnimTrack = nil
		currentSound = nil
		stopAmbientOverride(true)
		if rhythmActive then
			stopRhythm()
		end
	end))

	getRhythmKeybind()
end

function RhythmSpoofer.detach()
	if rhythmActive then
		stopRhythm()
	end
	rhythmMaid:clean()

	if rhythmCC then
		rhythmCC:Destroy()
		rhythmCC = nil
	end
end

return RhythmSpoofer
end)
__bundle_register("GUI/SpotifyWidget", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
local SpotifyClient = require("Utility/SpotifyClient")
local Filesystem = require("Utility/Filesystem")
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local Mouse = Players.LocalPlayer:GetMouse()

local artStorage = Filesystem.new("Luma/Spotify/Art")
local artCache = {}

local POLL_INTERVAL = 2

local SpotifyWidget = {}

local built = false
local library = nil

local outer, inner, artLabel, titleLabel, artistLabel
local barBack, barFill, scrubberDot, currentTimeLabel, durationLabel
local playPauseLabel

local lastPoll = 0
local nowPlaying = nil
local lastProgressMs = 0
local lastProgressAt = 0
local fetchingArt = {}
local scrubbing = false

local function formatTime(ms)
	local totalSeconds = math.floor((ms or 0) / 1000)
	local minutes = math.floor(totalSeconds / 60)
	local seconds = totalSeconds % 60
	return string.format("%d:%02d", minutes, seconds)
end

local function fetchAlbumArt(trackId, url)
	if not trackId or not url or artCache[trackId] or fetchingArt[trackId] then
		return
	end

	fetchingArt[trackId] = true

	task.spawn(function()
		local filename = trackId .. ".png"
		local contentId = nil

		local ok = pcall(function()
			if not artStorage:file(filename) then
				local response = request({ Url = url, Method = "GET" })
				if not response or not response.Success or not response.Body then
					return
				end
				artStorage:write(filename, response.Body)
			end

			contentId = getcustomasset(artStorage:append(filename))
		end)

		if ok and contentId then
			artCache[trackId] = contentId
			if nowPlaying and nowPlaying.trackId == trackId then
				artLabel.Image = contentId
			end
		end

		fetchingArt[trackId] = nil
	end)
end

local function makeControlButton(parent, text, position, callback)
	local buttonOuter = library:Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = position,
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 24),
		ZIndex = 210,
		Parent = parent,
	})

	local label = library:CreateLabel({
		Active = false,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
		TextSize = 18,
		Text = text,
		ZIndex = 211,
		Parent = buttonOuter,
	})

	library:OnHighlight(buttonOuter, label, { TextColor3 = "AccentColor" }, { TextColor3 = "FontColor" })

	label.InputBegan:Connect(function(Input)
		if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and not library:MouseIsOverOpenedFrame() then
			callback()
		end
	end)

	return buttonOuter, label
end

function SpotifyWidget.init(Library)
	if built then
		return
	end
	built = true
	library = Library

	outer = library:Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
		BorderColor3 = library.AccentColor,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 100),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 230, 0, 300),
		Visible = false,
		ZIndex = 205,
		Parent = library.ScreenGui,
	})

	library:AddToRegistry(outer, {
		BorderColor3 = "AccentColor",
	})

	library:Create("UICorner", {
		CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10),
		Parent = outer,
	})

	library:MakeDraggable(outer)

	inner = library:Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = library.MainColor,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 1),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 1, -2),
		ZIndex = 206,
		Parent = outer,
	})

	library:AddToRegistry(inner, {
		BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor",
	})

	library:Create("UICorner", {
		CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9),
		Parent = inner,
	})

	artLabel = library:Create("ImageLabel", {
		BackgroundColor3 = library:GetDarkerColor(library.MainColor),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 180),
		Image = "",
		ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop,
		ZIndex = 207,
		Parent = inner,
	})

	library:AddToRegistry(artLabel, {
		BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor",
	})

	library:Create("UICorner", {
		CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8),
		Parent = artLabel,
	})

	titleLabel = library:CreateLabel({
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 190),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 18),
		TextSize = 14,
		Text = "Nothing playing",
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
		ZIndex = 207,
		Parent = inner,
	})

	artistLabel = library:CreateLabel({
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 208),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 16),
		TextSize = 12,
		Text = "",
		TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 170),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
		ZIndex = 207,
		Parent = inner,
	})

	barBack = library:Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = library.OutlineColor,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 236),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 3),
		ZIndex = 207,
		Parent = inner,
	})

	library:AddToRegistry(barBack, {
		BackgroundColor3 = "OutlineColor",
	})

	library:Create("UICorner", {
		CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0),
		Parent = barBack,
	})

	barFill = library:Create("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = library.AccentColor,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),
		ZIndex = 208,
		Parent = barBack,
	})

	library:AddToRegistry(barFill, {
		BackgroundColor3 = "AccentColor",
	})

	library:Create("UICorner", {
		CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0),
		Parent = barFill,
	})

	scrubberDot = library:Create("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8),
		ZIndex = 209,
		Parent = barFill,
	})

	library:Create("UICorner", {
		CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0),
		Parent = scrubberDot,
	})

	local barHitArea = library:Create("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16),
		ZIndex = 212,
		Parent = barBack,
	})

	local function seekToMouseX()
		if not nowPlaying or nowPlaying.durationMs <= 0 then
			return nil
		end

		local relativeX = Mouse.X - barBack.AbsolutePosition.X
		local percent = math.clamp(relativeX / barBack.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)

		barFill.Size = UDim2.new(percent, 0, 1, 0)
		currentTimeLabel.Text = formatTime(percent * nowPlaying.durationMs)

		return percent
	end

	barHitArea.InputBegan:Connect(function(Input)
		if Input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			return
		end

		scrubbing = true
		local finalPercent

		repeat
			finalPercent = seekToMouseX() or finalPercent
			RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
		until not UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)

		scrubbing = false

		if finalPercent and nowPlaying and nowPlaying.durationMs > 0 then
			local seekMs = math.floor(finalPercent * nowPlaying.durationMs)
			lastProgressMs = seekMs
			lastProgressAt = os.clock()
			SpotifyClient.seek(seekMs)
		end
	end)

	currentTimeLabel = library:CreateLabel({
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 242),
		Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -8, 0, 14),
		TextSize = 11,
		Text = "0:00",
		TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 170),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		ZIndex = 207,
		Parent = inner,
	})

	durationLabel = library:CreateLabel({
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 2, 0, 242),
		Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -8, 0, 14),
		TextSize = 11,
		Text = "0:00",
		TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 170),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
		ZIndex = 207,
		Parent = inner,
	})

	local function forcePollSoon(delaySeconds)
		task.delay(delaySeconds or 0.4, function()
			lastPoll = 0
		end)
	end

	makeControlButton(inner, "<", UDim2.new(0.5, -55, 0, 264), function()
		SpotifyClient.previous()
		forcePollSoon(0.5)
	end)

	local _, resolvedPlayPauseLabel = makeControlButton(inner, "||", UDim2.new(0.5, -15, 0, 264), function()
		if nowPlaying then
			nowPlaying.isPlaying = not nowPlaying.isPlaying
			playPauseLabel.Text = nowPlaying.isPlaying and "||" or "▶"

			if nowPlaying.isPlaying then
				SpotifyClient.play()
			else
				SpotifyClient.pause()
			end
		else
			SpotifyClient.play()
		end

		forcePollSoon()
	end)
	playPauseLabel = resolvedPlayPauseLabel

	makeControlButton(inner, ">", UDim2.new(0.5, 25, 0, 264), function()
		SpotifyClient.next()
		forcePollSoon(0.5)
	end)

	RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if not outer.Visible then
			return
		end

		if os.clock() - lastPoll >= POLL_INTERVAL then
			lastPoll = os.clock()

			task.spawn(function()
				local result = SpotifyClient.getCurrentlyPlaying()
				nowPlaying = result

				if result then
					if not scrubbing then
						lastProgressMs = result.progressMs
						lastProgressAt = os.clock()
					end

					titleLabel.Text = result.trackName
					artistLabel.Text = result.artistName
					durationLabel.Text = formatTime(result.durationMs)
					playPauseLabel.Text = result.isPlaying and "||" or "▶"

					if not artCache[result.trackId] then
						artLabel.Image = ""
					end
					fetchAlbumArt(result.trackId, result.albumArtUrl)
				else
					titleLabel.Text = "Nothing playing"
					artistLabel.Text = ""
					durationLabel.Text = "0:00"
					currentTimeLabel.Text = "0:00"
					barFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
					artLabel.Image = ""
				end
			end)
		end

		if not nowPlaying or scrubbing then
			return
		end

		local elapsedMs = nowPlaying.isPlaying and ((os.clock() - lastProgressAt) * 1000) or 0
		local displayedMs = math.clamp(lastProgressMs + elapsedMs, 0, nowPlaying.durationMs)
		local percent = nowPlaying.durationMs > 0 and (displayedMs / nowPlaying.durationMs) or 0

		currentTimeLabel.Text = formatTime(displayedMs)
		barFill.Size = UDim2.new(percent, 0, 1, 0)
	end)
end

function SpotifyWidget.setVisible(visible)
	if outer then
		outer.Visible = visible
	end
end

return SpotifyWidget
end)
__bundle_register("Utility/SpotifyClient", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
local JSON = require("Utility/JSON")
local Filesystem = require("Utility/Filesystem")
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

local storage = Filesystem.new("Luma/Spotify")

local DEBUG = false
local function dbg(fmt, ...)
	if DEBUG then
		Logger.warn("[Spotify] " .. fmt, ...)
	end
end

local SpotifyClient = {}

local CLIENT_ID = "70dfc46fe4294dfcb51b5879f021f9d1"

local REDIRECT_URI = "http://127.0.0.1:8888/callback"
local SCOPES = "user-read-playback-state user-modify-playback-state user-read-currently-playing"
local TOKEN_URL = "https://accounts.spotify.com/api/token"
local API_BASE = "https://api.spotify.com/v1/me/player"

local BASE64_CHARS = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
local VERIFIER_CHARS = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789-._~"

local state = {
	clientId = CLIENT_ID,
	refreshToken = "",
	codeVerifier = nil,
	accessToken = nil,
	accessTokenExpiresAt = 0,
}

local function base64Encode(input)
	local out = {}
	local length = #input

	for i = 1, length, 3 do
		local b1, b2, b3 = string.byte(input, i, i + 2)
		b2 = b2 or 0
		b3 = b3 or 0

		local n = b1 * 65536 + b2 * 256 + b3

		local c1 = math.floor(n / 262144) % 64
		local c2 = math.floor(n / 4096) % 64
		local c3 = math.floor(n / 64) % 64
		local c4 = n % 64

		local chunk = string.sub(BASE64_CHARS, c1 + 1, c1 + 1)
			.. string.sub(BASE64_CHARS, c2 + 1, c2 + 1)
			.. (i + 1 <= length and string.sub(BASE64_CHARS, c3 + 1, c3 + 1) or "=")
			.. (i + 2 <= length and string.sub(BASE64_CHARS, c4 + 1, c4 + 1) or "=")

		table.insert(out, chunk)
	end

	return table.concat(out)
end

local function base64UrlEncode(input)
	return (base64Encode(input):gsub("[+/=]", { ["+"] = "-", ["/"] = "_", ["="] = "" }))
end

local function urlEncode(value)
	return (tostring(value):gsub("[^%w%-%.%_%~]", function(char)
		return string.format("%%%02X", string.byte(char))
	end))
end

local function encodeForm(fields)
	local parts = {}
	for key, value in next, fields do
		table.insert(parts, urlEncode(key) .. "=" .. urlEncode(value))
	end
	return table.concat(parts, "&")
end

local SHA256_K = {
	0x428a2f98, 0x71374491, 0xb5c0fbcf, 0xe9b5dba5, 0x3956c25b, 0x59f111f1, 0x923f82a4, 0xab1c5ed5,
	0xd807aa98, 0x12835b01, 0x243185be, 0x550c7dc3, 0x72be5d74, 0x80deb1fe, 0x9bdc06a7, 0xc19bf174,
	0xe49b69c1, 0xefbe4786, 0x0fc19dc6, 0x240ca1cc, 0x2de92c6f, 0x4a7484aa, 0x5cb0a9dc, 0x76f988da,
	0x983e5152, 0xa831c66d, 0xb00327c8, 0xbf597fc7, 0xc6e00bf3, 0xd5a79147, 0x06ca6351, 0x14292967,
	0x27b70a85, 0x2e1b2138, 0x4d2c6dfc, 0x53380d13, 0x650a7354, 0x766a0abb, 0x81c2c92e, 0x92722c85,
	0xa2bfe8a1, 0xa81a664b, 0xc24b8b70, 0xc76c51a3, 0xd192e819, 0xd6990624, 0xf40e3585, 0x106aa070,
	0x19a4c116, 0x1e376c08, 0x2748774c, 0x34b0bcb5, 0x391c0cb3, 0x4ed8aa4a, 0x5b9cca4f, 0x682e6ff3,
	0x748f82ee, 0x78a5636f, 0x84c87814, 0x8cc70208, 0x90befffa, 0xa4506ceb, 0xbef9a3f7, 0xc67178f2,
}

local function sha256(msg)
	local band, bxor, bnot, rshift, rrotate = bit32.band, bit32.bxor, bit32.bnot, bit32.rshift, bit32.rrotate

	local h0, h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, h7 =
		0x6a09e667, 0xbb67ae85, 0x3c6ef372, 0xa54ff53a, 0x510e527f, 0x9b05688c, 0x1f83d9ab, 0x5be0cd19

	local msgLen = #msg
	local bitLen = msgLen * 8

	msg = msg .. "\128"
	while #msg % 64 ~= 56 do
		msg = msg .. "\0"
	end

	for i = 7, 0, -1 do
		msg = msg .. string.char(band(rshift(bitLen, i * 8), 0xFF))
	end

	for chunkStart = 1, #msg, 64 do
		local w = {}
		for i = 0, 15 do
			local o = chunkStart + i * 4
			local b1, b2, b3, b4 = string.byte(msg, o, o + 3)
			w[i + 1] = b1 * 16777216 + b2 * 65536 + b3 * 256 + b4
		end

		for i = 17, 64 do
			local w15, w2 = w[i - 15], w[i - 2]
			local s0 = bxor(rrotate(w15, 7), rrotate(w15, 18), rshift(w15, 3))
			local s1 = bxor(rrotate(w2, 17), rrotate(w2, 19), rshift(w2, 10))
			w[i] = (w[i - 16] + s0 + w[i - 7] + s1) % 4294967296
		end

		local a, b, c, d, e, f, g, h = h0, h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, h7

		for i = 1, 64 do
			local S1 = bxor(rrotate(e, 6), rrotate(e, 11), rrotate(e, 25))
			local ch = bxor(band(e, f), band(bnot(e), g))
			local temp1 = (h + S1 + ch + SHA256_K[i] + w[i]) % 4294967296
			local S0 = bxor(rrotate(a, 2), rrotate(a, 13), rrotate(a, 22))
			local maj = bxor(band(a, b), band(a, c), band(b, c))
			local temp2 = (S0 + maj) % 4294967296

			h, g, f = g, f, e
			e = (d + temp1) % 4294967296
			d, c, b = c, b, a
			a = (temp1 + temp2) % 4294967296
		end

		h0 = (h0 + a) % 4294967296
		h1 = (h1 + b) % 4294967296
		h2 = (h2 + c) % 4294967296
		h3 = (h3 + d) % 4294967296
		h4 = (h4 + e) % 4294967296
		h5 = (h5 + f) % 4294967296
		h6 = (h6 + g) % 4294967296
		h7 = (h7 + h) % 4294967296
	end

	local out = {}
	for _, v in next, { h0, h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, h7 } do
		out[#out + 1] = string.char(
			band(rshift(v, 24), 0xFF),
			band(rshift(v, 16), 0xFF),
			band(rshift(v, 8), 0xFF),
			band(v, 0xFF)
		)
	end

	return table.concat(out)
end

local function generateCodeVerifier()
	local rng = Random.new()
	local chars = {}
	for i = 1, 64 do
		local idx = rng:NextInteger(1, #VERIFIER_CHARS)
		chars[i] = VERIFIER_CHARS:sub(idx, idx)
	end
	return table.concat(chars)
end

function SpotifyClient.load()
	if not storage:file("auth.json") then
		return
	end

	local ok, decoded = pcall(JSON.decode, storage:read("auth.json"))
	if not ok or type(decoded) ~= "table" then
		return
	end

	if type(decoded.clientId) == "string" and decoded.clientId ~= "" then
		state.clientId = decoded.clientId
	end
	state.refreshToken = decoded.refreshToken or ""
end

function SpotifyClient.save()
	storage:write(
		"auth.json",
		JSON.encode({
			clientId = state.clientId,
			refreshToken = state.refreshToken,
		})
	)
end

function SpotifyClient.setClientId(clientId)
	if clientId and clientId ~= "" then
		state.clientId = clientId
		SpotifyClient.save()
	end
end

function SpotifyClient.getClientId()
	return state.clientId
end

function SpotifyClient.isAuthorized()
	return state.refreshToken ~= ""
end

function SpotifyClient.getAuthUrl()
	state.codeVerifier = generateCodeVerifier()
	local codeChallenge = base64UrlEncode(sha256(state.codeVerifier))

	return "https://accounts.spotify.com/authorize?"
		.. encodeForm({
			client_id = state.clientId,
			response_type = "code",
			redirect_uri = REDIRECT_URI,
			scope = SCOPES,
			code_challenge_method = "S256",
			code_challenge = codeChallenge,
		})
end

local function extractCode(pastedUrl)
	return pastedUrl:match("[?&]code=([^&#]+)")
end

function SpotifyClient.completeAuth(pastedUrl)
	if not state.codeVerifier then
		return false, "Click 'Copy Auth URL' first -- authorization needs to start from there."
	end

	local code = extractCode(pastedUrl)
	if not code then
		return false, "Couldn't find a 'code' parameter in that URL."
	end

	local response = request({
		Url = TOKEN_URL,
		Method = "POST",
		Headers = {
			["Content-Type"] = "application/x-www-form-urlencoded",
		},
		Body = encodeForm({
			grant_type = "authorization_code",
			code = code,
			redirect_uri = REDIRECT_URI,
			client_id = state.clientId,
			code_verifier = state.codeVerifier,
		}),
	})

	state.codeVerifier = nil

	if not response or not response.Success then
		return false, "Token exchange request failed."
	end

	local ok, decoded = pcall(JSON.decode, response.Body)
	if not ok or not decoded or not decoded.refresh_token then
		return false, "Token exchange returned an unexpected response."
	end

	state.refreshToken = decoded.refresh_token
	state.accessToken = decoded.access_token
	state.accessTokenExpiresAt = os.clock() + (decoded.expires_in or 3600) - 30
	SpotifyClient.save()

	return true
end

local function refreshAccessToken()
	if state.refreshToken == "" then
		return false
	end

	local response = request({
		Url = TOKEN_URL,
		Method = "POST",
		Headers = {
			["Content-Type"] = "application/x-www-form-urlencoded",
		},
		Body = encodeForm({
			grant_type = "refresh_token",
			refresh_token = state.refreshToken,
			client_id = state.clientId,
		}),
	})

	if not response or not response.Success then
		return false
	end

	local ok, decoded = pcall(JSON.decode, response.Body)
	if not ok or not decoded or not decoded.access_token then
		return false
	end

	state.accessToken = decoded.access_token
	state.accessTokenExpiresAt = os.clock() + (decoded.expires_in or 3600) - 30

	if decoded.refresh_token then
		state.refreshToken = decoded.refresh_token
		SpotifyClient.save()
	end

	return true
end

local function ensureToken()
	if state.accessToken and os.clock() < state.accessTokenExpiresAt then
		return true
	end

	return refreshAccessToken()
end

local function authorizedRequest(method, path, body)
	if not ensureToken() then
		dbg("%s %s: ensureToken() failed, no valid access token", method, path)
		return nil
	end

	local response = request({
		Url = API_BASE .. path,
		Method = method,
		Headers = {
			["Authorization"] = "Bearer " .. state.accessToken,
			["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
		},
		Body = body or "{}",
	})

	if not response then
		dbg("%s %s: request() returned nil", method, path)
	elseif not response.Success then
		dbg("%s %s: failed, status=%s body=%s", method, path, tostring(response.StatusCode), tostring(response.Body))
	else
		dbg("%s %s: ok, status=%s", method, path, tostring(response.StatusCode))
	end

	return response
end

function SpotifyClient.getCurrentlyPlaying()
	local response = authorizedRequest("GET", "/currently-playing")
	if not response or not response.Success or response.StatusCode == 204 or not response.Body or response.Body == "" then
		return nil
	end

	local ok, decoded = pcall(JSON.decode, response.Body)
	if not ok or not decoded or not decoded.item then
		return nil
	end

	local images = decoded.item.album and decoded.item.album.images or {}
	local artists = {}
	for _, artist in next, decoded.item.artists or {} do
		table.insert(artists, artist.name)
	end

	return {
		trackId = decoded.item.id,
		trackName = decoded.item.name,
		artistName = table.concat(artists, ", "),
		albumArtUrl = images[1] and images[1].url,
		progressMs = decoded.progress_ms or 0,
		durationMs = decoded.item.duration_ms or 0,
		isPlaying = decoded.is_playing or false,
	}
end

function SpotifyClient.play()
	authorizedRequest("PUT", "/play")
end

function SpotifyClient.pause()
	authorizedRequest("PUT", "/pause")
end

function SpotifyClient.next()
	authorizedRequest("POST", "/next")
end

function SpotifyClient.previous()
	authorizedRequest("POST", "/previous")
end

function SpotifyClient.seek(positionMs)
	authorizedRequest("PUT", "/seek?position_ms=" .. math.floor(positionMs))
end

SpotifyClient.load()

return SpotifyClient
end)
__bundle_register("GUI/ThemeManager", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
return require("GUI/LinoriaAddons").ThemeManager
end)
__bundle_register("Menu/TimingBuilderTab", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
local TimingBuilderTab = {}

---@module GUI.Icons
local Icons = require("GUI/Icons")

---@module GUI.Library
local Library = require("GUI/Library")

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Environment
local Environment = require("Utility/Environment")

---@module Game.Timings.SaveManager
local SaveManager = require("Game/Timings/SaveManager")

---@module Game.Timings.ModuleManager
local ModuleManager = require("Game/Timings/ModuleManager")

---@module Game.Timings.AnimationTiming
local AnimationTiming = require("Game/Timings/AnimationTiming")

---@module Game.Timings.EffectTiming
local EffectTiming = require("Game/Timings/EffectTiming")

---@module Game.Timings.PartTiming
local PartTiming = require("Game/Timings/PartTiming")

---@module Game.Timings.SoundTiming
local SoundTiming = require("Game/Timings/SoundTiming")

---@module Game.Timings.Action
local Action = require("Game/Timings/Action")

---@module Features.Game.AnimationVisualizer
local AnimationVisualizer = require("Features/Game/AnimationVisualizer")

---@module Utility.JSON
local JSON = require("Utility/JSON")

local BASE64_CHARS = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
local function base64Encode(input)
	local out = {}
	local length = #input
	for i = 1, length, 3 do
		local b1, b2, b3 = string.byte(input, i, i + 2)
		b2 = b2 or 0
		b3 = b3 or 0
		local n = b1 * 65536 + b2 * 256 + b3
		local c1 = math.floor(n / 262144) % 64
		local c2 = math.floor(n / 4096) % 64
		local c3 = math.floor(n / 64) % 64
		local c4 = n % 64
		out[#out + 1] = string.sub(BASE64_CHARS, c1 + 1, c1 + 1)
			.. string.sub(BASE64_CHARS, c2 + 1, c2 + 1)
			.. (i + 1 <= length and string.sub(BASE64_CHARS, c3 + 1, c3 + 1) or "=")
			.. (i + 2 <= length and string.sub(BASE64_CHARS, c4 + 1, c4 + 1) or "=")
	end
	return table.concat(out)
end

local function countKeys(t)
	local n = 0
	for _ in next, t or {} do
		n = n + 1
	end
	return n
end

local function makePermissiveEnv()
	local base = getfenv(1)

	local dummy
	local dummyMeta = {
		__index = function()
			return dummy
		end,
		__newindex = function() end,
		__call = function()
			return dummy
		end,
		__concat = function()
			return dummy
		end,
	}
	for _, op in ipairs({ "__add", "__sub", "__mul", "__div", "__mod", "__pow", "__unm" }) do
		dummyMeta[op] = function()
			return dummy
		end
	end
	dummy = setmetatable({}, dummyMeta)

	return setmetatable({}, {
		__index = function(_, key)
			local real = base[key]
			if real ~= nil then
				return real
			end
			return dummy
		end,
		__newindex = function() end,
	})
end

---@param source string
---@return boolean ok, string? err
local function validateModuleSource(source)
	local chunk, compileErr = loadstring(source)
	if not chunk then
		local clean = tostring(compileErr):gsub('^%[string "[^"]*"%]:', "line ")
		return false, "Syntax error: " .. clean
	end

	pcall(function()
		setfenv(chunk, makePermissiveEnv())
	end)

	local ok, result = pcall(chunk)
	if not ok then
		local clean = tostring(result):gsub('^%[string "[^"]*"%]:', "line ")
		return false, "Error while loading: " .. clean
	end

	if type(result) ~= "function" then
		return false, "Module must `return function(self, timing) ... end` (got " .. type(result) .. ")."
	end

	return true
end

local TYPE_ORDER = { "Animation", "Part", "Sound", "Effect" }
local TYPES = {
	Animation = { new = function() return AnimationTiming.new() end, pair = function() return SaveManager.as end, idProp = "_id" },
	Part = { new = function() return PartTiming.new() end, pair = function() return SaveManager.ps end, idProp = "pname" },
	Sound = { new = function() return SoundTiming.new() end, pair = function() return SaveManager.ss end, idProp = "_id" },
	Effect = { new = function() return EffectTiming.new() end, pair = function() return SaveManager.es end, idProp = "ename" },
}

local TEMPLATE_NAMES = {}
local TEMPLATE_CODE = {}
for _, entry in ipairs(TEMPLATES) do
	TEMPLATE_NAMES[#TEMPLATE_NAMES + 1] = entry[1]
	TEMPLATE_CODE[entry[1]] = entry[2]
end

function TimingBuilderTab.ensurePersistence()
	if SaveManager.llcn then
		return
	end

	local name = "MyTimings"
	local exists = false
	for _, file in ipairs(SaveManager.list()) do
		if file == name then
			exists = true
			break
		end
	end

	if not exists then
		SaveManager.create(name)
	end

	SaveManager.load(name)
	SaveManager.autoload(name)
end

---@param box table
---@param getActive fun():Timing?
---@param getWritable fun():Timing?
---@return table
local function buildActionsEditor(box, getActive, getWritable)
	getWritable = getWritable or getActive
	local ctrl = {}
	local list, nameInput, typeDrop, delayInput, hbLength, hbWidth, hbHeight

	local function anc(apply)
		return function(value)
			---@note: Only mutate when an action is actually selected. Checking the selection before
			---getWritable() also avoids spuriously cloning an internal timing into the config just
			---from touching a field while nothing is selected (e.g. while configuring a new action).
			if not list.Value then
				return
			end
			local timing = getWritable()
			if not timing then
				return
			end
			local action = timing.actions:find(list.Value)
			if not action then
				return
			end
			apply(action, value)
			SaveManager.markDirty()
		end
	end

	list = box:AddDropdown(nil, {
		Text = "Actions",
		Values = {},
		AllowNull = true,
		Searchable = true,
		Callback = function(value)
			local timing = getActive()
			if not timing or not value then
				return
			end
			local action = timing.actions:find(value)
			if not action then
				return
			end
			nameInput:SetRawValue(action.name)
			typeDrop:SetRawValue(action._type)
			delayInput:SetRawValue(tostring(action._when or 0))
			hbWidth:SetRawValue(action.hitbox.X)
			hbHeight:SetRawValue(action.hitbox.Y)
			hbLength:SetRawValue(action.hitbox.Z)
		end,
	})

	typeDrop = box:AddDropdown(nil, {
		Text = "Action Type",
		Values = ACTION_TYPES,
		Default = 1,
		Callback = anc(function(action, value)
			action._type = value
		end),
	})

	delayInput = box:AddInput(nil, {
		Text = "Action Delay (ms)",
		Numeric = true,
		Finished = true,
		Callback = anc(function(action, value)
			action._when = tonumber(value) or 0
		end),
	})

	hbLength = box:AddSlider(nil, {
		Text = "Action Hitbox Length",
		Min = 0,
		Max = 300,
		Default = 0,
		Rounding = 0,
		Callback = anc(function(action, value)
			action.hitbox = Vector3.new(action.hitbox.X, action.hitbox.Y, value)
		end),
	})

	hbWidth = box:AddSlider(nil, {
		Text = "Action Hitbox Width",
		Min = 0,
		Max = 300,
		Default = 0,
		Rounding = 0,
		Callback = anc(function(action, value)
			action.hitbox = Vector3.new(value, action.hitbox.Y, action.hitbox.Z)
		end),
	})

	hbHeight = box:AddSlider(nil, {
		Text = "Action Hitbox Height",
		Min = 0,
		Max = 300,
		Default = 0,
		Rounding = 0,
		Callback = anc(function(action, value)
			action.hitbox = Vector3.new(action.hitbox.X, value, action.hitbox.Z)
		end),
	})

	box:AddDivider()

	nameInput = box:AddInput(nil, {
		Text = "Action Name",
	})

	box:AddButton("Create Action", function()
		local timing = getWritable()
		if not timing then
			return Logger.longNotify("Select or create a timing first.")
		end
		local name = nameInput.Value
		if not name or #name <= 0 then
			return Logger.longNotify("Enter an action name.")
		end
		if timing.actions:find(name) then
			return Logger.longNotify("The action '%s' already exists.", name)
		end
		---@note: Seed the new action from whatever is currently in the editor fields so "set the
		---type/delay/hitbox, name it, create" produces an action with those values -- instead of a
		---blank action while the values silently apply to the previously-selected action.
		local action = Action.new()
		action.name = name
		action._type = typeDrop.Value or "Parry"
		action._when = tonumber(delayInput.Value) or 0
		action.hitbox = Vector3.new(
			tonumber(hbWidth.Value) or 0,
			tonumber(hbHeight.Value) or 0,
			tonumber(hbLength.Value) or 0
		)
		timing.actions:push(action)
		SaveManager.markDirty()
		---@note: reload() clears the selection and resets the fields, so the editor returns to a
		---clean "new action" state and the next create won't inherit this one's values.
		ctrl.reload(timing)
		list:Display()
	end)

	box:AddButton("Duplicate Action", function()
		local timing = getWritable()
		if not timing then
			return Logger.longNotify("Select or create a timing first.")
		end
		local action = timing.actions:find(list.Value)
		if not action then
			return Logger.longNotify("Select an action to duplicate.")
		end
		local name = nameInput.Value
		if not name or #name <= 0 then
			return Logger.longNotify("Enter a name for the copy.")
		end
		if timing.actions:find(name) then
			return Logger.longNotify("The action '%s' already exists.", name)
		end
		local copy = action:clone()
		copy.name = name
		timing.actions:push(copy)
		SaveManager.markDirty()
		ctrl.reload(timing)
		list:SetValue(name)
		list:Display()
	end)

	box:AddButton("Remove Action", function()
		local timing = getWritable()
		if not timing then
			return
		end
		local action = timing.actions:find(list.Value)
		if not action then
			return Logger.longNotify("Select an action to remove.")
		end
		timing.actions:remove(action)
		SaveManager.markDirty()
		ctrl.reload(timing)
	end)

	---@param timing Timing?
	function ctrl.reload(timing)
		list:SetValues(timing and timing.actions:names() or {})
		list:SetValue(nil)
		list:Display()
		nameInput:SetRawValue("")
		typeDrop:SetRawValue("Parry")
		delayInput:SetRawValue("0")
		hbLength:SetRawValue(0)
		hbWidth:SetRawValue(0)
		hbHeight:SetRawValue(0)
	end

	return ctrl
end

		end),
	})

	soundBox:SetupDependencies({
		{ form.typeMarker, "Sound" },
	})

	---@param timing Timing?
	---@param typeKey string
	function form:load(timing, typeKey)
		self.typeMarker.Value = typeKey or "Animation"

		if timing then
			self.tag:SetRawValue(timing.tag)
			self.imdd:SetRawValue(timing.imdd)
			self.imxd:SetRawValue(timing.imxd)
			self.punishable:SetRawValue(timing.punishable)
			self.after:SetRawValue(timing.after)
			self.bfht:SetRawValue(timing.bfht)
			self.hso:SetRawValue(timing.hso)
			self.behavior:SetRawValue(timing.umoa and "Module" or "Static")
			self.smn:SetRawValue(timing.smn)
			self.smod:SetValues(ModuleManager.loaded())
			self.smod:SetRawValue(timing.smod)
			self.duih:SetRawValue(timing.duih)
			self.hbLength:SetRawValue(timing.hitbox.Z)
			self.hbWidth:SetRawValue(timing.hitbox.X)
			self.hbHeight:SetRawValue(timing.hitbox.Y)
			self.fhb:SetRawValue(timing.fhb)
			self.srpn:SetRawValue(timing.srpn)
			self.ndfb:SetRawValue(timing.ndfb)
			self.nvfb:SetRawValue(timing.nvfb)
			self.nbfb:SetRawValue(timing.nbfb)
			self.ibi:SetRawValue(timing.ibi)
			self.iapf:SetRawValue(timing.iapf)
			self.uif:SetRawValue(timing.uif)
			self.nfd:SetRawValue(timing.nfd)
			self.rsc:SetRawValue(timing.rsc)
			self.rsaid:SetRawValue(timing.rsaid or "")
			self.rsaw:SetRawValue(timing.rsaw)
			self.rpue:SetRawValue(timing.rpue)
			self.rsd:SetRawValue(tostring(timing._rsd or 0))
			self.rpd:SetRawValue(tostring(timing._rpd or 0))

			if typeKey == "Animation" then
				self.ha:SetRawValue(timing.ha)
				self.iae:SetRawValue(timing.iae)
				self.mat:SetRawValue(tostring(timing.mat or 2000))
				self.ieae:SetRawValue(timing.ieae)
				self.phd:SetRawValue(timing.phd)
				self.phds:SetRawValue(timing.phds)
				self.pfh:SetRawValue(timing.pfh)
				self.dp:SetRawValue(timing.dp)
				self.pfht:SetRawValue(timing.pfht)
			elseif typeKey == "Effect" then
				self.ilp:SetRawValue(timing.ilp)
				self.flp:SetRawValue(timing.flp)
			elseif typeKey == "Part" then
				self.uhc:SetRawValue(timing.uhc)
			elseif typeKey == "Sound" then
				self.alp:SetRawValue(timing.alp)
			end
		end

		self.actions.reload(timing)
		Library:UpdateDependencyBoxes()
	end

	return form
end

local LUA_KEYWORDS = {}
for _, kw in ipairs({
	"and", "break", "do", "else", "elseif", "end", "false", "for", "function", "goto",
	"if", "in", "local", "nil", "not", "or", "repeat", "return", "then", "true", "until",
	"while", "continue", "self",
}) do
	LUA_KEYWORDS[kw] = true
end

local HL_KEYWORD = "#f3a8d6"
local HL_STRING = "#a8e6b0"
local HL_NUMBER = "#f5c07a"
local HL_COMMENT = "#8b7fa0"

local function escapeRich(s)
	return (s:gsub("&", "&amp;"):gsub("<", "&lt;"):gsub(">", "&gt;"))
end

local function span(color, text)
	return '<font color="' .. color .. '">' .. escapeRich(text) .. "</font>"
end

local function highlightLua(code)
	local out = {}
	local i, n = 1, #code

	while i <= n do
		local c = code:sub(i, i)
		local two = code:sub(i, i + 1)

		if two == "--" then
			local eqs = code:sub(i + 2):match("^%[(=*)%[")
			if eqs then
				local close = "]" .. eqs .. "]"
				local endpos = code:find(close, i + 2, true)
				local chunk = endpos and code:sub(i, endpos + #close - 1) or code:sub(i)
				out[#out + 1] = span(HL_COMMENT, chunk)
				i = i + #chunk
			else
				local nl = code:find("\n", i, true)
				local chunk = nl and code:sub(i, nl - 1) or code:sub(i)
				out[#out + 1] = span(HL_COMMENT, chunk)
				i = i + #chunk
			end
		elseif c == '"' or c == "'" then
			local j = i + 1
			while j <= n do
				local cj = code:sub(j, j)
				if cj == "\\" then
					j = j + 2
				elseif cj == c or cj == "\n" then
					j = j + 1
					break
				else
					j = j + 1
				end
			end
			local chunk = code:sub(i, j - 1)
			out[#out + 1] = span(HL_STRING, chunk)
			i = i + #chunk
		elseif c == "[" and code:sub(i):match("^%[=*%[") then
			local eqs = code:sub(i):match("^%[(=*)%[")
			local close = "]" .. eqs .. "]"
			local endpos = code:find(close, i + #eqs + 2, true)
			local chunk = endpos and code:sub(i, endpos + #close - 1) or code:sub(i)
			out[#out + 1] = span(HL_STRING, chunk)
			i = i + #chunk
